### [1] [Agentopic: A Generative AI Agent Workflow for Explainable Topic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00833)
*Brice Valentin Kok-Shun,Johnny Chan,Gabrielle Peko,David Sundaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Agentopic 是一种基于多智能体协作的可解释主题建模工作流，利用大语言模型的推理能力实现透明、可追溯的主题分配，在 BBC 数据集上达到 0.95 F1-score，并生成层次化、语义连贯的主题结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主题建模方法（如 LDA、BERTopic）在主题分配或分组上缺乏透明度，用户无法理解主题背后的推理逻辑。

Method: 采用多个智能体协同完成主题识别、验证、层次化分组和自然语言解释，将可解释性嵌入整个工作流。

Result: 在 BBC 数据集上，有种子引导时 F1-score 达 0.95（与 GPT-4.1 持平）；无种子时生成 2045 个语义连贯主题，覆盖 6 个层次级别；同时为 BBC 数据集生成了丰富的解释性增强。

Conclusion: Agentopic 提供了一种可替代黑盒模型的可解释主题建模方案，特别适用于金融、医疗等对透明性要求高的关键应用场景。

Abstract: Agentopic is a novel agent-based workflow for explainable topic modeling that leverages the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). Existing topic modeling approaches such as Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) and BERTopic often lack transparency on how topics are assigned or grouped. Agentopic addresses this by using multiple agents that collaboratively perform topic identification, validation, hierarchical grouping, and natural language explanation. This design enables users to trace the reasoning behind topic assignments, enhancing interpretability without sacrificing accuracy. When seeded with topics from the British Broadcasting Corporation (BBC) dataset, Agentopic achieves an F1-score of 0.95, matching GPT-4.1, improving on LDA (0.93), and close to BERTopic (0.98). We used Agentopic to augment the BBC dataset with generated explanations to improve the dataset's richness and context. The unseeded Agentopic generated 2045 semantically coherent topics organized across six hierarchical levels, vastly enriching the original five-category structure. By embedding explainability throughout the workflow, Agentopic offers an interpretable alternative to black-box models, making it particularly valuable for crucial applications like finance and healthcare.

</details>


### [2] [Polynomial-Time Optimal Group Selection via the Double-Commutator Eigenvalue Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00834)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明：代数多样性框架中的群选择问题（从指数级子群中寻找最优协方差匹配群）可转化为由协方差矩阵的双交换子导出的广义特征值问题，从而将计算复杂度从指数级降至多项式级 O(d²M² + d³)，且最优群生成元可通过闭式解直接获得，无需迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数多样性框架用单个观测上的代数群作用替代了传统多观测时间平均进行二阶统计估计，但其核心的群选择问题——给定M维观测，寻找与未知协方差结构匹配最优的有限群——需要枚举对称群S_M的所有子群，复杂度为指数级，是阻碍该框架实际应用的关键瓶颈。

Method: 将群选择这一组合问题转化为由协方差矩阵的双交换子(double commutator)导出的广义特征值问题。通过构造双交换子矩阵并求解其最小特征向量，可直接在闭式中得到最优群生成元。该方法的计算复杂度为 O(d²M² + d³)，其中d为生成元基的维度。

Result: 证明了双交换子最小特征值为零当且仅当最优生成元位于基张成的空间中；当不在时，特征值大小提供了可认证的最优性差距。该方法同时满足多项式时间复杂度、闭式解和可认证性，且与独立成分分析(JADE)、结构化矩阵逼近问题和同步矩阵对角化等方向建立了联系。

Conclusion: 群选择问题不属于Garey和Johnson标准计算复杂性目录中的已知问题类别，代表了一类连接群论、矩阵分析和统计估计的新问题，而双交换子方法是唯一同时满足多项式时间、闭式解和可认证性的方法。

Abstract: The algebraic diversity framework replaces temporal averaging over multiple observations with algebraic group action on a single observation for second-order statistical estimation. The central open problem in this framework is $\textit{group selection}$: given an $M$-dimensional observation with unknown covariance structure, find the finite group whose spectral decomposition best matches the covariance. Naive enumeration of all subgroups of the symmetric group $S_M$ requires exponential time in $M$. We prove that this combinatorial problem reduces to a generalized eigenvalue problem derived from the double commutator of the covariance matrix, yielding a polynomial-time algorithm with complexity $O(d^2M^2 + d^3)$, where $d$ is the dimension of a generator basis. The minimum eigenvector of the double-commutator matrix directly constructs the optimal group generator in closed form, with no iterative optimization. The reduction is exact: the double-commutator minimum eigenvalue is zero if and only if the optimal generator lies in the span of the basis, and its magnitude provides a certifiable optimality gap when it does not. This problem does not appear in the standard catalogs of computational complexity (Garey and Johnson, 1979) and represents a new class linking group theory, matrix analysis, and statistical estimation. We establish connections to independent component analysis (JADE), structured matrix nearness problems, and simultaneous matrix diagonalization, and we show that the double-commutator formulation is the unique approach that is simultaneously polynomial-time, closed-form, and certifiable.

</details>


### [3] [Sparse Regression under Correlation and Weak Signals: A Reproducible Benchmark of Classical and Bayesian Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00835)
*Hao Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在相关特征、弱信号和高维p设置下，对Lasso等频率派稀疏回归方法与Horseshoe等贝叶斯方法进行大规模基准测试（2600+实验），发现贝叶斯方法在预测误差和覆盖率上更优，但Lasso在变量选择上与贝叶斯方法持平，是实际默认选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典稀疏回归方法（如Lasso）速度快但无不确定性估计，贝叶斯方法（如Horseshoe）提供完整后验但计算慢。两者在真正的稀疏回归困难场景（相关特征、弱信号、高维）下缺乏系统性的头对头比较。

Method: 在合成数据上（三种协方差结构，相关系数ρ最高0.9，四种信噪比水平，p∈{20,50,100}）和Diabetes真实数据集上，对OLS、Ridge、Lasso、Elastic Net、Horseshoe、Spike-and-Slab六种方法进行超过2600次实验基准测试。

Result: 贝叶斯方法预测误差更优（MSE 72 vs. 108-267），Horseshoe覆盖率达到近名义水平（94.8%）；Spike-and-Slab区间更窄但覆盖不足（91.9%）。变量选择上，Lasso与Spike-and-Slab F1≈0.47持平。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯方法在预测和不确定性量化上占优，但Lasso在变量选择上同样有效且计算更快，当不需要后验分布时Lasso是实用默认选择。Spike-and-Slab的连续松弛可能是其覆盖不足的原因。

Abstract: Choosing between classical and Bayesian sparse regression methods involves a real trade-off: penalized estimators like Lasso run in milliseconds but give no uncertainty estimates,while Horseshoe and Spike-and-Slab priors produce full posteriors but need MCMC chains that take minutes per fit.Surprisingly few studies compare these two families head-to-head under the conditions that actually make sparse regression hard -- correlated features, weak signals, and growing dimensionality. We benchmark six methods (OLS, Ridge,Lasso, Elastic Net, Horseshoe, Spike-and-Slab) on synthetic data with three covariance structures (rho up to 0.9), four SNR levels, and p in {20, 50, 100}, plus the Diabetes dataset,totalling over 2,600 experiments. The results are clear on some points and nuanced on others. Bayesian methods win on prediction error (MSE 72 vs. 108-267), and the Horseshoe delivers near-nominal 95% coverage (94.8%). But Spike-and-Slab,despite narrower intervals, under-covers at 91.9% -- its continuous relaxation likely plays a role. For variable selection, Lasso and Spike-and-Slab tie at F1 ~ 0.47, making Lasso the practical default when posteriors are not needed. Code and data are available at https://github.com/xiao98/sparse-bayesian-regression-bench.

</details>


### [4] [Fast Log-Domain Sinkhorn Optimal Transport with Warp-Level GPU Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00837)
*Hao Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FastSinkhorn是一种轻量级、原生的CUDA log-domain Sinkhorn算法实现，通过warp-level shuffle归约和共享内存分块实现高GPU利用率，在正则化参数小至10^{-4}时仍保持数值稳定，在8192×8192密集OT问题上相比POT库加速12倍、相比PyTorch基线加速5.9倍，仅消耗256 MB显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Sinkhorn算法实现要么在小正则化参数下数值不稳定，要么因深度学习框架带来显著开销，缺乏一个既数值稳定又高效的GPU原生实现。

Method: 采用纯log-domain Sinkhorn算法，结合warp-level shuffle归约（避免全局同步开销）和共享内存分块（共享内存tiling）技术，实现原生的CUDA内核设计。

Result: 在n=m=8192的密集OT问题上，相比POT库实现12倍加速，相比GPU加速的PyTorch基线实现5.9倍加速；支持epsilon=10^{-4}的极端小正则化参数；仅消耗256 MB GPU内存。

Conclusion: 原生CUDA内核配合精细的数值处理策略，可为大规模最优传输计算提供高效且实用的基础实现。

Abstract: Entropic regularized optimal transport (OT) via the Sinkhorn algorithm has become a fundamental tool in machine learning, yet existing implementations either suffer from numerical instability for small regularization parameters or incur significant overhead from deep learning frameworks. We present FastSinkhorn, a lightweight, native CUDA implementation of the log-domain Sinkhorn algorithm that combines warp-level shuffle reductions with shared-memory tiling to achieve high GPU utilization without sacrificing numerical stability. Our solver operates entirely in the log-domain, enabling robust computation for regularization parameters as small as epsilon = 10^{-4} where standard-domain methods fail. On dense OT problems with n = m = 8192, our implementation achieves 12x speedup over the widely-used POT library and 5.9x speedup over GPU-accelerated PyTorch baselines, while consuming only 256 MB of GPU memory. We validate our solver on image color transfer, 3D point cloud matching, and convergence analysis, demonstrating that native CUDA kernels with careful numerical treatment provide a practical and efficient foundation for large-scale optimal transport computation.

</details>


### [5] [GAZE: Grounded Agentic Zero-shot Evaluation with Viewer-Level Tools and Literature Retrieval on Rare Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00876)
*Duaa Alim,Mogtaba Alim,Liam Chalcroft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAZE框架，让医学视觉语言模型通过迭代式工具调用（图像查看工具+医学文献检索）实现类似放射科医生的诊断流程，在罕见病基准NOVA上无需微调即显著提升病灶定位和诊断准确率，且对罕见病理获益更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型（VLM）以单次前向方式处理图像生成文本，但放射科医生通常反复查看图像并查阅文献后才撰写报告。这种一次性模式限制了VLM在医学影像诊断中的表现，尤其是在罕见病等需要迭代推理和外部知识检索的场景。

Method: GAZE框架赋予医学VLM迭代式工具调用能力：① Viewer-level tools：缩放、窗宽窗位、对比度、边缘检测等图像操作工具；② Retrieval tools：PubMed（医学文献）和Open-i（影像图像）检索工具，由美国国家医学图书馆支持。所有输出经结构化schema验证，完整工具调用轨迹记录可审计。

Result: 在NOVA基准（906例脑MRI，281种罕见病）上：病灶定位mAP@0.3达58.2%，Top-1诊断准确率34.9%；仅结构化提示和schema验证就将mAP@0.3从20.2提升至29.4；工具使用对罕见病收益更大（IoU>0.3比例：罕见病从17%升至58%，常见病从25%升至68%）；检索消融显示诊断增益可能伴随定位损失，强调联合评估的必要性。

Conclusion: GAZE框架通过模拟放射科医生的迭代工作流和外部知识检索，有效提升了医学VLM在罕见病诊断和病灶定位中的表现。框架设计本身（结构化提示、schema验证）就是重要的实验变量，强调医学VLM评估应联合考察诊断、定位和图像描述三项任务。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) read an image and produce text in a single forward pass, whereas radiologists typically inspect an image several times and consult the literature before writing a report. We introduce GAZE (Grounded Agentic Zero-shot Evaluation), a framework that lets a medical VLM work in this iterative way by calling viewer-level tools (zoom, windowing, contrast, edge detection) and two retrieval tools backed by the U.S. National Library of Medicine (PubMed for medical literature, Open-i for radiological images), with structured outputs validated against a schema and full tool-call traces recorded for auditability. On NOVA, a benchmark of 906 brain MRI cases covering 281 rare neurological conditions, GAZE reaches 58.2 mean average precision (mAP) at intersection-over-union (IoU) 0.3 for lesion localisation and 34.9% Top-1 diagnostic accuracy under a joint protocol that scores captioning, diagnosis, and localisation from the image alone, without task-specific fine-tuning. Before any tool is used, structured prompting and schema-validated outputs already improve over the published Gemini 2.0 Flash baseline (20.2 to 29.4 mAP@0.3), so framework design is itself an experimental variable. Tool use helps rare pathologies disproportionately: the fraction of cases with IoU > 0.3 rises from 17% to 58% for diagnoses with three or fewer examples versus 25% to 68% for common conditions ($\geq$10 cases), with gains tracking engagement (Gemini 3 Flash: Cohen's d = 0.79, 11.8 tool calls per case; Gemini 2.0 Flash: tools used in 8.2% of cases, no significant benefit). Retrieval ablations additionally reveal a model-dependent trade-off in which gains in diagnosis can coincide with losses in localisation, reinforcing the case for joint evaluation of diagnosis, localisation, and captioning in medical VLMs.

</details>


### [6] [Linking spatial biology and clinical histology via Haiku](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00925)
*Yan Cui,Jacob S. Leiby,Wenhui Lei,Dokyoon Kim,Yanxiang Deng,Aaron T. Mayer,Zhenqin Wu,Alexandro E. Trevino,Zhi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Haiku是一个基于三重态对比学习的三模态（空间蛋白质组学、H&E组织学、临床元数据）融合模型，在26.7M张mIF斑块上训练，实现了跨模态检索、临床预测和零样本生物标志物推断，并引入反事实预测框架用于发现组织微环境中的分子变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子、形态学和临床数据的整合对基础与转化生物医学研究至关重要，但现有系统缺乏统一的框架来联合建模这三种模态。

Method: 采用三重态对比学习，在共享嵌入空间中对齐三种模态的数据；训练数据为来自1,606名患者3,218张切片的26.7M个mIF空间蛋白质组学斑块，匹配H&E组织学和临床元数据。

Result: 跨模态检索Recall@50达0.611（基线接近零）；生存预测C-index 0.737（相对提升+7.91%）；零样本生物标志物推断平均Pearson相关系数0.718（52种生物标志物）。

Conclusion: 三模态对齐通过Haiku实现了空间生物学的整合分析，将分子测量与临床背景关联起来，支持生物探索和假设生成。

Abstract: Integrating molecular, morphological, and clinical data is essential for basic and translational biomedical research, yet systematic frameworks for jointly modeling these modalities remain limited. Here we present Haiku, a tri-modal contrastive learning model trained on multiplexed immunofluorescence (mIF). It comprises 26.7 million spatial proteomics patches from 3,218 tissue sections across 1,606 patients spanning 11 organ types, with matched hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) histology and clinical metadata aligned in a shared embedding space. Haiku enables three-way cross-modal retrieval, improves downstream classification and clinical prediction tasks over unimodal baselines, and supports zero-shot biomarker inference through fusion retrieval conditioned on clinical metadata-only text descriptions. Across tasks, Haiku outperforms competing approaches, achieving cross-modal retrieval (Recall@50 up to 0.611 versus near-zero baseline), survival prediction (C-index 0.737, +7.91% relative improvement), and zero-shot biomarker inference (mean Pearson correlation 0.718 across 52 biomarkers). Furthermore, we introduce a counterfactual prediction framework in which modifying only clinical metadata while fixing tissue morphology surfaces niche-specific molecular shifts associated with breast cancer stage progression and lung cancer survival outcomes. In a lung adenocarcinoma case study, the counterfactual analysis recovers niche-specific shifts characterized by increased CD8 and granzyme B, reduced PD-L1, and decreased Ki67, broadly consistent with patterns reported for favorable outcomes. We present these counterfactual results as exploratory, hypothesis-generating signals rather than mechanistic claims. These capabilities demonstrate that tri-modal alignment via Haiku enables integrative analysis of spatial biology, bridging molecular measurements with clinical context for biological exploration.

</details>


### [7] [A Review of the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve and a Proof About the Area Beneath It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00926)
*Steven Redolfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了ROC曲线下面积（AUC）的概率解释——即AUC等于分类器将随机正样本排在随机负样本之上的概率——推导了当假设不满足时该解释的误差界，并提供了ROC曲线的文献综述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AUC被广泛引用为具有“随机正样本排在随机负样本之上”的概率解释，但该解释的严格数学形式化及其假设条件缺乏系统梳理。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，并为该解释的有效性提供定量边界。

Method: 采用数学形式化方法，从概率论角度严格推导AUC与排序概率之间的等价关系；通过松弛假设条件，推导出误差上界；辅以文献综述梳理ROC曲线的发展脉络。

Result: 严格证明了AUC等于P(正样本得分 > 负样本得分)这一经典解释；给出了当模型假设（如连续得分分布）不成立时，AUC与真实排序概率之间的偏差上界。

Conclusion: 该研究为AUC的概率解释提供了坚实的理论依据，明确了其适用条件与潜在偏差，对分类器性能评估的理论基础具有重要贡献。

Abstract: The Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) curve of a binary classifier has often been utilized to measure the performance of the classifier. The area beneath this curve is used in particular because of its quoted probabilistic interpretation as being equal to the probability that the classifier will rank a random positive observation above a random negative observation. This paper formalizes this claim, produces a bound on how far away from the truth it is if a hypothesis is not met, and gives a small literature review of the ROC curve.

</details>


### [8] [CGM-JEPA: Learning Consistent Continuous Glucose Monitor Representations via Predictive Self-Supervised Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00933)
*Hada Melino Muhammad,Zechen Li,Flora Salim,Ahmed A. Metwally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGM-JEPA和X-CGM-JEPA，一种基于自监督学习（掩码潜在表示预测）的连续血糖监测数据表示方法，通过跨模态抽象解决单一视图表示在部署时无法迁移的问题。在~389k条CGM记录上预训练，在三类临床评估场景下，X-CGM-JEPA在AUROC指标上排名第一或第二，最高超过最强基线6.5个百分点，并缩小了种族亚组的AUROC差距25-54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CGM数据存在多视图（时间序列、静脉OGTT、Glucodensity摘要），单一视图表示无法在模态或场景迁移时有效工作，且基线方法在不同迁移条件下表现不一致。需要一种能捕捉高层次时间与分布结构、不依赖于特定视图的抽象表示。

Method: CGM-JEPA采用自监督预训练框架，预测掩码潜在表示而非原始值，实现跨模态抽象；X-CGM-JEPA进一步引入掩码Glucodensity跨视图目标，获取互补分布信息。在~389k条未标注CGM数据上预训练，评估三个场景：队列泛化、静脉到CGM迁移、家庭CGM。

Result: X-CGM-JEPA在所有三个场景下对两个临床终点均排名第一或第二，在队列泛化上超过最强基线最多6.5个百分点，在静脉到CGM迁移上超过3.6个百分点（配对Wilcoxon检验p<0.001）。模态迁移下均值AUROC持平但弱势亚组（种族）AUROC差距缩小25-54%；稀疏静脉数据上分布视图提升聚类指标（ARI+39%，NMI+40%）。

Conclusion: 跨模态抽象表示能有效解决CGM部署中的视图迁移问题，X-CGM-JEPA在多个迁移场景下表现稳定且优于现有基线，同时能缓解亚组公平性差距。

Abstract: Continuous Glucose Monitoring (CGM) can detect early metabolic subphenotypes (insulin resistance, IR; $β$-cell dysfunction), but population-scale deployment faces two coupled problems. First, the same physiological state appears through multiple views (CGM time series, venous OGTT, Glucodensity summaries), so single-view representations fail to transfer when deployment shifts the modality or setting. Second, baselines perform inconsistently across these shifts. Both problems point to one remedy: representations that abstract away from any single view to capture higher-level temporal and distributional structure. We propose CGM-JEPA, a self-supervised pretraining framework which predicts masked latent representations rather than raw values, yielding abstraction that transfers across modalities. X-CGM-JEPA adds a masked Glucodensity cross-view objective for complementary distributional information. We pretrain on $\sim$389k unlabeled CGM readings from 228 subjects and evaluate on two clinical cohorts ($N=27$ and $N=17$ public-release subsets) across three regimes (cohort generalization, venous-to-CGM transfer, home CGM) under 20-iteration $\times$ 2-fold cross-validation. X-CGM-JEPA ranks first or second on AUROC for both endpoints across all three regimes while no baseline does, exceeding the strongest baseline by up to $+6.5$ pp in cohort generalization and $+3.6$ pp in venous-to-CGM transfer (paired Wilcoxon, $p<0.001$). Under modality shift, it matches mean AUROC while redistributing toward weaker subgroups (ethnicity AUROC gap shrinks 25-54%); on sparse in-domain venous data, the distributional view lifts label-aware clustering (ARI $+39\%$, NMI $+40\%$). Code and weights: https://github.com/cruiseresearchgroup/CGM-JEPA

</details>


### [9] [Structured Analytic Coherent Point Drift for Non-Rigid Point Set Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00934)
*Wei Feng,Haiyong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Analytic-CPD，一种结构化解析变体的相干点漂移（CPD）方法用于非刚性点集配准。该方法保留CPD的后验对应层，但将点索引的高斯核位移场M-step替换为有限维结构化解析映射估计器。通过重心恒等式将高斯混合模型的后验概率凝聚为加权软目标点，将CPD的成对软对应目标转化为加权解析拟合问题。形变由向量值函数的截断多元泰勒映射表示，形变参数数量由环境维度和解析阶数控制，而非移动点上的M×M核系统。进一步引入度延续策略，通过逐步激活高阶解析模式来稳定大形变配准。在二维解析形变和三维平滑非解析形变上的实验表明，在代表性大形变场景中，Analytic-CPD比标准CPD实现了更低的最终误差和更快的收敛速度。结果表明，CPD风格的概率对应与结构化解析映射为基于核的非刚性配准提供了一种紧凑且可解释的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CPD使用高斯核位移场，其形变参数数量依赖于移动点数量（M×M核矩阵），导致在大形变场景下计算效率低、可解释性差。本文旨在设计一种解析形变模型，用少量参数（由维度和阶数控制）替代核方法，同时保留CPD的概率对应框架。

Method: 1) 保留CPD的GMM后验概率层用于软对应估计；2) 通过重心恒等式将后验概率凝聚为加权软目标点；3) 用截断多元泰勒映射表示形变，参数仅由维度d和阶数p控制（O(d^{p+1})）；4) 引入度延续策略逐步激活高阶模式以稳定大形变配准。

Result: 在二维解析形变和三维平滑非解析形变数据集上，Analytic-CPD相比标准CPD在配准误差上更低，收敛速度更快，尤其在大形变场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: CPD风格的概率对应与结构化解析映射的结合，提供了一种紧凑、可解释且计算高效的替代方案，可替代基于核的非刚性配准方法。

Abstract: We introduce Analytic-CPD, a structured analytic variant of coherent point drift for non-rigid point set registration. The method retains the CPD posterior correspondence layer, but replaces the point-indexed Gaussian-kernel displacement-field M-step with a finite-dimensional structured analytic mapping estimator. Posterior probabilities from the Gaussian mixture model are condensed through a barycentric identity into weighted soft target points, converting the CPD pairwise soft-correspondence objective into a weighted analytic fitting problem. The deformation is represented by a truncated multivariate Taylor mapping of a vector-valued function, so the number of deformation parameters is controlled by the ambient dimension and the analytic order rather than by an M-by-M kernel system over the moving points. A degree-continuation strategy is further introduced to stabilize large-deformation registration by progressively activating higher-order analytic modes. Experiments on two-dimensional analytic deformations and three-dimensional smooth non-analytic deformations show that Analytic-CPD achieves lower final errors and faster convergence than standard CPD in representative large-deformation settings. The results suggest that CPD-style probabilistic correspondences and structured analytic mappings provide a compact and interpretable alternative to kernel-based non-rigid registration. Code is available at https://github.com/monge-ampere/Analytic-CPD.

</details>


### [10] [Watch Your Step: Information Injection in Diffusion Models via Shadow Timestep Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00935)
*An Huang,Junggab Son,Zuobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Shadow Timestep Embedding (STE)机制，揭示了扩散模型中时间步嵌入空间可被用于恶意信息注入的侧信道，并通过理论分析证明了不同时间步的表示能力差异可用于攻击和防御目的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中的时间步嵌入组件虽然包含大量信息，但在安全风险和可靠溯源方面尚未得到充分研究，存在被恶意利用的潜在漏洞。

Method: 通过分析时间步嵌入空间，发现不同时间步具有不同的表示能力可编码侧信道信息；将时间步嵌入视为位置编码映射，并引入互相干性评估来解释不相交时间步区间的可分离性；通过调度器接口实现信息的编码与利用。

Result: 验证了时间步嵌入可作为承载专用信息的强大侧信道，不同时间步区间的可分离性支持信息的编码与解码。

Conclusion: 时间步维度为对抗性生成建模提供了新方向，揭示了扩散模型中未被充分利用的时序空间可被用于恶意信息注入，同时为理解和利用时序维度进行攻击与防御奠定了基础。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the foundation of modern generative systems, with most research focusing primarily on improving generation efficiency and output quality. The timestep embedding component is a crucial part of the diffusion pipeline, which provides a temporal conditioning signal to the denoising network, enabling it to adapt its predictions across different noise levels throughout the process. Despite their potential to contain substantial information, timestep embeddings remain underexplored in current research, especially for security risks and reliable provenance. To fill this gap, we introduce Shadow Timestep Embedding (STE), a novel mechanism that investigates the underutilized temporal space for malicious information injection into diffusion models. In particular, when zooming in on the timestep embedding space, we find that different timesteps exhibit distinct representational capabilities that can encode side-channel information. Moreover, such encoded information can be utilized for attack and defense purposes through the scheduler interface. We present a theoretical analysis of timestep embeddings as position-encoding mappings and derive a mutual coherence evaluation that explains the separability of disjoint timestep intervals. Our findings reveal the diffusion model's timestep as a powerful side channel for carrying dedicated information, motivating new directions for adversarial generative modeling by understanding the temporal dimension.

</details>


### [11] [EventADL: Open-Box Anomaly Detection and Localization Framework for Events in Cloud-Based Service Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00936)
*Luan Pham,Victor Nicolet,Joey Dodds,Hui Guan,Daniel Kroening*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EventADL首次提出基于事件的开放盒式异常检测与根因定位框架，利用事件语义模式（ESPs）和事件频率模式（EFPs）捕获正常系统交互，通过干预图实现根因定位，在三个真实云服务系统上达到90%以上F1分数和100% top-3根因定位准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测与定位（ADL）方法主要聚焦于指标和日志数据，忽略了云系统中事件数据的价值。为填补这一空白，该工作对520个真实事件进行系统分析，提出首个基于事件的ADL框架。

Method: EventADL包含三个阶段：离线训练阶段学习事件语义模式（ESPs）和事件频率模式（EFPs）；在线异常检测阶段检测偏离任一模式的事件流；根因定位阶段构建干预图建模系统交互与异常之间的关系，实现自动根因定位。框架支持无标签数据且结果可解释。

Result: 在三个真实云服务系统和两个真实事件上，异常检测F1分数≥90%，根因定位top-3准确率达100%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 事件数据是云系统异常检测与定位的有效信息源，EventADL通过结合语义和频率模式能够高效、可解释地完成ADL任务。

Abstract: Anomaly detection and localization (ADL) is critical for maintaining reliability and availability in cloud systems. Recent ADL developments focus on metric and log data, leaving event data unexplored. To address this gap, we propose EventADL, the first open-box event-based ADL framework for cloud-based service systems. To motivate the design of our framework, we conduct a systematic analysis on 520 real-world incidents, and provide insights into how anomalies and their root causes manifest through event data. EventADL has three phases: offline training, online anomaly detection, and root cause localization. During the training phase, EventADL first learns Event Semantic Patterns (ESPs), which capture normal interactions between system entities using historical event data, and then learns Event Frequency Patterns (EFPs), which capture the normal frequency of known ESPs. In the online anomaly detection phase, any data in the event stream that deviates significantly from either pattern is identified as anomalous. For localization, EventADL constructs an Intervention Graph that models the relationships between recent system interactions and the detected anomalies for automatic root cause localization. The framework is designed to operate efficiently with unlabeled data and to produce interpretable anomalies with their corresponding root causes. Our evaluation on three real cloud service systems and two real-world incidents demonstrates that EventADL outperforms existing methods, achieving F1-scores of at least 90% for anomaly detection and 100% top-3 accuracy in root cause localization.

</details>


### [12] [Fusing Urban Structure and Semantics: A Conditional Diffusion Model for Cross-City OD Matrix Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00938)
*Bin Chen,Zhuoya Meng,Fang Yang,Runkang Guo,Jingtao Ding,Yin Zhang,Chuan Ai,Zhengqiu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SEDAN模型，将城市建模为属性图，通过图Transformer融合区域语义属性与空间结构（邻接矩阵和距离矩阵），实现跨城市泛化的通勤OD矩阵生成，在真实数据集上RMSE比SOTA提升7.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体意图、地理约束和社会动态的联合影响导致通勤模式存在显著异质性，使得现有生成模型难以跨城市泛化。

Method: 将城市建模为属性图（节点为区域及语义特征，边为通勤流），设计融合机制：图Transformer建模语义交互（潜在出行需求），邻接矩阵引导注意力权重（邻近区域加强），距离矩阵作为扩散条件（空间邻近性与出行阻抗），联合生成行为合理且地理一致的OD矩阵。

Result: 在美国城市真实OD数据集上，RMSE比SOTA基线WEDAN提升7.38%，且在异质城市场景和不同结构模式下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 语义与空间约束的融合能有效生成跨城市泛化的通勤OD矩阵，为城市治理和交通规划提供了可推广的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of commuting flows is important for urban governance, traffic planning, and resource allocation. However, the combined influence of individual intentions, geographic constraints, and social dynamics leads to considerable heterogeneity in commuting patterns, making it difficult to develop generation models that generalize across cities. To address this issue, we propose SEDAN, a Structure-Enhanced Diffusion model conditioned on Attributed Nodes for generalizable OD matrix generation. SEDAN models a city as an attributed graph. Each region is treated as a node with demographic and point-of-interest features, and commuting flows are modeled as weighted edges. Adjacency and distance matrices are incorporated to characterize spatial structure. Based on this representation, we design a fusion mechanism within SEDAN to jointly model semantic information and spatial information. Regional semantic attributes are used to model latent travel demand through graph-transformer-based node interactions, while spatial structure is injected into the generation process as explicit constraints. The adjacency matrix guides attention weights to strengthen interactions between neighboring regions. Meanwhile, the distance matrix serves as a diffusion condition to capture spatial proximity and travel impedance. The fusion of urban semantics and spatial constraints enables SEDAN to generate OD matrices that are both behaviorally plausible and geographically coherent. Experiments on real-world OD datasets from U.S. cities show that SEDAN achieves a 7.38\% improvement in RMSE over the state-of-the-art baseline, WEDAN. It also remains robust across heterogeneous urban scenarios and varying structural patterns. Our work provides an effective and generalizable solution for commuting OD matrix generation. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SEDAN.

</details>


### [13] [Divergence is Uncertainty: A Closed-Form Posterior Covariance for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00941)
*Jiarui Xing,Song Wang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出散度-不确定性恒等式，证明对于任意预训练的流匹配模型，后验协方差的迹等于速度场的散度（乘以已知时间相关因子再加常数），无需重新训练或修改架构即可精确计算生成不确定性，在MNIST上以比集成方法低约10000倍的计算成本获得语义上有意义的不确定性图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配框架已成为生成建模的主流方法，但对其样本进行不确定性量化仍是一个未解决的问题。现有方法存在训练成本高、推理成本高或精度不足等权衡问题。

Method: 证明对于任意预训练的流匹配速度场，干净数据关于当前状态的后验协方差的迹等于速度场的散度乘以已知时间相关因子再加一个常数（散度-不确定性恒等式）。矩阵级形式仅依赖于速度雅可比矩阵。该恒等式是精确且事后可计算的，无需重新训练或修改架构。

Result: 在MNIST上，逐像素不确定性图具有语义意义，集中在数字边界（样本间变化最大区域），标量不确定性分数跟踪实际预测误差。以比集成或蒙特卡洛dropout低约10000倍的总计算量实现。

Conclusion: 现有方法所需的训练成本、推理成本或精度之间的权衡并非必要。该恒等式在单次前向传播中即可获得端到端生成不确定性，消除了先前方法所需的多步方差传播。

Abstract: Flow matching has become a leading framework for generative modeling, but quantifying the uncertainty of its samples remains an open problem. Existing approaches retrain the model with auxiliary variance heads, maintain costly ensembles, or propagate approximate covariance through many integration steps, trading off training cost, inference cost, or accuracy. We show that none of these trade-offs is necessary. We prove that, for any pre-trained flow matching velocity field, the trace of the posterior covariance over the clean data given the current state equals, in closed form, the divergence of the velocity field, up to a known time-dependent prefactor and an additive constant. We call this the \emph{divergence-uncertainty identity} for flow matching. The matrix-level form of the identity is similarly closed-form, depending solely on the velocity Jacobian. Because the identity is exact and post-hoc, it is computable on any pre-trained flow matching model, with no retraining and no architectural modification. For one-step generators such as MeanFlow, the same identity yields the exact end-to-end generation uncertainty in a single forward pass, eliminating the multi-step variance propagation required by all prior methods. Experiments on MNIST confirm that the resulting per-pixel uncertainty maps are semantically meaningful, concentrating on digit boundaries where inter-sample variation is highest, and that the scalar uncertainty score tracks actual prediction error, all at roughly 10,000$\times$ less total compute than ensembling or Monte Carlo dropout.

</details>


### [14] [A Theory of Generalization in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01172)
*Elon Litman,Gabe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了深度学习泛化的非渐近理论，通过经验神经正切核将输出空间划分为信号和噪声两个正交子空间。信号通道中误差快速消散，噪声通道中残差被近零特征值捕获。推导出无需验证数据的精确种群风险目标，可作为Adam之上的SNR预条件子，加速grokking 5倍、抑制PINN中的记忆化、改进DPO微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习理论缺乏对泛化机制的统一理解，特别是当神经切向核在特征学习阶段发生O(1)量级变化时，泛化仍然成立的原理尚不清晰。需要建立非渐近的理论框架来解释良性过拟合、双下降、隐式偏差和grokking等看似矛盾的现象。

Method: 利用经验神经正切核（NTK）对输出空间进行谱分解，将输出空间划分为信号子空间和噪声子空间。在信号通道中，小批量SGD使种群信号通过线性漂移累积，而记忆化被抑制为扩散随机游走。推导出精确的种群风险目标函数，该目标测量信号通道中的噪声，可转化为Adam上的SNR预条件子。

Result: 理论证明当NTK在算子范数下变化O(1)时泛化仍然成立（即全特征学习 regime）。推导出的种群风险目标在实践中表现为Adam上的SNR预条件子，仅增加一个状态向量。实验上加速grokking 5倍，抑制PINN和隐式神经表示中的记忆化，改进噪声偏好下的DPO微调，且保持与参考策略3倍接近。

Conclusion: 该理论统一解释了良性过拟合、双下降、隐式偏差和grokking等深度学习现象。提出的SNR预条件子无需验证数据即可在训练过程中监测和改善泛化，适用于任意架构、损失函数和优化器，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: We present a non-asymptotic theory of generalization in deep learning where the empirical neural tangent kernel partitions the output space. In directions corresponding to signal, error dissipates rapidly; in the vast orthogonal dimensions corresponding to noise, the kernel's near-zero eigenvalues trap residual error in a test-invisible reservoir. Within the signal channel, minibatch SGD ensures that coherent population signal accumulates via fast linear drift, while idiosyncratic memorization is suppressed into a slow, diffusive random walk. We prove generalization survives even when the kernel evolves $\mathcal{O}(1)$ in operator norm, the full feature-learning regime. This theory naturally explains disparate phenomena in deep learning theory, such as benign overfitting, double descent, implicit bias, and grokking. Lastly, we derive an exact population-risk objective from a single training run with no validation data, for any architecture, loss, or optimizer, and prove that it measures precisely the noise in the signal channel. This objective reduces in practice to an SNR preconditioner on top of Adam, adding one state vector at no extra cost; it accelerates grokking by $5 \times$, suppresses memorization in PINNs and implicit neural representations, and improves DPO fine-tuning under noisy preferences while staying $3 \times$ closer to the reference policy.

</details>


### [15] [The Partial Testimony of Logs: Evaluation of Language Model Generation under Confounded Model Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01311)
*Jikai Jin,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离线评估语言模型时因用户自选择导致的混杂偏差，提出一种三源设计（大尺度观测日志+小规模随机实验+离线模拟器），通过识别定理证明实验与模拟器足以恢复因果模型价值，观测日志仅用于降低估计误差。在合成数据与真实任务上评估了六类估计器，性能取决于实验监督量与奖励对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线评估语言模型时，模型选择与用户判断存在混杂偏差（混淆变量同时影响模型选择与评分），导致原始比较混杂了自选择人群而非估计共同感兴趣量。小规模随机实验可打破偏差但稀缺昂贵，需利用现有观测数据与模拟器实现无偏且高效的评估。

Method: 提出三源设计：大规模混淆观测日志提供规模，小随机实验提供无偏评分，离线模拟器在缓存上下文上回放候选模型。通过识别定理证明实验+模拟器足以恢复因果模型价值，观测日志仅用于后续降方差。评估六类估计器家族。

Result: 在受控半合成验证和两个真实任务（摘要生成、代码生成）缓存基准上评估。无单一估计器在所有场景占优；相对性能取决于无偏实验监督量以及目标奖励与观测日志导出结构的对齐程度。

Conclusion: 实验与模拟器的组合可因果识别模型价值，观测日志仅用于提升精度而非纠正偏差；不同估计器在不同数据条件下表现各异，需根据实验监督量与奖励对齐程度选择。

Abstract: Offline evaluation of language models from usage logs is biased when model choice is confounded: the same user-side factors that influence which model is used can also influence how its output is judged, so raw comparisons of logged scores mix self-selected populations rather than estimating a common quantity of interest. A small randomized experiment can break this bias by overriding model choice, but in practice such experiments are scarce and costly. We study a three-source design that combines a large confounded observational log (OBS) for scale, a small randomized experiment (EXP) for unconfounded scoring, and an offline simulator (SIM) that replays candidate models on cached contexts. Our main result is an identification theorem showing that the randomized experiment and the simulator are together enough to recover causal model values; the observational log enters only afterward, to reduce estimation error rather than to make the causal comparison valid. Six estimator families are evaluated in a controlled semi-synthetic validation and in two real-task cached benchmarks for summarization and coding. No family dominates every regime; relative performance depends on the amount of unbiased EXP supervision and on how closely the target reward aligns with OBS-derived structure.

</details>


### [16] [Finite-Sample Analysis of Elimination in Active Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01039)
*Ziyuan Lin,Hoang Ngoc Nguyen,Jie Xu,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种消除增强的Track-and-Stop算法，通过逐步剪枝特定对手集并重新分配感知资源，在有限样本下加速主动假设检验的停止时间，并通过非渐近上界和实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键应用中，固定置信度的有限样本主动假设检验问题至关重要，现有方法在停止时间上仍有优化空间，尤其是通过假设消除来提升有限样本性能。

Method: 提出消除增强的Track-and-Stop算法，逐步剪枝冠军特定的主动对手集，将感知资源重新分配给剩余假设，并引入攻击性参数来调节消除速度与置信保证之间的权衡。

Result: 推导出期望停止时间的非渐近上界，有限样本增益体现在非主导项上，源于对缩减假设集的更紧追踪和浓度常数；实验在合成高斯数据上验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 假设消除能够有效降低主动假设检验的停止时间，攻击性参数提供了灵活的性能-置信度权衡机制。

Abstract: A fixed-confidence, finite-sample problem of active hypothesis testing arises in many safety-critical applications. Situated in the context of sequential hypothesis testing, this paper studies the effect of hypothesis elimination on the stopping time. We introduce an elimination-augmented Track-and-Stop algorithm, in which champion-specific active-opponent sets are progressively pruned, and sensing effort is reallocated toward the surviving alternatives. Our analysis derives a non-asymptotic upper bound on the expected stopping time. The gain in finite-sample from elimination appears on the scale of the non-leading term, resulting from tighter tracking and concentration constants on the reduced hypothesis set. Furthermore, we introduce an aggressiveness parameter to modulate the trade-off between faster elimination and weaker confidence guarantee. An experimental study on synthetic Gaussian instances confirms the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [17] [Evaluating LLMs on Large-Scale Graph Property Estimation via Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01484)
*Sunil Kumar Maurya,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EstGraph基准数据集，用于评估LLM在大型有限可访问图上的推理能力，并设计了四个估计图属性的任务，结合随机游走采样方法在上下文长度限制内有效传递大规模图信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图推理基准仅包含小规模图，无法评估LLM在现实大规模、有限可访问图上的推理能力。

Method: 提出EstGraph基准，包含四个大型图属性估计任务；采用基于随机游走采样的提示构建方法，在LLM上下文长度限制内传递大规模图（百万节点级）的有效信息。

Result: 在多种图数据集上评估了LLM在这些任务上的推理能力。

Conclusion: 随机游走采样可有效将大规模图信息压缩至LLM上下文窗口内，用于估计图属性。

Abstract: With the rapidly improving reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), there is also a rising demand to use them in a wide variety of domains. This brings about the need to carefully evaluate the limits of the capabilities of these models with various tests and benchmarks. Graph structures are ubiquitous in real-world data, and are often used to represent and analyze relationship patterns within data. Many benchmarks have already been proposed in the graph literature to test the reasoning ability of LLMs to follow and execute graph algorithms. However, due to the limited context length of LLMs, these benchmarks consist of very small graphs. In real-world data, the size of graphs can be significantly larger, and in many cases, not fully accessible. In this paper, we examine a class of problems that arises with very large graphs having limited accessibility. We propose a large graph benchmark dataset, EstGraph, and introduce four distinct tasks designed to estimate large graph properties. We evaluate the reasoning abilities of LLMs on these tasks using a wide variety of graph datasets. In addition, we provide task-specific prompt constructions based on random walk sampling of large graphs (up to millions of nodes) that effectively convey sufficient information to LLMs within the limits of context length.

</details>


### [18] [Learning in the Fisher Subspace: A Guided Initialization for LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01046)
*Zhi-Quan Feng,Ying-Jia Lin,Hung-Yu Kao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Fisher信息的LoRA初始化方法，利用下游数据的曲率信息选择适应子空间，替代传统仅依赖预训练权重的初始化策略，显著提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA初始化仅依赖预训练权重的内在属性（如权重几何结构），忽略了模型与下游数据分布的交互，导致初始化选取了与任务无关的方向，限制了微调效果。

Method: 将LoRA初始化形式化为在下游数据分布下识别参数空间方向的影响程度，提出Fisher引导框架，利用下游数据产生的曲率信息刻画参数扰动对模型预测的影响，从而选择与目标任务对齐的LoRA方向。

Result: 在多种任务和模态上的实验结果表明，数据感知的初始化方法相比现有方法持续且显著地提升了下游性能。

Conclusion: 数据感知的敏感性（而非仅权重幅值）应指导适应子空间的选择，该Fisher引导框架提供了原则性的、任务依赖的LoRA方向选择标准。

Abstract: LoRA adapts large language models (LLMs) by restricting updates to low-rank subspaces of pre-trained weights. While this substantially reduces training cost, the effectiveness of adaptation critically depends on which subspace is chosen at initialization: a poor initialization that allocates capacity to task-irrelevant directions can severely hinder downstream performance. Existing initialization strategies primarily rely on the intrinsic properties of pre-trained weights, implicitly assuming that weight geometry alone reflects task relevance. However, such criteria overlook how the model interacts with the downstream data distribution. In this work, we formulate LoRA initialization as identifying the degree of impact of directions in parameter space under the target data distribution. We argue that data-aware sensitivity, rather than weight-only magnitude, should govern the choice of adaptation subspaces. Building on this perspective, we propose a Fisher-guided framework that leverages curvature information induced by downstream data to characterize how parameter perturbations influence model predictions. This perspective yields a principled, task-dependent criterion for selecting LoRA directions that better align adaptation with the target objective. Empirical results across diverse tasks and modalities demonstrate that data-aware initialization consistently and significantly improves downstream performance over existing approaches.

</details>


### [19] [Stable GFlowNets with Probabilistic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01729)
*Zengxiang Lei,Ananth Shreekumar,Jonathan Rosenthal,Ruoyu Song,Alvaro A. Cardenas,Daniel J. Fremont,Dongyan Xu,Satish Ukkusuri,Z. Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对GFlowNet训练不稳定、损失尖峰和模式崩溃问题，首先揭示TV距离小与训练损失大之间的矛盾，然后推导损失-TV界以证明全局保真度，最后提出Stable GFlowNets算法实现稳定训练并提升分布保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFlowNet虽然理论上能从非归一化奖励中采样，但实际训练中常出现损失尖峰和模式崩溃等不稳定现象，亟需从理论上理解其训练不稳定的根源并设计稳定化方法。

Method: 1) 敏感性分析：证明小的TV距离并不保证有界的训练损失；2) 反向保证：从有界的轨迹平衡损失推导出全局保真度的损失-TV界；3) Stable GFlowNets算法：基于理论结果稳定训练。

Result: Stable GFlowNets在训练行为上更稳定，且分布保真度优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析与算法设计，有效解决了GFlowNet训练不稳定的问题，为GFlowNet的可靠训练提供了理论保障和实用算法。

Abstract: Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) learn to sample states proportional to an unnormalized reward. Despite their theoretical promise, practical training is often unstable, exhibiting severe loss spikes and mode collapse. To tackle this, we first assess the sensitivity of GFlowNet objectives, demonstrating that a small Total Variation (TV) distance between the learned and target distributions does not preclude unbounded training loss. Motivated by this mismatch, we establish converse guarantees by deriving loss-to-TV bounds that certify global fidelity from bounded trajectory balance losses. Lastly, we propose Stable GFlowNets, an algorithm that leverages our theoretical results to stabilize training, and empirically demonstrate improved training behavior and superior distributional fidelity.

</details>


### [20] [LEAP: Layer-wise Exit-Aware Pretraining for Efficient Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01058)
*Shashank Kapadia,Deep Naryan Mishra,Sujal Reddy Alugubelli,Haoan Wang,Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Rishi Bhatia,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了层对齐蒸馏与基于收敛的早退机制在标准部署条件下存在系统不兼容性：蒸馏抑制了表示收敛，导致早退失效。为此提出LEAP辅助训练目标，通过确保中间层逼近最终层表示来调和这一矛盾。LEAP-MiniLM在batch=1、NVIDIA L4上实现1.61×实测加速（θ=0.95），91.9%样本在第7层退出，理论层数减少1.80×，而标准蒸馏模型零有效加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层对齐蒸馏和基于收敛的早退机制是Transformer推理中两种主流计算效率范式，但现有研究未发现二者在标准部署条件下存在系统性不兼容——蒸馏目标使中间层对齐教师表示，抑制了早退机制所依赖的表示收敛，导致早退在蒸馏模型上完全失效。

Method: 提出LEAP（Layer-wise Exit-Aware Pretraining）辅助训练目标：无需架构修改，在标准蒸馏基础上增加一个约束，强制中间层逼近最终层表示，从而调和蒸馏与早退的不兼容性。

Result: LEAP-MiniLM在batch=1、NVIDIA L4上实现1.61×实测加速（θ=0.95），91.9%样本在第7层退出，理论层数减少1.80×，而标准蒸馏模型零有效加速。在STS-B（0.760±0.006）和BEIR基准上验证有效，并提供延迟测量、决策阈值和部署准则等操作指导。

Conclusion: LEAP有效解决了蒸馏与早退的不兼容问题，使蒸馏模型也能利用基于收敛的早退实现显著加速，并提供了完整的部署指导。

Abstract: Layer-aligned distillation and convergence-based early exit represent two predominant computational efficiency paradigms for transformer inference; yet we establish that they exhibit systematic incompatibility under standard deployment conditions for convergence-based early exit. Distillation objectives that align intermediate student layers to teacher representations suppress the representational convergence that early-exit mechanisms exploit, rendering such mechanisms ineffective on distilled models. We introduce LEAP (Layer-wise Exit-Aware Pretraining), an auxiliary training objective that reconciles this incompatibility. LEAP requires no architectural modifications; it augments standard distillation with a single constraint ensuring intermediate layers approximate final-layer representations. LEAP-MiniLM achieves 1.61$\times$ measured wall-clock speedup (batch=1, NVIDIA L4) at $θ$=0.95, with 91.9% of samples exiting by layer 7 and 1.80$\times$ theoretical layer reduction, where standard distilled models achieve zero effective speedup. We validate across sentence similarity (STS-B: 0.760 $\pm$ 0.006) and retrieval benchmarks (BEIR), providing operational guidance including latency measurements, decision thresholds, and deployment criteria.

</details>


### [21] [Large margin classifier with graph-based adaptive regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02027)
*Vítor M. Hanriot,Turíbio T. Salis,Luiz C. B. Torres,Frederico Coelho,Antonio P. Braga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在基于Gabriel图的二分类器中引入每类正则化超参数，通过灵活阈值处理异常值和类别不平衡问题，经Friedman检验验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Gabriel图分类器使用固定阈值，难以同时处理异常值和类别不平衡问题，限制了分类性能。

Method: 在Gabriel图二分类器中引入每类独立的正则化超参数，通过质量指数在边缘区域和异常值环境下的行为分析，实现灵活阈值设置，并利用超参数调优算法优化解空间。

Result: 灵活阈值方法能有效消除异常值，并通过为多数类和少数类分别生成更高和更低的阈值来解决类别不平衡问题，Friedman检验证实该方法显著提升了Gabriel图分类器的性能。

Conclusion: 每类正则化超参数通过扩展解空间，为Gabriel图分类器提供了更优的解决方案，尤其适用于存在异常值和类别不平衡的数据场景。

Abstract: This paper introduces the use of per-class regularization hyperparameters in Gabriel graph-based binary classifiers. We demonstrate how the quality index used for regularization behaves both in the margin region and in the presence of outliers, and how incorporating this regularization flexibility can lead to solutions that effectively eliminate outliers while training the classifier. We also show how it can address class imbalance by generating higher and lower thresholds for the majority and minority classes, respectively. Thus, rather than having a single solution based on fixed thresholds, flexible thresholds expand the solution space and can be optimized through hyperparameter tuning algorithms. Friedman test shows that flexible thresholds are capable of improving Gabriel graph-based classifiers.

</details>


### [22] [GEODE: Angle-Adaptive OOD Detection with Universal Scorer Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01063)
*Bruno Abrahao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GEODE方法，通过角度自适应范数损失复现Outlier Exposure的边界校准效果，在不依赖辅助异常数据的情况下，在所有七个标准评分器上均实现优异的OOD检测性能，解决了OE方法评分器依赖性强的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OE方法虽强但存在评分器依赖的权衡（MSP强、KNN弱），且需要精心挑选辅助数据。作者希望揭示OE有效的原因，并设计一种无需辅助数据、对所有评分器都有效的OOD检测方法。

Method: GEODE（几何保持检测）方法：提出角度自适应范数损失，目标值根据样本与最近类均值的余弦相似度逐样本缩放。该方法基于神经坍缩（neural collapse）的四条定理进行理论证明，核心思想是保留对边界结构重要的特征几何结构。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，GEODE在所有七个标准评分器上均表现优异（近OOD AUROC 89.0-92.3，远OOD达93.05），无任何评分器出现灾难性失效。与OE结合后MSP达95.0、KNN达94.8。在CIFAR-100上GEODE在所有评分器上超越OE。在WRN-28-10上持续有效（Energy +4.5，3个随机种子）。

Conclusion: OE的本质是边界校准而非OOD覆盖，GEODE通过合成方式复现了这种校准效果。与将OOD推向分类器零空间的方法（如PFS，KNN AUROC仅14.38，比随机还差）不同，GEODE的自适应目标保留了距离型评分器依赖的几何结构，因此对所有评分器都有效。

Abstract: Outlier Exposure (OE) is among the strongest training-based OOD detectors on standard benchmarks but exhibits scorer-dependent tradeoffs (e.g., strong on MSP, weak on KNN) and requires curated auxiliary data. We show why OE works: its features sit at the same geometric locus as real near-OOD data, with the boundary-adjacent quartile driving nearly all of OE's gain. OE is boundary calibration, not OOD coverage. GEODE (GEOmetry-preserving DEtection) replicates this calibration synthetically through an angle-adaptive norm loss in which targets scale per-sample with cosine similarity to the nearest class mean, preserving feature geometry where boundary structure matters. Four theorems grounded in neural collapse justify the design. GEODE works across all seven standard scorers on CIFAR-10 (near-OOD AUROC 89.0-92.3, far-OOD reaching 93.05; no catastrophic failure on any scorer). Since the OOD regime is unknown at deployment, this is the test that matters. GEODE outperforms vanilla CE at matched epoch counts. Combined with OE, GEODE reaches 95.0 MSP / 94.8 KNN on CIFAR-10 and beats OE on every scorer on CIFAR-100. The gains hold on WRN-28-10 (+4.5 Energy, 3 seeds). Unlike methods that push OOD into the classifier null space (e.g., PFS, 14.38 KNN AUROC, worse than random), GEODE's adaptive target preserves the geometry that distance-based scorers depend on.

</details>


### [23] [On the Optimal Sample Complexity of Offline Multi-Armed Bandits with KL Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02141)
*Kaixuan Ji,Qiwei Di,Heyang Zhao,Qingyue Zhao,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格分析了KL正则化下的离线多臂老虎机问题，给出了KL-PCB算法在正则化强度大小时分别达到$\tilde{O}(ηSAC^{π^*}/ε)$和$\tildeΩ(SAC^{π^*}/ε^2)$的样本复杂度，并提供了匹配的下界，从而在正则化强度全范围内实现了对样本复杂度的近乎完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL正则化在离线决策中被广泛使用，但其离线学习的精确样本复杂度尚未被充分刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在离线多臂老虎机设定下，对KL-PCB算法进行锐利分析，推导其样本复杂度的上界；同时构造相应的样本复杂度下界，使上下界在整个正则化强度范围内匹配。

Result: 正则化参数$η$较大（$η=\tilde{O}(ε^{-1})$）时样本复杂度为$\tilde{O}(ηSAC^{π^*}/ε)$，较小（$η=\tildeΩ(ε^{-1})$）时为$\tildeΩ(SAC^{π^*}/ε^2)$，且下界与上界匹配。

Conclusion: 本文提供了离线KL正则化多臂老虎机样本复杂度的近乎完整刻画。

Abstract: Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization is widely used in offline decision-making and offers several benefits, motivating recent work on the sample complexity of offline learning with respect to KL-regularized performance metrics. Nevertheless, the exact sample complexity of KL-regularized offline learning remains largely from fully characterized. In this paper, we study this question in the setting of multi-armed bandits (MABs). We provide a sharp analysis of KL-PCB (Zhao et al., 2026), showing that it achieves a sample complexity of $\tilde{O}(ηSAC^{π^*}/ε)$ under large regularization $η= \tilde{O}(ε^{-1})$, and a sample complexity of $\tildeΩ(SAC^{π^*}/ε^2)$ under small regularization $η= \tildeΩ(ε^{-1})$, where $η$ is the regularization parameter, $S$ is the number of contexts, $A$ is the number of arms, $C^{π^*}$ policy coverage coefficient at the optimal policy $π^*$, $ε$ is the desired sub-optimality, and $\tilde{O}$ and $\tildeΩ$ hide all poly-logarithmic factors. We further provide a pair of sharper sample complexity lower bounds, which matches the upper bounds over the entire range of regularization strengths. Overall, our results provide a nearly complete characterization of offline multi-armed bandits with KL regularization.

</details>


### [24] [A dimensional R2 regression metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01066)
*Jaesung Yoo,Stefan Lemke,Jian Zhong Guo,Kanaka Rajan,Adam Hantman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Dim-R2评分，这是对传统R2评分的扩展，解决了其维度限制（仅支持二维）、单标量隐藏精度模式以及对低方差噪声敏感的问题，支持任意维度数据并提供多维视角的回归评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统R2评分存在三个关键局限：仅支持最多二维输入、将评分简化为单一标量从而隐藏了预测精度的丰富模式、对低方差噪声通道敏感导致产生大且不可解释的负值。

Method: 提出Dimensional R2评分（Dim-R2），作为R2的简单扩展，可以接受任意维度的数据，提供多维度的准确性视角，并降低对噪声的敏感性。

Result: 在合成正弦数据和三个多维回归数据集上展示了Dim-R2的优势，证明其是一种可解释且灵活的评估指标。

Conclusion: Dim-R2能够突出回归精度的模式，为回归建模提供指导，是一种优于传统R2的替代评估方案。

Abstract: R2 score is the standard metric for evaluating regression tasks, offering a normalized magnitude-agnostic measure of accuracy that captures variance. However, R2 has three key limitations: it is limited to at most two dimensional inputs, it reduces the score to a single scalar that hides rich patterns of prediction accuracy, and it is sensitive to low-variance noise channels which can yield large, uninterpretable negative values. We introduce the Dimensional R2 score (Dim-R2), a simple extension of R2 that accepts data of arbitrary dimensionality, provides a multidimensional view of accuracy, and reduces sensitivity to noise. We demonstrate its advantages on both synthetic sinusoidal data and three multidimensional regression datasets. Dim-R2 offers an interpretable and flexible metric that highlights patterns in regression accuracy, guiding regression modeling.

</details>


### [25] [Generalized Distributional Alignment Games for Unbiased Answer-Level Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02435)
*Mehryar Mohri,Jon Schneider,Yutao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地解决了分布对齐博弈中的结构估计偏差问题：通过将对齐博弈推广到任意Bregman散度并利用U-统计量构建无偏估计器，针对KL散度博弈推导出全局鲁棒的极小极大多项式估计器（达到最优统计误差界Θ(1/K²)），并提出方差最优增强多项式优化程序（AQP）估计器，在不增加在线计算开销的前提下实现加速收敛和稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ALFT算法依赖从小批量估计对数奖励，由于Jensen不等式引入系统性偏差，导致训练不稳定。本文旨在系统性地消除这一结构估计偏差。

Method: (1) 将对齐博弈推广到任意Bregman散度，对多项式奖励族利用U-统计量构建精确无偏估计器；(2) 针对KL散度博弈，基于Ditzian-Totik定理推导全局鲁棒的极小极大多项式估计器，达到最优误差界Θ(1/K²)；(3) 综合上述方法提出方差最优增强多项式优化程序（AQP）估计器，系统性降低方差。

Result: 提出的极小极大估计器达到最优统计误差界Θ(1/K²)；AQP估计器在实现最优偏差的同时，零在线计算开销下实现加速博弈收敛和更稳定训练。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和算法设计，系统解决了ALFT中分布对齐博弈的估计偏差问题，所提方法在统计最优性和计算效率上均有显著优势。

Abstract: The Distributional Alignment Game framework provides a powerful variational perspective on Answer-Level Fine-Tuning (ALFT). However, standard algorithms for these games rely on estimating logarithmic rewards from small batches, introducing a systematic bias due to Jensen's inequality that can destabilize training. In this paper, we systematically resolve this structural estimation bias. First, we generalize the alignment game to arbitrary Bregman divergences, showing that for a family of geometries inducing polynomial rewards, we can construct provably exact and unbiased estimators using U-statistics. Second, for the canonical KL divergence game where an exact solution is impossible, we derive a globally robust minimax polynomial estimator that is provably optimal, achieving the fundamental statistical error limit of $Θ(1/K^2)$, which we establish via the Ditzian-Totik theorem. Finally, we synthesize these two approaches to propose a novel Variance-Optimal Augmented Polynomial Optimization Program (AQP) Estimator, proving that by systematically reducing variance, our method achieves not only optimal bias but also provably accelerated game convergence, leading to more efficient and stable training with zero online computational overhead.

</details>


### [26] [Learning Discriminators for Resampling in the Ensemble Gaussian Mixture Filter through a Normalizing Flow Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01089)
*Zain Jabbar,Andrey A. Popov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种判别器引导的重采样方法（Discriminator-Informed Resampling），通过标准化流学习物理合理性判别器，改进集合高斯混合滤波器（EnGMF）中生成非物理后验样本的问题，在Ikeda映射和Lorenz '63系统上验证了低集合规模下误差的显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准EnGMF的重采样步骤可能生成物理上不真实的后验样本，导致后续预报失去物理意义，需要一种机制来约束样本的物理合理性。

Method: 在EnGMF的后验重采样阶段引入判别器，对候选粒子基于物理合理性进行接受/拒绝；判别器通过标准化流（Normalizing Flow）学习得到。

Result: 在Ikeda映射和Lorenz '63系统上的数值实验表明，判别器引导的重采样在低集合规模下持续降低了误差。

Conclusion: 判别器引导的重采样是一种有效的改进方案，能够在保持EnGMF收敛性的同时，避免生成非物理样本，提升低集合场景下的滤波精度。

Abstract: The ensemble Gaussian mixture filter (EnGMF) is a powerful, convergent particle filter capable of medium-to-high dimensional non-linear filtering. The EnGMF relies on a resampling step that can generate physically unrealistic posterior samples, that would subsequently produce physically meaningless forecasts. This work introduces the discriminator-informed resampling procedure, that augments the posterior resampling step with a discriminator that accepts or rejects candidate particles based on their physical plausibility. In this work these discriminators are learned through a normalizing flow approach. Numerical experiments on both the Ikeda map and the Lorenz '63 system show that discriminator informed resampling procedure consistently reduces error relative to the standard EnGMF in low-ensemble regimes.

</details>


### [27] [Efficient Preference Poisoning Attack on Offline RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02495)
*Chenye Yang,Weiyu Xu,Lifeng Lai*

TL;DR: 本文研究针对离线RLHF（如DPO）的偏好标签翻转攻击，将攻击问题转化为结构化二进制稀疏逼近问题，提出BAL-A和BMP-A两种攻击方法，并从理论和实验两方面验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RLHF管道（如DPO）依赖预收集的偏好数据集进行训练，这使得它们容易受到偏好投毒攻击。现有研究尚未充分探索此类攻击的机制与有效方法。

Method: 首先证明翻转一个偏好标签会在DPO梯度中引入参数无关的偏移；基于此性质，将目标投毒问题转化为结构化二进制稀疏逼近问题。提出两种攻击方法：(1) BAL-A：将二进制翻转选择问题嵌入二进制感知格，利用LLL约简和Babai最近平面算法求解；(2) BMP-A：自适应二进制匹配追踪算法，处理非归一化梯度字典，提供基于相干性的恢复保证和鲁棒性证书。

Result: 在合成字典和Stanford Human Preferences数据集上的实验验证了理论分析，表明字典几何特性对攻击成功率有决定性影响。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了离线RLHF偏好学习在安全方面的脆弱性，提出的攻击方法为偏好投毒攻击提供了理论基础和实用工具，同时字典几何特性是影响攻击效果的关键因素。

Abstract: Offline Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) pipelines such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) train on a pre-collected preference dataset, which makes them vulnerable to preference poisoning attack. We study label flip attacks against log-linear DPO. We first illustrate that flipping one preference label induces a parameter-independent shift in the DPO gradient. Using this key property, we can then convert the targeted poisoning problem into a structured binary sparse approximation problem. To solve this problem, we develop two attack methods: Binary-Aware Lattice Attack (BAL-A) and Binary Matching Pursuit Attack (BMP-A). BAL-A embeds the binary flip selection problem into a binary-aware lattice and applies Lenstra-Lenstra-Lovász reduction and Babai's nearest plane algorithm; we provide sufficient conditions that enforce binary coefficients and recover the minimum-flip objective. BMP-A adapts binary matching pursuit to our non-normalized gradient dictionary and yields coherence-based recovery guarantees and robustness (impossibility) certificates for $K$-flip budgets. Experiments on synthetic dictionaries and the Stanford Human Preferences dataset validate the theory and highlight how dictionary geometry governs attack success.

</details>

Method: 采用Infoprop Dyna，一种基于不确定感知的模型强化学习（MBRL）框架，使机器人直接从真实世界交互中学习，无需物理仿真器。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has the potential to enable robots with fast, nonlinear, and unstable dynamics to reach the limits of their performance. However, most recent advances rely on carefully designed physics-based simulators and domain randomization to achieve successful sim-to-real transfer within reasonable wall-clock time. In this work, we bypass the need for such simulators and demonstrate that Infoprop Dyna, a state-of-the-art uncertainty-aware model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) framework, can enable robots to learn directly from real-world interactions. Using Infoprop Dyna, the Mini Wheelbot, an underactuated unicycle robot, learns to race around a track within 11 minutes of real-world experience.

</details>


### [29] [When Less is Enough: Efficient Inference via Collaborative Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01111)
*Yilei Chen,Sharut Gupta,Yannis Paschalidis,Ayush Sekhari,Aldo Pacchiano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DUET是一个两阶段协作推理框架，由能力强的模型生成推理信号，轻量级模型解读该信号生成最终答案，通过长度惩罚联合训练目标优化，在保持推理性能的同时将大模型输出token节省高达60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖单一大型模型进行端到端推理和预测往往会产生高昂的推理成本，需要在不牺牲任务性能的前提下降低推理开销。

Method: 提出DUET双模型高效两阶段推理框架：第一阶段由能力强的模型产生推理信号，第二阶段由轻量级模型解读该信号并生成最终答案。同时提出长度惩罚联合训练目标，鼓励能力强的模型仅传输对轻量级模型求解任务足够的信息。

Result: 在AIME和GPQA等具有挑战性的推理基准上，相比单独使用大型模型进行端到端推理，DUET节省了大模型高达60%的输出token，同时保持了强大的推理性能。

Conclusion: DUET通过将推理密集型计算分配给能力强的模型、非推理密集型部分委托给轻量级模型，实现了在显著降低推理成本的同时保持任务性能，是一种高效的协作推理范式。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce DUET (Dual-model Efficient Two-stage inference), a collaborative inference framework in which a capable model and a lightweight model work together to solve a task. Relying on a single large model to perform end-to-end reasoning and prediction often incurs substantial inference cost. In contrast, DUET decomposes inference into two stages: the capable model produces a reasoning signal, and the lightweight model interprets this signal to generate the final answer, allowing reasoning-intensive computation to be handled by the capable model while non-reasoning-intensive components are delegated to the lightweight model without sacrificing task performance. To achieve this objective, we propose a length-penalized joint training objective that encourages the capable model to transmit only the information that is sufficient for the lightweight model to solve the task. As a result, DUET maintains strong reasoning performance with substantially lower inference cost than end-to-end inference using a large model alone, saving up to 60% of the large model's output tokens on challenging reasoning benchmarks, including AIME and GPQA.

</details>


### [30] [Machine Learning-Augmented Acceleration of Iterative Ptychographic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01122)
*Bowen Zheng,Katayun Kamdin,David Shapiro,Alexander Ditter,Dayne Sasaki,Emma Bernard,Roopali Kukreja,Petrus H. Zwart,Slavomír Nemšák,Apurva Mehta,Nicholas Schwarz,Alexander Hexemer,Tanny Chavez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种机器学习增强的迭代叠层衍射成像重构算法，通过引入学习到的快速前向算子来加速收敛，在保持物理一致性的同时将收敛所需迭代次数减少两倍以上，并已实际部署于同步辐射光束线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的迭代叠层衍射成像重构算法在实际实验条件下收敛速度较慢，需要大量迭代才能达到收敛，限制了实时实验操作的效率。

Method: 在标准迭代初始化（warm-up）后，引入一个经过训练的机器学习快速前向算子，将当前状态直接推进到更接近收敛的状态，之后恢复传统迭代更新。模型在多样化叠层衍射数据集上训练，并在不同年份采集的实验数据上评估。

Result: 与常规迭代求解器相比，该方法在保持相当重构质量的同时，泊松负对数似然收敛更快，实际墙钟时间减少超过两倍。

Conclusion: 该方法已集成到现有重构流程中并在同步辐射光束线投入生产，证明了其在实时实验操作中的实用性和时间泛化能力。

Abstract: Iterative ptychographic reconstruction algorithms are widely used for coherent diffractive imaging but can exhibit slow convergence under realistic experimental conditions. We propose a machine learning-augmented approach that accelerates iterative ptychographic reconstruction by introducing a learned fast-forward operator applied during reconstruction. Following an initial warm-up using standard iterations, the fast-forward operator advances the reconstruction toward a more converged state, after which conventional iterative updates are resumed. This strategy preserves the physical consistency and flexibility of established ptychographic solvers while reducing the number of iterations required for convergence. The model is trained on diverse ptychographic datasets and evaluated on experimental data acquired in a different year, demonstrating robustness and temporal generalization. Compared with conventional iterative solvers, the machine learning-augmented method achieves comparable reconstruction quality while converging faster in terms of Poisson negative log-likelihood, yielding over a two-fold reduction in wall-clock time. The approach has been integrated into an existing reconstruction pipeline and deployed in production at a synchrotron beamline, demonstrating practicality for real-time experimental operation.

</details>


### [31] [Extreme Weather Bench: A framework and benchmark for evaluation of high-impact weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01126)
*Amy McGovern,Taylor Mandelbaum,Daniel Rothenberg,Nicholas Loveday,Corey Potvin,Montgomery Flora,Linus Magnusson,Eric Gilleland,John Allen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了极端天气基准（Extreme Weather Bench, EWB），一个由社区驱动的开源基准测试套件，旨在通过标准化的案例研究、观测数据、基于影响的指标和开源代码，全面评估AI和数值天气预报（NWP）模型在高影响天气事件上的表现，从而提升模型的可信度和科学进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI天气模型评估多采用全球尺度评估或少量案例/区域的手动选择，缺乏统一、全面的高影响天气基准。为像其他AI领域那样推动各尺度天气模型科学进步，亟需一个开源、社区驱动的基准测试套件。

Method: EWB提供了一个标准化的案例研究集（涵盖多种时空尺度和天气类型）、观测数据、基于影响的评估指标以及开源代码，供用户验证和比较其模型在高影响天气事件上的表现。该系统由社区驱动，将持续迭代增加更多现象、测试案例和指标。

Result: EWB作为一个开源社区驱动的基准套件，能够实现不同模型之间的真正比较，并通过对具体高影响现象的案例研究来评估模型。

Conclusion: 验证模型在一组标准案例（尤其是对公众高影响的事件）上的表现是提升AI模型可信度的关键。EWB将推动所有天气模型的科学进步，并通过与全球天气和预报验证社区合作持续发展。

Abstract: Forecasting the wide variety of high-impact weather events experienced globally is a challenge for both Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Numerical Weather Prediction (NWP) models and it is critical that such models be properly verified before deployment. Although AI weather models are rapidly evolving, much of their evaluation is currently done either with a global-scale evaluation or by hand-picking a small number of case studies or a region. A widely-used open-source benchmark suite focusing on high-impact weather will help to drive the science forward for all scales of weather models, as it has for other AI fields. Here we introduce Extreme Weather Bench (EWB), a new community-driven benchmark suite that facilitates model validation and verification on a variety of high-impact hazards that matter to people around the globe. EWB provides a standard set of case studies (spanning across multiple spatial and temporal scales and different parts of the weather spectrum), observational data, impact-based metrics, and open-source code for users to evaluate their models. Verifying that a model works against a standard set of case studies, especially events that are high-impact for the general public, is a key piece of improving the trustworthiness of AI models. EWB will help to drive the science forward for all weather models, enabling true comparisons across models and evaluating models on specific high-impact phenomena through the use of case studies. EWB is a free open-source community-driven system and will continue to evolve to include additional phenomena, test cases and metrics in collaboration with the worldwide weather and forecast verification community.

</details>


### [32] [Forager: a lightweight testbed for continual learning with partial observability in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01131)
*Steven Tang,Xinze Xiong,Anna Hakhverdyan,Andrew Patterson,Jacob Adkins,Jiamin He,Esraa Elelimy,Parham Mohammad Panahi,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Forager，一个轻量级、部分可观测的持续强化学习（CRL）环境，用于研究CRL智能体在部分可观测性下的学习能力丧失问题，并证明了利用状态构建比现有缓解方法更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRL实验大多关注完全可观测MDP中添加不可观测非平稳性导致的塑性丧失问题，忽略了部分可观测性的重要性，且许多部分可观测CRL环境计算成本过高。

Method: 设计并实现了Forager环境，具有恒定内存开销的轻量级部分可观测CRL环境，提供一系列实验和示例任务。

Result: Forager对当前CRL智能体具有挑战性；智能体表现出塑性丧失；提出的缓解措施有一定帮助；但最有用的方法是利用状态构建。

Conclusion: Forager变体能生成无尽的新任务流，清晰揭示了当前CRL智能体的局限性，为部分可观测CRL研究提供了有效平台。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning (CRL), good performance requires never-ending learning, acting, and exploration in a big, partially observable world. Most CRL experiments have focused on loss of plasticity -- the inability to keep learning -- in one-off experiments where some unobservable non-stationarity is added to classic fully observable MDPs. Further, these experiments rarely consider the role of partial observability and the importance of CRL agents that use memory or recurrence. One potential reason for this focus on mitigating loss of plasticity without considering partial observability is that many partially-observable CRL environments are prohibitively expensive. In this paper, we introduce Forager, a light-weight partially-observable CRL environment with a constant memory footprint. We provide a set of experiments and sample tasks demonstrating that Forager is challenging for current CRL agents and yet also allows for in-depth study of those agents. We demonstrate that agents exhibit loss of plasticity, proposed mitigations can help, but that most useful is to leverage state construction. We conclude with a variant of Forager that generates an unending stream of new tasks to learn that clearly highlights the limitations of current CRL agents.

</details>

Abstract: ARC-AGI-2 is a benchmark of human-intuitive visual puzzles that measures a machine's ability to generalize from limited examples, interpret symbolic meaning, and flexibly apply rules in varying contexts. In this paper, we discuss our approach to solving the ARC-AGI-2 puzzles with TinyLM, with additional fine-tuning at test time, including Test-Time-Training (TTT) and Products of Experts (POE). Our model achieves 96.1% accuracy on the training set and 21.7% accuracy on the evaluation set.

### [34] [Minimizing Collateral Damage in Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01167)
*Tam Nguyen,Tu Anh Nguyen,Sina Alemohammad,Richard G. Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束优化的激活引导方法，通过考虑激活值的经验二阶矩矩阵来加权非目标特征的预期平方附带变化，从而在控制LLM行为时减少对无关任务性能的损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的激活引导方法（如向量加法）在改变模型行为时会导致"附带损害"，即无意中改变非目标特征方向的激活对齐，这是因为标准方法隐含地假设非目标特征是各向同性的。作者旨在数学形式化这一损害并提出更精确的控制方法。

Method: 将激活引导建模为约束优化问题，寻找新激活值以最小化由激活值经验二阶矩矩阵加权的预期平方附带变化，该权重编码了不同特征方向上扰动的非均匀成本，区别于各向同性方法。

Result: 所提方法实现了更精确的激活控制，同时减少了对无关任务性能的退化。

Conclusion: 通过考虑激活值的经验二阶矩结构，可以有效缓解标准激活引导方法中的附带损害问题，在保持目标特征控制精度的同时更好地保留模型在无关任务上的表现。

Abstract: Activation steering is a method for controlling Large Language Model (LLM) behavior by intervening in its internal representations to increase the alignment with a specific target feature direction. However, standard interventions, such as vector addition, often cause ``collateral damage", defined as unintended changes in the alignment of activations along other non-target feature directions. This damage occurs because standard methods implicitly assume the isotropy of non-target features. In this work, we provide a mathematical formalization of collateral damage and introduce a principled framework that models steering as a constrained optimization problem. Our method finds a new activation that minimizes the expected squared collateral change weighted by the empirical second-moment matrix of activations. This weighting encodes the nonuniform cost of the perturbation in different feature directions, in contrast to isotropic approaches that penalize changes uniformly in all feature directions. By accounting for the empirical second-moment of activations, our approach achieves more precise control while reducing the degradation of model performance on unrelated tasks.

</details>


### [35] [Linear-Readout Floors and Threshold Recovery in Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01192)
*Hector Borobia,Elies Seguí-Mas,Guillermina Tormo-Carbó*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出Hänni等人与Adler & Shavit关于叠加计算中递归容量看似矛盾的结论（$O(d^{3/2})$ vs 近二次容量）实际上并不冲突，因二者维持不同的接口不变量。通过秩-迹Welch型下界证明单位对角线性读出最坏情况串扰为$\Omega(d^{-1/2})$，且该下界在平均意义下对单位范数紧框架是紧的。在二次特征负载$F=d^2$下，随机支撑阈值恢复可在稀疏度$s=O(d/\log d)$时成功，而线性读出在伯努利稀疏状态下仍遭受$\Omega(s/d)$平均每坐标平方误差。匹配Welch下界与Hänni修正层的容差解释了$d^{3/2}$尺度是该模板的兼容性阈值而非通用上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和叠加计算中两个看似矛盾的递归容量结论，澄清其冲突源于不同的接口不变量（interface invariants），而非理论上的不一致。

Method: 1) 推导秩-迹Welch型下界，刻画单位对角线性读出的最坏情况串扰；2) 对比线性读出与阈值非线性恢复在不同稀疏度下的性能；3) 将Welch下界与Hänni修正层容差匹配，解释其$d^{3/2}$尺度的来源。

Result: 1) 单位对角线性读出最坏情况串扰$\Omega(d^{-1/2})$，该下界对单位范数紧框架是紧的；2) 随机支撑阈值恢复在$F=d^2, s=O(d/\log d)$时成功，而线性读出误差$\Omega(s/d)$；3) $d^{3/2}$尺度是Hänni模板的兼容性阈值，非通用上界。

Conclusion: 两个容量结论并不矛盾，区别在于接口不变量不同；稳健的非线性重置超越Hänni模板仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Two recent approaches to computation in superposition reach different recursive capacity regimes: Hänni et al. certify $\tilde{O}(d^{3/2})$ computable features in width $d$ via an approximate-linear recursive template, while Adler and Shavit reach near-quadratic capacity (up to logarithmic factors) using thresholded Boolean recovery. The main contribution of this paper is conceptual: we argue these results are not contradictory because they maintain different interface invariants, and we formalize the distinction.
  As a tool, we record a rank-trace Welch-type lower bound for biorthogonal linear readouts: for $F \gg d$, the worst-case off-diagonal cross-talk of any unit-diagonal linear readout is $Ω(d^{-1/2})$, and the bound is tight on average for unit-norm tight frames. At quadratic feature load $F=d^2$, random-support threshold recovery succeeds for sparsities $s=O(d/\log d)$, while linear readouts still incur $Ω(s/d)$ average per-coordinate squared error on Bernoulli sparse states. Matching the Welch floor against the published tolerance of the Hänni correction layer explains the $d^{3/2}$ scale as a compatibility threshold for that template, not a universal upper bound. Robust nonlinear reset beyond the Hänni template is left open.

</details>

Result: 识别出注意力学习中存在的递归式聚焦-稀释循环，并严格分解为四个阶段：嵌入和投影快速凝聚为秩一结构；注意力参数增长并引发高频聚焦；注意力演化导致嵌入产生高阶扰动，触发质量再分配机制稀释聚焦；低频token间的微小不对称性打破退化临界点，开启新循环。

Conclusion: 该循环动态在合成马尔可夫数据、WikiText和TinyStories上均得到验证，为理解Transformer训练动力学提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have achieved remarkable success across a wide range of domains, yet our understanding of their training dynamics remains limited. In this work, we identify a recurrent focus-dilution cycle in attention learning and provide a rigorous explanation in a one-layer Transformer setting for Markovian data via gradient-flow analysis. Using stage-wise linearization around critical points, we show that a single focus-dilution cycle can be decomposed into a sequence of distinct stages. First, embedding and projection rapidly condense to a rank-one structure, while attention parameters remain effectively frozen. Then, the attention parameters begin to increase, inducing a frequency-driven focus toward high-frequency tokens. As attention continues to evolve, it generates next-order perturbations in embeddings, leading to a mass-redistribution mechanism that progressively dilutes this focus. Finally, small asymmetries among low-frequency tokens lift a degenerate critical point, opening new embedding directions and initiating the next cycle. Experiments on synthetic Markovian data as well as WikiText and TinyStories corroborate the predicted stages and cyclical dynamics.

</details>


### [37] [Local Hessian Spectral Filtering for Robust Intrinsic Dimension Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01221)
*Genki Osada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出局部Hessian谱维度（LHSD）方法，通过对log密度Hessian进行谱滤波来估计局部本征维度，解决了扩散模型在高维空间中现有LID方法失效的问题，并使用随机Lanczos求积实现线性可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的局部本征维度（LID）估计方法在高维空间中失效，原因是来自大量法线方向的噪声淹没了切线方向的信号。

Method: 提出LHSD方法，对log密度Hessian矩阵进行谱滤波，显式截断与法线方向相关的大特征值，从而计数零曲率的切线方向；使用随机Lanczos求积（SLQ）实现，避免构建完整Hessian矩阵，达到与维度D的线性可扩展性。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明LHSD具有优越的鲁棒性，并能有效检测大规模扩散模型中的记忆化现象。

Conclusion: LHSD通过谱滤波策略解决了高维空间中LID估计的噪声问题，兼具理论有效性和计算可行性。

Abstract: While diffusion models enable new approaches for estimating Local Intrinsic Dimension (LID), existing methods fail in high-dimensional spaces where noise from vast normal directions overwhelms the tangent signal. We propose Local Hessian Spectral Dimension (LHSD), which resolves this by applying spectral filtering to the log-density Hessian, explicitly cutting off large eigenvalues associated with normal directions to count zero-curvature tangent directions. Implemented using Stochastic Lanczos Quadrature (SLQ), LHSD avoids full Hessian construction, achieving linear scalability with dimension $D$. Experiments on synthetic and real data confirm LHSD's superior robustness and its utility in detecting memorization in large-scale diffusion models.

</details>


### [38] [Arbitrarily Conditioned Hierarchical Flows for Spatiotemporal Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01226)
*Keyan Chen,Qiwei Yuan,Zhitong Xu,Bin Shen,Shandian Zhe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ARCH（任意条件层级流模型），一种基于层级流匹配的时空事件建模框架。它采用历史编码器-生成解码器架构和混合掩码策略，支持统一处理预测、逆向推断和轨迹重建等任务，在合成和真实数据集上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点过程模型依赖强结构假设，通常局限于自回归的逐事件预测，难以支持更广泛的推断任务，如逆向推断、轨迹重建和缺失事件位置恢复。

Method: 提出ARCH框架，基于层级流匹配（hierarchical flow matching），采用历史编码器-生成解码器架构，并引入混合掩码策略（hybrid masking strategy），实现对任意观测事件的灵活条件建模。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，ARCH在预测和条件推断任务中均持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: ARCH提供了一个统一的框架，能够同时处理预测、逆向推断和部分轨迹恢复等多种推断任务，且更具表达力和准确性。

Abstract: Events in spatiotemporal systems are ubiquitous, yet modeling their complex distributions remains challenging. Existing point process models often rely on strong structural assumptions and are typically limited to autoregressive, event-by-event prediction. As a result, they struggle to support broader inference tasks such as inverse inference, trajectory reconstruction, and recovery of missing event locations. We introduce Arbitrarily Conditioned Hierarchical Flows (ARCH), a hierarchical flow matching framework for spatiotemporal event modeling. ARCH is expressive enough to capture complex event distributions while enabling tractable and accurate computation of conditional intensities, which quantify instantaneous event risk. Built on a history-encoder-generative-decoder architecture, ARCH introduces a hybrid masking strategy for flexible conditioning on arbitrary observed events. This enables a unified treatment of forecasting, inverse inference, and partial trajectory recovery within a single framework. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that ARCH consistently outperforms existing baselines across both prediction and conditional inference tasks.

</details>


### [39] [Attention Sinks in Massively Multilingual Neural Machine Translation:Discovery, Analysis, and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01229)
*Hillary Mutisya,John Mugane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NLLB-200模型中83%-91%的跨注意力被非内容标记（句尾符、语言标签、标点）占据，导致原始指标严重低估内容相似度。通过内容过滤方法可恢复真实的语言对齐信号，并在非洲语言等测试中验证了该伪影的普遍性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NMT跨注意力分析未注意到非内容标记占据绝大多数注意力权重，导致对模型语言对齐结构的评估存在系统性偏差。

Method: 1) 量化NLLB-200中非内容标记的注意力占比；2) 提出内容过滤方法（移除非内容标记并重新归一化）；3) 在8种语言（含4种非洲语言）的1000个平行句上进行验证。

Result: 1) 非内容标记占据83%-91%注意力；2) 原始内容相似度36.7% vs 过滤后70.7%；3) 发现教师强制与生成模式间16.9个百分点的差距；4) 揭示语言家族聚类和索马里语的SOV单调对齐悖论。

Conclusion: 跨注意力"注意力沉没"伪影是词汇设计而非位置偏差导致的因果机制，内容过滤是必要的前处理步骤，文章开源了相关工具和校正数据集。

Abstract: Cross-attention patterns in neural machine translation (NMT) are widely used to study how multilingual models align linguistic structure. We report a systematic artifact in cross-attention analysis of NLLB-200 (600M): non-content tokens - primarily end-of-sequence tokens, language tags, and punctuation - capture 83 percent to 91 percent of total cross-attention mass. We term these "attention sinks," extending findings from LLMs [Xiao et al., 2023] to NMT cross-attention and identifying a causal mechanism rooted in vocabulary design rather than position bias. This artifact causes raw metrics to underestimate content-level similarity by nearly half (36.7 percent raw vs. 70.7 percent filtered), rendering uncorrected analyses unreliable. To address this, we validate a content-only filtering methodology that removes non-content tokens and renormalizes the distribution. Applying this to 1,000 parallel sentences across African languages (Swahili, Kikuyu, Somali, Luo) and non-African benchmarks (German, Turkish, Chinese, Hindi), we confirm the artifact is universal and recover masked linguistic signals: a 16.9 percentage-point gap between teacher-forcing and generation modes, clear language-family clustering in attention entropy, and a hidden Somali paradox linking SOV word order to monotonic alignment. We release our filtering toolkit and corrected datasets to support reproducible interpretability research on multilingual NMT.

</details>


### [40] [Rhamba: Region-Aware Hybrid Attention-Mamba Framework for Self-Supervised Learning in Resting-State fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01240)
*Ruthwik Reddy Doodipala,Pankaj Pandey,Pratheek Eranki,Carolina Torres-Rojas,Manob Jyoti Saikia,Ranganatha Sitaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rhamba框架，将解剖引导掩码与混合Attention-Mamba架构相结合用于fMRI预训练，在ABIDE数据集上预训练并微调于COBRE和ADHD-200数据集，发现掩码策略与架构交互影响性能，混合MA架构取得最优平均AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督预训练在神经影像分析中具有潜力，但区域感知掩码和混合序列建模的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Rhamba框架，包含解剖引导掩码（Any/Majority/Pure三种策略）和四种架构变体（Mamba only、Alternate、AM、MA），在ABIDE数据集上进行预训练，然后在下游分类任务上微调。

Result: 掩码策略强烈影响重构行为（Any > Majority > Pure），但该趋势未直接转化为下游性能提升；MA混合架构取得最高平均AUROC；Rhamba优于现有方法；区域分析显示性能峰值取决于掩码策略与架构的交互。

Conclusion: Rhamba提供了一个灵活框架，在大规模fMRI表示学习中平衡可解释性、可扩展性和性能。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining is promising for large-scale neuroimaging, yet the impact of region-aware masking and hybrid sequence modeling remains underexplored. In this work, we introduce Rhamba, a region-aware pretraining framework that integrates anatomically guided masking with hybrid Attention-Mamba architectures for resting state functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) analysis. Models were pretrained on the ABIDE dataset using region-aligned patch embeddings and three masking strategies (Any, Majority, and Pure) with increasing spatial specificity. We evaluated four architectural variants: a Mamba only model, an Alternate architecture with interleaved Mamba and Attention blocks, and two hybrid encoder-decoder configurations (Attention-Mamba (AM) and Mamba-Attention (MA)). The pretrained models were fine-tuned on downstream classification tasks using the COBRE and ADHD-200 datasets for schizophrenia and attention-deficit/hyperactivity disorder discrimination. We employed Integrated Gradients, an explainable AI method, to identify the brain regions contributing to model predictions. Masking strategy strongly influenced reconstruction behavior, with reconstruction loss following a consistent ordering (Any > Majority > Pure).
