PepSpecBench standardizes data preprocessing across complementary public datasets, enforces a strict backbone-disjoint splitting strategy to eliminate sequence leakage, and evaluates diverse architectures within a shared fragment-ion representation space. It further introduces a comprehensive multi-species evaluation suite and physically grounded metadata perturbation probes to assess model robustness and instrument awareness. We uncover previously unrecognized performance discrepancies and robustness limitations across six representative models, providing actionable insights for future model design, evaluation and practical deployment.

</details>


### [78] [TRAP: Tail-aware Ranking Attack for World-Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01950)
*Siyuan Duan,Ke Zhang,Xizhao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对世界模型的新型后门攻击框架TRAP，利用想象轨迹的排序结构漏洞，通过尾部感知排序损失和双门控机制，在触发条件下重定向规划结果，导致性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击主要针对局部特征或单步预测，对于具有动力学先验和规划过程的世界模型效果不佳，因为浅层扰动会被吸收或稀释。需要探索世界模型特有的后门漏洞。

Method: 提出TRAP框架：(1) 尾部感知排序损失，聚焦于决策关键轨迹的优化；(2) 双门控机制，稳定优化并控制攻击惩罚的时机与位置。

Result: 在DreamerV3和TD-MPC2上的多任务实验表明，TRAP能够持续诱导行为偏差并导致显著的性能下降。

Conclusion: 世界模型存在基于想象轨迹排序结构的独特后门漏洞，需要针对世界模型智能体进行专门的安全性评估。

Abstract: World models enable long-horizon planning by internally generating and evaluating imagined trajectories, making them a promising foundation for generalist agents. However, this imagination-driven decision process also introduces new security risks. Existing backdoor attacks typically aim to manipulate local features, one-step predictions, or instantaneous policy outputs. While such objectives may suffice for weaker reactive models, they are often ineffective against world models, where the learned dynamics prior and planning process can absorb or wash out the effects of shallow perturbations. More importantly, we find that world models exhibit a distinct backdoor vulnerability rooted in the long-tailed ranking structure of imagined trajectories, where disrupting the ordering of a few decision-critical trajectories can systematically hijack planning.
  To exploit this vulnerability, we propose TRAP, a backdoor attack framework for world models that targets imagined trajectory ranking. TRAP combines a tail-aware ranking loss to focus optimization on decision-critical trajectories with dual gating mechanisms that stabilize optimization and regulate when and where the attack penalty is applied. Under trigger conditions, TRAP alters the relative ranking of imagined trajectories to redirect planning outcomes, while largely maintaining the normal ranking structure on clean inputs. Experiments on DreamerV3 and TD-MPC2 across diverse tasks show that TRAP consistently induces sustained behavioral deviations and significant performance degradation, highlighting the need for dedicated security evaluation of world-model-based agents.

</details>


### [79] [Flexi-LoRA with Input-Adaptive Ranks: Efficient Finetuning for Speech and Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01959)
*Zongqian Li,Yixuan Su,Han Zhou,Zihao Fu,Nigel Collier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flexi-LoRA提出了一种基于输入复杂度动态调整LoRA秩的参数高效微调框架，在训练和推理中保持秩动态一致性，以更少参数在问答、数学推理和语音任务上超越静态LoRA，尤其对严格推理链任务提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）采用静态参数分配，无法适应不同复杂度的输入，导致资源浪费或性能不足。

Method: 提出Flexi-LoRA框架，根据输入复杂度动态调整LoRA秩，并在训练和推理阶段保持秩分配的一致性。

Result: 在问答、数学推理和语音任务上，Flexi-LoRA以更少参数持续优于静态LoRA；数学推理任务对秩动态的依赖性高于QA任务；性能提升在严格推理链任务上更为显著。

Conclusion: 输入依赖的参数分配能以更少参数实现更高性能，通过精简实现混合专家框架的关键优势，为未来输入自适应高效微调研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods like Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) have become essential for deploying large language models, yet their static parameter allocation remains suboptimal for inputs of varying complexity. We present Flexi-LoRA, a novel framework that dynamically adjusts LoRA ranks based on input complexity during both training and inference. Through empirical analysis across question answering, mathematical reasoning, and speech tasks, we demonstrate that maintaining consistency between training and inference dynamics is important for effective adaptation, particularly for sequential reasoning tasks. Our findings reveal that input-dependent parameter allocation achieves higher performance with fewer parameters by optimally matching rank configurations to question complexity. Furthermore, task-specific dependency on rank dynamics varies, with mathematical reasoning tasks exhibiting higher dependency than QA tasks. Successful adaptation manifests not only in correctness but also in reasoning quality and instruction adherence. Flexi-LoRA consistently outperforms static LoRA while using fewer parameters, with performance gains more pronounced on tasks requiring strict reasoning chains. Our approach realizes key benefits of mixture-of-experts frameworks through a more streamlined implementation, reducing parameter redundancy while improving model capabilities. We provide comprehensive empirical studies across diverse tasks, establishing a basis for future work in input-adaptive and efficient fine-tuning approaches.

</details>


### [80] [Multi-User Dueling Bandits: A Fair Approach using Nash Social Welfare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01961)
*Maheed H. Ahmed,Mahsa Ghasemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在异构用户偏好下的公平性决斗老虎机问题，采用纳什社会福利目标来促进公平，并首次给出了该问题的 regret 下界 Ω(T^{2/3} min(K,D)^{1/3})，同时提出了 Fair-Explore-Then-Commit 和 Fair-ε-Greedy 两种算法，其 regret 上界在 T 的依赖上与下界匹配（至多对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好学习模型通常基于所有人类评估者的平均偏好进行训练，当偏好存在较大差异时，这种平均化处理会对少数群体不公平。本文旨在解决异构用户偏好下的公平性决斗老虎机问题。

Method: 将每个用户视为具有（可能不同的）Condorcet 胜者，以此作为基准评估臂的表现；采用纳什社会福利目标（最大化用户效用的乘积）来惩罚不平等；提出 Fair-Explore-Then-Commit 和 Fair-ε-Greedy 两种算法，包含 Condorcet 胜者识别阶段。

Result: 建立了 regret 下界 Ω(T^{2/3} min(K,D)^{1/3})，这是首个量化异构偏好决斗老虎机中公平性代价的结果；所提算法的 regret 上界在 T 的依赖上与下界匹配（至多对数因子）。

Conclusion: 本文首次在决斗老虎机框架中量化了公平性的代价，并提出了达到近乎最优 regret 的算法，为异构偏好下的公平在线学习提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Learning from human preference data is becoming a useful tool, from fine-tuning large language models to training reinforcement learning agents. However, in most scenarios, the model is trained on the average preference of all human evaluators, which, under large variations of preferences, can be unfair to minority groups. In this work, we consider fairness in dueling bandits, a standard framework for online learning from preference data. We assume that each user has a (potentially distinct) Condorcet winner, which is an arm preferred to every other arm. Using these user-specific Condorcet winners as reference points, we evaluate and score arms according to their performance relative to the corresponding winner. To promote fairness across heterogeneous users, we adopt the well-established Nash Social Welfare objective, which maximizes the product of user utilities, thereby inherently penalizing inequality and preventing the marginalization of any single user. Within this framework, we construct a hard instance to establish a regret lower bound of $Ω(T^{2/3}\min(K,D)^\frac{1}{3})$ for a time horizon $T$, $K$ arms, and $D$ users, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first result quantifying the cost of fairness in dueling bandits with heterogeneous preferences. We then present the Fair-Explore-Then-Commit and Fair-$ε$-Greedy algorithms with a Condorcet winner identification phase. We further derive their regret upper bounds that match the lower-bound dependence on $T$ up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [81] [Retrieval with Multiple Query Vectors through Anomalous Pattern Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01965)
*Allassan Tchangmena A Nken,Baimam Boukar Jean Jacques,Miriam Rateike,Celia Cintas,Skyler Speakman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于异常模式检测的多查询向量检索方法，通过识别查询集中异常维度并检索在该维度上同样异常的数据库向量，实验表明增加查询数量能提升检索性能，尤其在从1到8的范围内提升最显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统向量检索通常只考虑单个查询向量，但复杂推理和检索任务往往需要多个查询向量同时输入。现有方法无法有效利用多个查询向量之间的共同信息进行检索。

Method: 给定一个查询向量集合Q（|Q|≥1），首先识别Q中那些在维度上显著异常（区别于其他维度）的子集；然后扫描向量数据库，检索出在同样这些维度上也呈现异常的向量作为检索结果。

Result: 在两个图像数据集、一个文本数据集和一个表格数据集上进行了验证。总体而言，更大的查询集带来更好的检索性能，其中从1个查询增加到8个查询时提升最为显著，继续增加查询数量则收益递减。

Conclusion: 多查询向量检索通过异常模式检测是有效的，且查询集规模与检索性能呈正相关但边际收益递减。

Abstract: A classical vector retrieval problem typically considers a \emph{single} query embedding vector as input and retrieves the most similar embedding vectors from a vector database. However, complex reasoning and retrieval tasks frequently require \emph{multiple query vectors}, rather than a single one. In this work, we propose a retrieval method that considers multiple query vectors simultaneously and retrieves the most relevant vectors from the database using concepts from anomalous pattern detection. Specifically, our approach leverages a set of query vectors $Q$ (with $|Q|\geq 1$), and identifies the subset of vector dimensions within $Q$ that standout (anomalous) from the rest of dimensions. Next, we scan the vector database to retrieve the set of vectors that are also anomalous across the previously identified vector dimensions and return them as our retrieved set of vectors. We validate our approach on two image datasets, a text dataset, and a tabular dataset. Overall, we observe that, across most datasets, larger query sets lead to improved retrieval performance. The improvement is most pronounced when increasing the query sets from 1 to 8, while the gains become smaller beyond that.

</details>


### [82] [MER-DG: Modality-Entropy Regularization for Multimodal Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01967)
*Yavuz Yarici,Ghassan AlRegib*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多模态模型在领域泛化中面临“融合过拟合”问题，即联合优化导致编码器利用模态间共现的领域特定统计关系而非学习不变特征。本文提出MER-DG方法，通过最大化编码器特征分布的熵来保持特征多样性，在EPIC-Kitchens和HAC基准上平均提升约5%（vs标准融合）和约2%（vs现有最优方法）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准多模态架构采用联合优化方式，导致编码器倾向于利用源域中特定的模态间共现关系（cross-modal co-occurrences），而非学习领域不变特征，从而在目标域泛化时性能下降。

Method: 提出MER-DG（模态熵正则化用于领域泛化），通过最大化每个编码器特征分布的熵来保持特征多样性；该方法与架构无关，可作为附加损失项集成到现有多模态框架中。

Result: 在EPIC-Kitchens和HAC基准上，相比标准融合平均提升约5%，相比现有最优方法平均提升约2%。

Conclusion: 识别了多模态领域泛化中的“融合过拟合”失效模式，并提出MER-DG有效缓解该问题，验证了特征熵正则化的有效性。

Abstract: Deploying multimodal models in real-world scenarios requires generalization to new environments where recording conditions differ from training, a challenge known as multimodal domain generalization (MMDG). Standard architectures employ separate encoders for each modality and a fusion module, training the system end-to-end by optimizing on the fused features. In this paper, we identify that such joint optimization causes encoders to exploit cross-modal co-occurrences, statistical relationships between modalities that arise from source-specific recording conditions, rather than learning domain-invariant features. We term this failure mode Fusion Overfitting. To address this, we propose Modality-Entropy Regularization for Domain Generalization (MER-DG), which maximizes the entropy of each encoder's feature distribution to preserve feature diversity. MER-DG is architecture-agnostic and integrates into existing multimodal frameworks as an additive loss term. Extensive experiments on EPIC-Kitchens and HAC benchmarks demonstrate average improvements of approximately 5% over standard fusion and approximately 2% over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [83] [RamanBench: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Machine Learning on Raman Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02003)
*Mario Koddenbrock,Christoph Lange,Robin Legner,Martin Jäger,Martin Kögler,Mariano N. Cruz Bournazou,Peter Neubauer,Felix Biessmann,Erik Rodner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RamanBench 是首个大规模、完全可复现的拉曼光谱机器学习基准测试，统一了74个数据集（含16个新发布）、325,668条光谱，覆盖4个领域。基准测试了28个模型，发现表格基础模型（如TabPFN）优于领域特定方法，但尚无方法能跨数据集泛化，揭示了关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉曼光谱领域缺乏标准化基准，存在数据集碎片化、评估不一致、模型无法捕捉光谱数据结构等问题，限制了ML在该领域的进展。

Method: 构建了包含74个数据集（325,668条光谱）的统一基准，涵盖分类与回归任务，标准化评估协议；基准测试了28种模型，包括经典方法（PLS）、拉曼专用模型（RamanNet）、表格基础模型（TabPFN）和时间序列方法（ROCKET）。

Result: 表格基础模型（TFM）持续优于领域专用方法和梯度提升基线，时间序列模型具有竞争力，但没有任何方法能在数据集间泛化。

Conclusion: 当前无方法能跨数据集泛化，存在根本性差距，邀请社区贡献新方法以推动医疗诊断、生物研究和材料科学等关键应用的发展。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) has transformed many scientific fields, yet key applications still lack standardized benchmarks. Raman spectroscopy, a widely used technique for non-invasive molecular analysis, is one such field where progress is limited by fragmented datasets, inconsistent evaluation, and models that fail to capture the structure of spectral data. We introduce RamanBench, the first large-scale, fully reproducible benchmark for ML on Raman spectroscopy, consisting of streamlined data access, evaluation protocols and code, as well as a live leaderboard. It unifies 74 datasets (including 16 first released with this benchmark) across four domains, comprising 325,668 spectra and spanning classification and regression tasks under diverse experimental conditions. We benchmark 28 models under a standardized protocol, including classical methods (e.g., PLS), Raman-specific (e.g., RamanNet), Tabular Foundation Model (TFM) (e.g., TabPFN), and time-series approaches (e.g., ROCKET). TFM consistently outperform domain-specific and gradient boosting baselines, while time-series models remain competitive. However, no method generalizes across datasets, revealing a fundamental gap. Therefore, we invite the community to contribute new approaches to our living benchmark, with the potential to accelerate advances in critical applications such as medical diagnostics, biological research, and materials science.

</details>


### [84] [How Can One Choose the Best CAM-Based Explainability Method for a CNN Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02007)
*Daniel da Silva Costa,Pedro Nuno de Souza Moura,Adriana C. F. Alvim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用多种距离度量（如曼哈顿距离和相关性）来评估可解释性方法生成的显著性图与人类感知的相似度，通过众包排序和RBO指标比较，发现LayerCAM、Score-CAM和IS-CAM最符合人类感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法评估主要依赖IoU指标，但显著性图缺乏已知形状，IoU可能不是最佳度量。作者假设存在更优的度量方法，能筛选出最接近人类感知的可解释性方法。

Method: 使用ImageNet中Chihuahua图像子集，应用多种CAM-based可解释性方法生成显著性图；采用多种距离度量（曼哈顿距离、相关性等）计算显著性图与人工标注边界框的对齐程度；通过众包收集人类对最佳显著性图的偏好排序；使用Rank-Biased Overlap (RBO)指标比较不同度量的排序与人类排序的一致性。

Result: 曼哈顿距离和相关性是最符合人类感知的两个度量指标；LayerCAM、Score-CAM和IS-CAM是三种最符合人类感知的可解释性方法。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效找到最接近人类感知的可解释性方法，为可解释性评估提供了超越传统IoU的新思路。

Abstract: In recent years, several advances have been observed in Deep Learning with surprising results. Models in this area have been increasingly used in numerous applications, including those sensitive to human life, which require clear explanations and justifications. Various explainability methods have been proposed, but not many metrics to evaluate these methods. The most commonly used metric is the Intersection over Union (IoU). However, due to the characteristics of the results of the explainability methods, called saliency maps, which do not have a known shape, we hypothesise that there must be a better metric that allows one to find an explainability method that produces results that best resemble the human perception. We propose using different metrics to assess the similarity between human perception and the explanation saliency maps to find a better metric. An investigation was conducted employing a subset of the Chihuahuas images from ImageNet dataset. Several CAM-based explainability methods were used to generate saliency maps for each chihuahua image. Alignment was measured by applying distance metrics between the bounding box of human annotations and the saliency maps produced by each explainability method. Rankings of the best saliency maps were created using the results of the distance metrics and compared to the ranking obtained using people's choice, collected through crowdsourcing, of the best explanation saliency maps for each selected image. Comparison between rankings was performed using the Rank-Biased Overlap (RBO) metric. The results indicate the feasibility of our method to find the explainability method that best resembles human perception. In our experiments, the two metrics that best resemble human perception corresponded to Manhattan and Correlation. Besides, the best explainability methods regarding human perception were LayerCAM, Score-CAM, and IS-CAM.

</details>


### [85] [Bringing Order to Asynchronous SGD: Towards Optimality under Data-Dependent Delays with Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02043)
*Tehila Dahan,Roie Reshef,Sharon Goldstein,Kfir Y. Levy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于动量的异步SGD框架，在数据依赖延迟的凸与非凸平滑场景下首次实现最优收敛速率，并设计了鲁棒的学习率调度方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步SGD方法处理梯度滞后时存在系统性偏差（简单样本过表达、复杂样本被抑制），且数据依赖延迟场景下Lipschitz假设导致次优收敛率，凸平滑情形未被充分研究。

Method: 提出基于动量（momentum）的异步优化框架，保留延迟梯度信息而非简单衰减或丢弃；推导出适用于数据依赖延迟的鲁棒学习率调度策略。

Result: 在凸与非凸平滑设定下，针对数据依赖延迟首次证明了最优收敛速率；提供了简化的超参数调优方案。

Conclusion: 动量机制能有效缓解梯度滞后偏差，实现了异步SGD在标准假设下的理论最优收敛保证，并降低了实际调参复杂度。

Abstract: Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (SGD) enables scalable distributed training but suffers from gradient staleness. Existing mitigation strategies, such as delay-adaptive learning rates and staleness-aware filtering, typically attenuate or discard delayed gradients, introducing systematic bias: updates from simpler or faster-to-process samples are overrepresented, while gradients from more complex samples are delayed or suppressed. In contrast, prior approaches to data-dependent delays rely on a Lipschitz assumption that yields suboptimal rates or leave the smooth, convex case unaddressed. We propose a momentum-based asynchronous framework designed to preserve information from delayed gradients while mitigating the effects of staleness. We establish the first optimal convergence rates for data-dependent delays in both convex and non-convex smooth setups, providing a new result for asynchronous optimization under standard assumptions. Additionally, we derive robust learning-rate schedules that simplify hyperparameter tuning in practice.

</details>


### [86] [DR-SNE: Density-Regularized Stochastic Neighbor Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02060)
*Maksim Kazanskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DR-SNE通过在随机邻域嵌入中引入密度正则化项，在保持局部邻域结构的同时更好地保留了数据密度信息，在异常检测等密度敏感任务上表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有降维方法如t-SNE虽能保持局部邻域结构，但未显式考虑概率质量的分布方式，常导致数据密度信息失真。

Method: 将降维重新表述为条件结构（局部关系）和相对密度结构（局部密度统计）的联合对齐，提出DR-SNE，在SNE目标函数中增加基于归一化对数密度估计的密度正则化项。

Result: DR-SNE在保持竞争性邻域保真度的同时改善了密度保存，在多个数据集的异常检测等密度敏感任务上取得性能提升。

Conclusion: 在降维中融入密度信息能够补充仅关注几何结构的目标，为密度敏感任务提供更优的低维表示。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction methods such as t-SNE are designed to preserve local neighborhood structure but do not explicitly account for how probability mass is distributed, often leading to distortions of data density. We reformulate dimensionality reduction as the joint alignment of two components: (i) conditional structure, capturing local relationships, and (ii) relative density structure, captured via local density statistics. Based on this perspective, we introduce Density-Regularized SNE (DR-SNE), which augments the stochastic neighbor embedding objective with a density regularization term derived from normalized log-density estimates. Unlike prior approaches such as DensMAP and DenSNE, which rely on local scale consistency, DR-SNE directly aligns normalized density estimates, providing a simple and scale-invariant mechanism for preserving relative density variations. Empirically, DR-SNE improves density preservation while maintaining competitive neighborhood fidelity, and yields gains on density-sensitive tasks such as anomaly detection across multiple datasets. These results suggest that incorporating density information complements geometry-focused objectives in dimensionality reduction.

</details>


### [87] [Weight Clipping for Robust Conformal Inference under Unbounded Covariate Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02072)
*James Wang,Surbhi Goel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对加权共形预测（WCP）在协变量偏移下因密度比无界或估计不准导致的欠覆盖问题，提出CLISF方法（裁剪最小二乘重要性拟合）进行低方差密度比估计，并通过轻微膨胀目标覆盖率来校正欠覆盖，首次为共形推断中的权重裁剪提供理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WCP在处理协变量偏移时，当密度比无界或需要学习估计时会出现严重的欠覆盖，原因在于密度比学习的过拟合以及非一致性评分阈值估计的高方差。现有理论保证的样本复杂度会随真实密度比的高阶矩爆炸增长，亟需解决方法。

Method: 提出CLISF方法用于低方差密度比估计；将CLISF学习的密度比代入WCP；使用轻微膨胀的目标覆盖率运行WCP来校正欠覆盖，并从数据中估计所需膨胀量。

Result: CLISF学习到的密度比在WCP中具有有界的期望欠覆盖；通过膨胀目标覆盖率可校正欠覆盖且膨胀量可从数据估计；首次实现数据集条件覆盖的样本复杂度不随真实密度比高阶矩增长的理论保证。

Conclusion: 通过CLISF和覆盖率膨胀策略，有效解决了WCP在协变量偏移下的欠覆盖问题，提供了更稳健的理论保证，并在真实和合成数据上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) provides powerful, distribution-free prediction sets, but its guarantees rely on the exchangeability of training and test data, which is often violated in practice due to covariate shifts. While weighted conformal prediction (WCP) is designed to handle such shifts, it can suffer from significant undercoverage when the density ratio between the distributions is unbounded and/or must be learned. This is because of both overfitting in learning the density ratio, and high variance in estimating the nonconformity score threshold. To address this, we introduce clipped least-squares importance fitting (CLISF) as a reduced-variance method for density ratio estimation. Specifically, we show that density ratios learned using CLISF, when plugged into WCP, have bounded expected undercoverage. Furthermore, we show that the undercoverage can be corrected by running WCP with a slightly inflated coverage target; crucially, we are able to estimate the required level of inflation from the data. We provide the first theoretical guarantees for weight clipping in conformal inference, achieving dataset-conditional coverage with a sample complexity that does not blow up with the higher moments of the true density ratio -- a key limitation of prior work. We verify our results on real-world benchmarks and synthetic data.

</details>


### [88] [GETA-3DGS: Automatic Joint Structured Pruning and Quantization for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02086)
*Baobing Zhang,Wanxin Sui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GETA-3DGS是首个端到端自动联合结构化剪枝与量化框架，用于3D高斯泼溅压缩，相比原始3DGS实现约5倍存储缩减，无需人工调节阈值，且支持异构混合精度分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS压缩方法将剪枝、量化和熵编码视为分离阶段，依赖人工启发式规则（如透明度阈值、固定位宽、球谐截断），跨场景泛化能力有限，且用户无法指定目标码率或质量预算。

Method: 提出GETA-3DGS框架：(i) 3DGS感知的量化感知依赖图(QADG)，将每个高斯基元视为包含五个属性子节点和度感知球谐子节点的组；(ii) 渲染感知的显著性融合，结合透射率加权贡献、屏幕空间梯度和像素覆盖度生成高斯级重要性分数；(iii) 异构逐属性混合精度方案，在投影部分显著性引导(PPSG)下降保证下与结构稀疏性协同优化。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks and Temples和Deep Blending数据集上，直接操作原始高斯基元，实现约5倍存储缩减，无需逐场景阈值。均匀6位量化在视角依赖场景上导致-6.74 dB性能损失，而异构分配匹配信息论逆向注水分析。

Conclusion: GETA-3DGS与现有编解码器（如HAC++、CompGS）互补，熵编码作为下游可组合使用，首次实现端到端联合剪枝-量化3DGS压缩。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) is a state-of-the-art representation for real-time photorealistic novel-view synthesis, yet a single high-fidelity scene typically occupies hundreds of megabytes to several gigabytes, exceeding the budgets of mobile, immersive, and volumetric video platforms. Existing 3DGS compression methods (e.g., HAC++, FlexGaussian, LP-3DGS) treat pruning, quantization, and entropy coding as separate stages and rely on hand-tuned heuristics (opacity thresholds, fixed bit-widths, SH truncation), limiting cross-scene generalization and preventing users from specifying a target rate or quality budget. We propose GETA-3DGS, to our knowledge the first end-to-end automatic joint structured pruning and quantization framework for 3DGS. Building on GETA for joint pruning-quantization of deep networks, we contribute: (i) a 3DGS-aware quantization-aware dependency graph (QADG) treating each Gaussian primitive as a group with five attribute sub-nodes and degree-aware SH sub-nodes; (ii) a render-aware saliency fusing transmittance-weighted contribution, screen-space gradient, and pixel coverage into a Gaussian-level importance score; and (iii) a heterogeneous per-attribute mixed-precision scheme co-optimized with structural sparsity under a projected partial saliency-guided (PPSG) descent guarantee. On Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks and Temples, and Deep Blending, GETA-3DGS operates directly on raw Gaussian primitives rather than a post-hoc anchor representation, delivering ~5x storage reduction over Vanilla 3DGS with no per-scene thresholds. Bit-width policy is the dominant rate-distortion lever: a uniform 6-bit cap costs up to -6.74 dB on view-dependent scenes versus our heterogeneous allocation, matching an information-theoretic reverse-water-filling analysis we develop. GETA-3DGS is complementary to existing codecs: entropy coding (HAC++, CompGS) is downstream, so the two can be composed.

</details>


### [89] [Bridging the Gap Between Average and Discounted TD Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02103)
*Haoxing Tian,Zaiwei Chen,Ioannis Ch. Paschalidis,Alex Olshevsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对平均奖励设置下的TD学习提出了一种新算法，通过从两条马尔可夫轨迹采样，保证收敛到投影贝尔曼方程的唯一解。该算法在线性函数逼近和表格设置下均适用，收敛界不含显式维度项，并将样本复杂度从四阶降至二阶条件数依赖，与折扣设置下的效率相匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均奖励设置下的TD学习面临贝尔曼算子非压缩性的理论困难，现有方法存在局限性，亟需一种既能保证收敛性又能提升样本效率的统一分析方法。

Method: 提出一种利用两条马尔可夫轨迹采样的新算法，通过投影贝尔曼方程确保收敛到唯一解，分析框架统一适用于线性函数逼近和表格设置。

Result: 收敛分析不含显式维度项，样本复杂度从先前工作的四阶条件数依赖降至二阶，达到与折扣设置相同的效率水平。

Conclusion: 该算法克服了平均奖励设置下TD学习的理论障碍，实现了与折扣设置可比的收敛保证和样本效率，为平均奖励强化学习提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The analysis of Temporal Difference (TD) learning in the average-reward setting faces notable theoretical difficulties because the Bellman operator is not contractive with respect to any norm. This complicates standard analyses of stochastic updates that are effective in discounted settings. Although a considerable body of literature addresses these challenges, existing theoretical approaches come with limitations. We introduce a novel algorithm designed explicitly for policy evaluation in the average-reward setting, utilizing sampling from two Markovian trajectories. Our proposed method overcomes previous limitations by guaranteeing convergence to the unique solution of a properly defined projected Bellman equation. Notably, and in contrast to earlier work, our convergence analysis is uniformly applicable to both linear function approximation and tabular settings and does not involve explicit dimension-dependent terms in its convergence bounds. These results align with what is known to hold in the discounted setting. Furthermore, our algorithm achieves improved dependence on the problem's condition number, reducing the sample complexity from quartic, as in prior literature, to quadratic scaling, and thus matching the efficiency seen in the discounted setting.

</details>


### [90] [Sharpness-Aware Pretraining Mitigates Catastrophic Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02105)
*Ishaan Watts,Catherine Li,Sachin Goyal,Jacob Mitchell Springer,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现，预训练优化器选择影响模型几何结构，通过偏向更平坦最小值（如SAM、大学习率、缩短学习率退火周期）的优化方法，能使基础模型在后续微调和量化中保留更多能力，在20M-150M参数规模上遗忘率降低多达80%，并成功验证于1B参数模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预训练优化仅追求最强基础模型，忽略了模型几何结构对后续参数更新（如后训练、量化）中能力保留程度的影响。论文旨在探索能产生更平坦最小值的预训练优化策略，以提升下游适应性。

Method: 研究了三种偏向平坦最小值的预训练优化方法：Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM)、大学习率、缩短学习率退火周期。在20M至150M参数规模模型上进行实验，并在OLMo-2-1B检查点上验证了短SAM中期训练的有效性。

Result: 这些干预方法在五个常见数据集的后训练中持续提升下游性能，遗忘率降低高达80%。在1B模型上，短SAM中期训练使MetaMath后训练遗忘率降低31%，4-bit量化遗忘率降低40%。

Conclusion: 预训练中采用偏向平坦最小值的优化策略能显著提高模型在后续适应阶段的能力保留，该原则在不同规模模型上均成立，为改进预训练范式提供了新方向。

Abstract: Pretraining optimizers are tuned to produce the strongest possible base model, on the assumption that a stronger starting point yields a stronger model after subsequent changes like post-training and quantization. This overlooks the geometry of the base model which controls how much of the base model's capabilities survive subsequent parameter updates. We study three pretraining optimization approaches that bias optimization toward flatter minima: Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM), large learning rates, and shortened learning rate annealing periods. Across model sizes ranging from 20M to 150M parameters, we find that these interventions consistently improve downstream performance after post-training on five common datasets with up to 80% less forgetting. These principles hold at scale: a short SAM mid-training phase applied to an existing OLMo-2-1B checkpoint reduces forgetting by 31% after MetaMath post-training and by 40% after 4-bit quantization.

</details>


### [91] [Geometric and Spectral Alignment for Deep Neural Network I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02108)
*Ziran Liu,Wei Wang,Jinhao Wang,Pengcheng Wang,Xinyi Sui,Cihan Ruan,Nam Ling,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明深度残差架构中Frobenius归一化层因子的奇异谱具有确定性商-几何估计，建立了功率律谱与Cartan轨道、Gibbs族、Fisher信息线及Bures-Wasserstein曲线之间的等价关系，并给出松弛感知的间隔不等式控制指数漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度残差架构的Jacobian可建模为近恒等矩阵的乘积，但其奇异谱的几何结构尚未被充分刻画。本文旨在建立层因子奇异谱的确定性几何估计，揭示功率律谱背后的轨形结构，并推导控制界面径向振幅与残差变化的刚性定理。

Method: 将满秩因子从GL(d)通过A→A^T A映射到正锥，再映射到有序特征值数据。在Frobenius归一化下，精确功率律谱构成迹归一化Cartan轨道，该轨道同时是秩的Gibbs族、Fisher信息线以及线元为d/4倍Fisher信息的Bures-Wasserstein曲线。核心刚性定理为松弛感知间隔不等式，将界面径向振幅、非回溯松弛和有符号残差变化与拟合Cartan坐标的位移关联起来。

Result: 在精确图零松弛情形下，深度L预算给出阶为(log M)/L的指数漂移；一般情形下松弛和残差增量会增广该界。分离了标量顶径向控制与全Cartan谱控制（后者需要Bures/Hellinger残差变化）。证明了近似功率律和度量图版本、逆下界、Fisher-KL/Bures作用估计以及归一化残差链的近恒等展开。有效秩被定义为谱能量分位数，给出有限宽功率律尾界和稳健的秩窗口转移估计。

Conclusion: 近恒等结果验证了传输预算，图质量保持可度量。经验静态权重指数轮廓可作为诊断工具，但完整验证需要同一算子链的界面预算、松弛和残差信息。

Abstract: Deep residual architectures are modeled as products of near-identity Jacobians. This paper proves deterministic quotient-geometric estimates for singular spectra of Frobenius-normalized layer factors, emphasizing a normalized top-radial Cartan coordinate and fitted power-law chart.
  Full-rank factors are mapped from $\mathrm{GL}(d)$ to the positive cone by $A\mapsto A^\top A$, then to ordered eigenvalue data. Under Frobenius normalization, exact power-law spectra form a trace-normalized Cartan orbit. This orbit is a Gibbs family on ranks, a Fisher information line, and a Bures--Wasserstein curve with line element $d/4$ times Fisher information.
  The main rigidity theorem is a slack-aware margin inequality: interface radial amplitude, non-backtracking slack, and signed residual variation control displacement of the fitted Cartan coordinate. In the exact-chart zero-slack case, a depth-$L$ budget gives exponent drift of order $(\log M)/L$; generally, slack and residual increments augment the bound.
  We separate scalar top-radial from full-Cartan spectral control, which also needs Bures/Hellinger residual variation. We prove approximate-power-law and metric-chart versions, converse lower bounds, Fisher--KL/Bures action estimates, and near-identity expansions for normalized residual chains.
  Near-identity results verify transport budgets; chart quality remains measurable. Effective rank is a spectral-energy quantile, giving finite-width power-law tail bounds and robust rank-window transition estimates. Empirical static-weight exponent profiles serve as diagnostics; full verification also requires interface budgets, slacks, and residuals for the same operator chain.

</details>


### [92] [Statistical Consistency and Generalization of Contrastive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02116)
*Yuanfan Li,Xiyuan Wei,Tianbao Yang,Yiming Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为对比表示学习（CRL）建立了统一的统计学习理论，证明了对比损失与最优排序的统计一致性，推导了随负样本数增加而改善的泛化界（O(1/m + 1/√n)和O(1/√m + 1/√n)），并通过大规模视觉-语言模型实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CRL理论分析存在三个关键局限：(i)统计一致性理解不足；(ii)泛化界随负样本数增加而变差，与经验观察矛盾；(iii)检索性能的理论关注有限。本文旨在填补这些空白。

Method: 提出统一统计学习理论框架：下游任务采用AUC型种群准则评估检索质量，证明对比损失的统计一致性并建立校准型不等式；上游训练分别分析监督与自监督对比目标，推导出负样本数m和锚点数n显式依赖的泛化界。

Result: 监督对比学习泛化界为O(1/m + 1/√n)，自监督对比学习泛化界为O(1/√m + 1/√n)；泛化界随m增大而改善，解释了大规模负样本集的经验优势，并揭示了m与n之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论框架统一了CRL的统计一致性、泛化性能与检索质量分析，解决了现有理论的矛盾，为CRL的实际应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Contrastive representation learning (CRL) underpins many modern foundation models. Despite recent theoretical progress, existing analyses suffer from several key limitations: (i) the statistical consistency of CRL remains poorly understood; (ii) available generalization bounds deteriorate as the number of negative samples increases, contradicting the empirical benefits of large negative sets; and (iii) the retrieval performance of CRL has received limited theoretical attention.
  In this paper, we develop a unified statistical learning theory for CRL. For downstream tasks, we evaluate retrieval quality using an AUC-type population criterion and show that the contrastive loss is \emph{statistically consistent} with optimal ranking. We further establish a \emph{calibration-style inequality} that quantitatively relates excess contrastive risk to excess retrieval suboptimality. For upstream training, we study both supervised and self-supervised contrastive objectives and derive generalization bounds of order $O(1/m + 1/\sqrt{n})$ and $O(1/\sqrt{m} + 1/\sqrt{n})$, respectively, where $m$ denotes the number of negative samples and $n$ the number of anchor points. These bounds not only explain the empirical advantages of large negative sets but also reveal an explicit trade-off between $m$ and $n$. Extensive experiments on large-scale vision--language models corroborate our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [93] [STABLEVAL: Disagreement-Aware and Stable Evaluation of AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02122)
*Akash Bonagiri,Gerard Janno Anderias,Saee Patil,Angelina Lai,Devang Borkar,Gezheng Kang,Ishant Gandhi,Setareh Rafatirad,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STABLEVAL是一个基于分歧感知的评估框架，通过建模潜在项目正确性和标注者特定混淆模式，生成后验期望项目得分和校准的智能体级分数，相比多数投票在标注者异质性和对抗噪声下具有更稳定的系统排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票机制丢弃了标注者可靠性和项目级歧义信息，导致在不同标注者子集上系统排名不稳定；现有标签去噪方法（如Dawid-Skene）针对的是硬标签恢复而非稳定评估。

Method: 提出STABLEVAL框架，建模潜在项目正确性和标注者特定混淆模式，生成后验期望项目得分；将排名稳定性形式化为一级评估目标；在合成实验和真实人工标注基准上对比多数投票。

Result: 在标注者异质性和对抗噪声条件下，多数投票表现出递增的评分误差和排名不稳定性，而STABLEVAL产生更稳定且统计学上更可靠的系统排名。

Conclusion: 建模分歧对于实现鲁棒且可复现的AI评估至关重要，STABLEVAL为不确定性感知的系统评估提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Human evaluation remains the primary standard for assessing modern AI systems, yet annotator disagreement, bias, and variability make system rankings fragile under standard majority vote aggregation. Majority vote discards annotator reliability and item-level ambiguity, often yielding unstable comparisons across annotator subsets. We introduce STABLEVAL, a disagreement-aware evaluation framework that models latent item correctness and annotator-specific confusion patterns to produce posterior expected item credit and calibrated agent-level scores. Unlike label-denoising approaches such as Dawid-Skene, STABLEVAL is explicitly designed for stable and uncertainty-aware system evaluation rather than hard label recovery. We formalize ranking stability as a first-class evaluation objective and analyze how aggregation methods preserve or distort underlying annotator behavior. Across controlled synthetic experiments and multiple real-world human-annotated benchmarks, majority vote exhibits increasing score error and ranking instability under annotator heterogeneity and adversarial noise, while STABLEVAL yields more stable and statistically grounded system rankings. These results demonstrate that modeling disagreement is essential for robust and reproducible AI evaluation.

</details>


### [94] [Boundary Mass and the Soft-to-Hard Limit in Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02124)
*Reza Rastegar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了softmax路由的混合专家模型在温度趋近于零时的奇异性，证明了边界质量（即前两个路由器得分差距很小的概率）与平板宽度呈线性关系，并通过余面积/管状估计给出了定量风险边界和Γ-收敛性，揭示了零温极限由路由接口周围的薄几何层控制。在教师-学生设定下，证明了条件景观转移原理，以及局部对称破缺机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解softmax路由MoE模型在温度趋于零（趋近硬路由）时的极限行为，特别是路由决策边界附近的奇异性如何影响模型的风险和优化景观。

Method: 通过余面积/管状估计分析边界质量，证明其在平滑性和横截性假设下与平板宽度呈线性关系；利用Γ-收敛理论建立软目标到硬路由目标的收敛性；在教师-学生框架下推导条件景观转移原理。

Result: 边界质量与平板宽度线性相关，主导常数由路由接口上的面积分给出；软目标Γ-收敛到硬路由目标；在教师-学生设定下，小温度软路由继承硬路由的教师恢复能力和严格鞍点行为。

Conclusion: 零温极限由路由接口周围的薄几何层控制，而非整个输入空间；该几何核心可用于分析教师-学生设定下的优化景观和对称破缺机制。

Abstract: Softmax-routed mixture-of-experts models approach hard routing as the temperature tends to zero, but this limit is singular near routing ties. This paper studies that singularity at the population level for squared-loss MoE regression. The central object is the \emph{boundary mass}, namely the probability that the top two router scores are separated by only a small margin. Under smoothness and transversality assumptions on the router and input law, we prove coarea/tube estimates showing that this mass is linear in the slab width, with leading constant given by a surface integral over the routing interface in the binary case. These estimates yield quantitative soft-to-hard risk bounds and, under compactness and uniform margin control, $Γ$-convergence of the soft objectives to the hard-routing objective. The main conclusion is that the zero-temperature limit is controlled by a thin geometric layer around routing interfaces, not by the full input space. We then use this geometric core in two more model-dependent directions. In a teacher--student setting, we prove a conditional landscape-transfer principle showing that, when the profiled hard-routing problem has favorable identifiability and curvature and the relevant derivatives transfer at boundary-layer scale, small-temperature soft routing inherits approximate teacher recovery and strict-saddle behavior away from teacher-equivalent partitions. We also give a reduced two-expert Gaussian calculation that illustrates a local symmetry-breaking mechanism aligned with the teacher separator.

</details>


### [95] [Personalized Federated Learning for Gradient Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02143)
*Dongwon Kim,Gyuejeong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出pFLAlign，一种梯度对齐框架，通过在本地训练和聚合过程中维持客户端特定信息，解决个性化联邦学习中梯度高方差和聚合失真的问题，理论推导基于PAC贝叶斯分析，实验表明其达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有pFL方法难以可靠地保留个性化信息：本地训练因数据有限且异构导致梯度高方差，聚合过程进一步扭曲客户端特定的优化方向。

Method: 提出pFLAlign框架，包含两个互补机制：(1) 在本地训练阶段自适应调整梯度方向以降低方差；(2) 在聚合阶段通过将全局模型与每个客户端的个性化方向重新对齐来缓解聚合失真。理论推导基于PAC贝叶斯分析。

Result: 实验和消融研究一致表明，pFLAlign在个性化性能和训练稳定性上均有提升，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: pFLAlign通过梯度对齐有效保留了客户端特定信息，为个性化联邦学习提供了理论保证和实际性能改进。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning (pFL) aims to adapt models to client specific data distributions, yet it often fails to reliably preserve personalized information. Local training is hindered by high variance gradients induced by limited and heterogeneous client data, while aggregation further distorts client specific optimization directions. To address these challenges, we propose pFLAlign, a gradient alignment framework to maintain client specific information during both local training and aggregation. pFLAlign consists of two complementary mechanisms: one adapts local gradient directions to reduce variance during client side optimization, and the other mitigates aggregation induced distortion by realigning the global model with each client's personalized direction. Theoretically, we derive pFLAlign from a PAC Bayesian analysis, which reveals how personalized gradient alignment preserves client specific information. Our experiments and ablation studies show that pFLAlign consistently improves personalization performance and training stability, achieving state of the art results.

</details>


### [96] [Projection-Free Transformers via Gaussian Kernel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02144)
*Debarshi Kundu,Archisman Ghosh,Swaroop Ghosh,Vasant Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯核注意力(GKA)，用RBF核替代标准Transformer中的点积注意力，每个头仅学习一个带宽参数σ_h，从而简化注意力机制并建立与经典核平滑方法的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer中学习的线性投影(Q/K/V)是否是必要的，能否被更简单的基于相似性的扩散算子替代。

Method: 提出Gaussian Kernel Attention (GKA)，使用高斯径向基函数(RBF)核计算token间的亲和度，每个头仅学习一个带宽参数σ_h，保留单一输出投影W_O以兼容标准Transformer接口；通过掩码和重归一化实现因果掩码和滑动窗口约束。

Result: 在深度20的nanochat框架中，GKA模型参数为标准注意力的0.42倍，训练FLOPs为0.49倍，训练稳定，训练-验证差距接近零，在标准基准上表现具有竞争力，但在该计算规模下BPB更高。

Conclusion: GKA提供了一种极简、可解释的注意力机制，具有明确的局部性尺度，为Transformer设计中的精度-效率权衡提供了新的维度。

Abstract: Self-attention in Transformers is typically implemented as $\mathrm{softmax}(QK^\top/\sqrt{d})V$, where $Q=XW_Q$, $K=XW_K$, and $V=XW_V$ are learned linear projections of the input $X$. We ask whether these learned projections are necessary, or whether they can be replaced by a simpler similarity-based diffusion operator. We introduce \textbf{Gaussian Kernel Attention} (GKA), a drop-in replacement for dot-product attention that computes token affinities directly using a Gaussian radial basis function (RBF) kernel applied to per-head token features. Each head learns only a bandwidth parameter $σ_h$, while a single output projection $W_O$ preserves compatibility with the standard Transformer interface. GKA can be interpreted as normalized kernel regression over tokens, linking modern Transformer architectures to classical non-local filtering and kernel smoothing methods. We evaluate GKA in both vision and language modeling settings. For autoregressive language modeling within the \texttt{nanochat} framework, we implement causal masking and sliding-window constraints by masking and renormalizing the Gaussian kernel. At depth 20, a GKA model with $0.42\times$ the parameters and $0.49\times$ the total training FLOPs of a standard attention baseline trains stably, exhibits a near-zero train-validation gap, and demonstrates competitive behavior on standard benchmarks, albeit with higher bits-per-byte (BPB) at this compute scale. Overall, GKA provides a minimal, interpretable attention mechanism with an explicit locality scale, offering a dimension in the accuracy-efficiency trade-off for Transformer design.

</details>


### [97] [Experience Constrained Hierarchical Federated Reinforcement Learning for Large-scale UAV Teams in Hazardous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02165)
*Qinwei Huang,Rui Zuo,Simon Khan,Qinru Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出经验约束分层联邦强化学习（EC-HFRL）框架，发现在无人机团队的危险环境中，增加学习者参与度并不一定能提升联邦强化学习的训练性能，性能主要受经验重用策略和梯度转换经验的主导影响，而小批量大小决定有效经验回放暴露，参与度仅起次要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的联邦学习假设增加学习者参与度会提升训练性能，但在无人机团队危险环境下的联邦强化学习中，由于安全、能量和任务时长等限制，经验生成严重受限，这一假设可能不成立，因此需要重新审视参与度与学习性能之间的关系。

Method: 提出EC-HFRL框架，将集群视为联邦学习智能体，簇内多个学习器作为并行学习资源共享经验池。通过分析梯度转换经验的主导作用，研究小批量大小和簇内参与度对经验重用和学习性能的影响。

Result: 增加参与度并不一定提升学习性能；性能与经验重用策略及梯度转换经验的主导性密切相关；小批量大小决定有效回放暴露，而簇内参与度提高会增加经验重用水平；性能区域与学习信号结构强相关，而非联邦聚合效应。

Conclusion: 在经验约束的联邦强化学习中，学习者参与度的作用是有限且次要的，澄清了参与度与聚合效应在性能提升中的实际角色。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning assumes that greater learner participation improves training performance, by leveraging abundant, independently generated local data. However, in federated reinforcement learning (FRL) for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) teams in hazardous environments where experience generation is severely constrained by safety considerations, energy limitations, and mission duration, this assumption may break. This work introduces Experience-Constrained Hierarchical Federated Reinforcement Learning (EC-HFRL), a framework in which clusters act as federated learning agents, while multiple intra-cluster learners represent parallel learning resources that reuse a shared experience pool. We show that increasing participation does not necessarily improve learning performance. Instead, learning performance is strongly associated with experience reuse strategy and the dominance of key analytically identified gradient transition experiences within a cluster. In particular, minibatch size primarily determines effective replay exposure, while higher intra-cluster participation increases reuse level. Empirical results demonstrate that the performance regimes are strongly associated with the structure of the learning signal, rather than federated aggregation effects, clarifying the limited and secondary role of learner participation in experience-constrained FRL.

</details>


### [98] [Manifold-Aligned Guided Integrated Gradients for Reliable Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02167)
*Soyeon Kim,Seongwoo Lim,Kyowoon Lee,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Manifold-Aligned Guided Integrated Gradients (MA-GIG)，通过在预训练VAE的隐空间中构建归因路径，将路径偏置到生成流形上，减少输入空间中不可信区域的暴露，从而获得更忠实的特征归因解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Integrated Gradients (IG) 在基线与输入之间的积分路径经过梯度噪声区域时会产生不可靠的解释；Guided Integrated Gradients 虽通过自适应更新低梯度幅值特征来降低敏感性，但其输入空间引导仍会产生偏离数据流形的中间输入。

Method: MA-GIG 利用预训练变分自编码器(VAE)的隐空间来构建归因路径：在隐空间中进行引导积分，然后解码得到中间状态，从而将路径偏置到生成流形上，减少暴露于不合理的输入空间区域。

Result: 定性和定量评估表明，MA-GIG 通过在接近输入的路径特征上聚合梯度，产生更忠实的解释，减少了离流形噪声，并在多个数据集和分类器上优于先前的基于路径的归因方法。

Conclusion: MA-GIG 通过在隐空间中构建归因路径有效解决了IG类方法的流形偏离问题，提升了特征归因的可靠性和忠实度。

Abstract: Feature attribution is central to diagnosing and trusting deep neural networks, and Integrated Gradients (IG) is widely used due to its axiomatic properties. However, IG can yield unreliable explanations when the integration path between a baseline and the input passes through regions with noisy gradients. While Guided Integrated Gradients reduces this sensitivity by adaptively updating low-gradient-magnitude features, input-space guidance still produces intermediate inputs that deviate from the data manifold. To address this limitation, we propose \emph{Manifold-Aligned Guided Integrated Gradients} (MA-GIG), which constructs attribution paths in the latent space of a pre-trained variational autoencoder. By decoding intermediate latent states, MA-GIG biases the path toward the learned generative manifold and reduces exposure to implausible input-space regions. Through qualitative and quantitative evaluations, we demonstrate that MA-GIG produces faithful explanations by aggregating gradients on path features proximal to the input. Consequently, our method reduces off-manifold noise and outperforms prior path-based attribution methods across multiple datasets and classifiers. Our code is available at https://github.com/leekwoon/ma-gig/.

</details>


### [99] [Manifold-Constrained Adversarial Training for Long-Tailed Robustness via Geometric Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02183)
*Guanmeng Xian,Ning Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出流形约束对抗训练（MCAT）统一框架，通过惩罚特征空间中偏离类条件流形的行为确保对抗样本语义有效性，并利用ETF启发正则化促进类别间平衡几何分离，理论上建立了几何分离与鲁棒边界的下界联系，实验证明在长尾分布下显著提升整体、均衡及尾部类别的对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对抗训练在平衡数据集上有效，但在长尾类分布下鲁棒性严重退化，尾部类别面临高鲁棒错误率和不稳定的决策边界。

Method: 提出流形约束对抗训练（MCAT），包含两个核心组件：1) 惩罚对抗样本偏离类条件流形的语义有效性约束；2) 基于ETF（等角紧框架）的正则化促进类别间平衡几何分离。

Result: 在标准长尾基准数据集上，MCAT在整体、均衡和尾部类别的对抗鲁棒性上均取得一致的显著改进。

Conclusion: 几何分离与对抗鲁棒边界之间存在理论联系，流形约束的对抗风险上界可控制高密度语义区域的鲁棒风险，MCAT有效解决长尾分布下的对抗鲁棒退化问题。

Abstract: Adversarial training is effective on balanced datasets, but its robustness degrades under longtailed class distributions, where tail classes suffer high robust error and unstable decision boundaries. We propose Manifold-Constrained Adversarial Training (MCAT), a unified framework that enforces the semantic validity of adversarial examples by penalizing deviations from class-conditional manifolds in feature space, while promoting balanced geometric separation across classes via an ETF-inspired regularization. We provide theoretical results that link geometric separation to lower bounds on adversarially robust margins, and show that manifold-constrained adversarial risk upperbounds robust risk on high-density semantic regions. Extensive experiments on standard longtailed benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in overall, balanced, and tail-class adversarial robustness.

</details>


### [100] [DurableUn: Quantization-Induced Recovery Attacks in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02196)
*Abdullah Ahmad Khan,Ferdous Sohel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现INT4量化会系统性恢复机器遗忘删除的内容（量化恢复攻击QRA），提出FA-RA-Q-INT4三难困境，并设计了量化感知遗忘方法DURABLEUN-SAF解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评估假设部署与遗忘时精度一致，但实际中LLM以低比特精度部署。作者发现INT4量化会恢复已被遗忘的内容，即使模型在BF16下通过了合规审计，因此需要系统研究遗忘在量化下的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 首次系统研究NF4+LoRA适配器空间INT4量化下的遗忘鲁棒性，在LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct上评估7种方法。2) 提出量化恢复攻击(QRA)。3) 提出DURABLEUN-SAF，利用直通估计器(STE)通过INT4取整传播梯度，是一种量化感知遗忘目标。4) 进行密集Pareto扫描发现鲁棒性存在尖锐相变。

Result: INT8量化无害；INT4量化可导致遗忘内容恢复最多22倍。DURABLEUN-SAF是唯一在BF16、INT8、INT4上均获得稳定耐久性证书的方法（Q-INT4=0.043±0.002，认证率3/3），而SalUn在其发布超参数下认证率仅1/3。

Conclusion: 呼吁将Q-INT4作为评估标准指标纳入机器遗忘基准，与遗忘准确率(FA)和保留准确率(RA)并列。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove specified training data to satisfy privacy regulations such as GDPR. However, existing evaluations assume identical precision at unlearning and deployment, overlooking that production LLMs are deployed at low-bit precision. We show that INT4 quantization systematically restores forgotten content even when models pass compliance audits at bfloat16 (BF16), we term this the quantization recovery attack (QRA). We conduct the first systematic study of unlearning robustness under adapter-space INT4 quantization in the NF4+LoRA regime, evaluating seven methods on LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct across TOFU, MUSE-News, and WikiBio-WPU. INT8 is benign; INT4 induces recovery of up to 22x, worsening with dataset difficulty. We identify the FA-RA-Q-INT4 trilemma: no method simultaneously achieves strong forgetting, high utility, and quantization robustness. A dense Pareto sweep reveals a sharp phase transition once robustness is achieved, retaining accuracy collapses regardless of further tuning. To address this, we propose DURABLEUN-SAF (Sharpness-Aware Forgetting), a quantization-aware objective using Straight-Through Estimator gradients through INT4 rounding. DURABLEUN-SAF is the only method to achieve a stable empirical (0.047, {BF16, INT8, INT4})- durability certificate: Q-INT4= 0.043 +- 0.002, cert rate= 3/3, versus SalUn's cert rate= 1/3 at its own published hyperparameters. We call for Q-INT4 to be adopted as a standard evaluation metric alongside FA and RA.

</details>


### [101] [Break the Block: Dynamic-size Reasoning Blocks for Diffusion Large Language Models via Monotonic Entropy Descent with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02263)
*Yan Jiang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出b1，一种通过单调熵下降目标学习动态大小推理块的dLLM后训练框架，解决了固定块生成在推理任务中缺乏连贯性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)采用固定大小的半自回归块生成范式，存在两个关键瓶颈：1)不同推理任务对应不同的最优解码块大小，固定大小不适用于所有任务；2)同一任务内部，刚性块划分会破坏逻辑流，降低推理连贯性。

Method: 通过实证观察发现，错误推理在块间呈现波动不稳定的熵趋势，而正确推理则遵循一致的下降趋势。基于此，提出b1框架：以单调熵下降(Monotonic Entropy Descent)为目标，结合强化学习，学习动态大小的推理块，作为即插即用模块集成到现有dLLM后训练算法中。

Result: 在多种推理基准上的大量实验表明，b1相较于现有固定大小块基线方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 动态大小推理块能够有效提升dLLM的推理连贯性和效果，单调熵下降是一个有效的优化目标，且b1具有良好的模块兼容性。

Abstract: Recent diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have demonstrated both effectiveness and efficiency in reasoning via a block-based semi-autoregressive generation paradigm. Despite their progress, the fixed-size block generations remain a critical bottleneck for effective and coherent reasoning. 1. From a global perspective, different reasoning tasks would correspond to different optimal decoding block sizes, which makes a ``one-size-fits-all'' assumption ineffective. 2. Even within a single reasoning task, the rigid block partitioning would break the logical flow and reduce reasoning coherence. Through empirical observations, we reveal that for block-wise entropy, incorrect reasoning exhibits a fluctuating and unsteady trend between blocks, whereas the correctly generated tasks follow a consistent descending trend. Therefore, this paper proposes b1, a novel post-training framework for dLLMs that learns dynamic-size reasoning blocks via a Monotonic Entropy Descent objective with reinforcement learning to enhance reasoning coherence.b1 integrates seamlessly as a plug-and-play module with existing dLLM's post-training algorithms. Extensive experiments across various reasoning benchmarks showcase b1's consistent improvement over existing fixed-size block baselines. Our code has been released at https://github.com/YanJiangJerry/Block-R1.

</details>


### [102] [HELIX: Hybrid Encoding with Learnable Identity and Cross-dimensional Synthesis for Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02278)
*Fengming Zhang,Wenjie Du,Huan Zhang,Ke Yu,Shen Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HELIX通过为每个特征分配可学习的特征身份嵌入（Feature Identity），结合混合时域-特征注意力机制，在时间序列插补任务中超越16个基线模型，并在5个数据集上取得最优性能。机制分析表明，其学习到的特征身份与潜在的物理/语义结构逐层对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力的时间序列插补方法每层都会重新发现特征关系，缺乏持久的锚点来保持一致的表示；而基于图的方法依赖预定义拓扑且假设同质空间关系，无法处理混合空间位置与语义变量的数据集。

Method: 为每个特征分配一个可学习的特征身份嵌入（persistent embedding），捕获其内在语义属性；通过时间协变端到端学习任意特征依赖关系，无需预定义拓扑；集成混合时域-特征注意力机制。

Result: 在5个公开数据集的21个实验设置中，超越了全部16个基线模型，达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: HELIX学习的特征身份和依赖关系能逐层与潜在的物理和语义结构对齐，更有效地将跨特征结构转化为插补精度。

Abstract: Time series imputation benefits from leveraging cross-feature correlations, yet existing attention-based methods re-discover feature relationships at each layer, lacking persistent anchors to maintain consistent representations. To address this, we propose HELIX, which assigns each feature a learnable feature identity, a persistent embedding that captures intrinsic semantic properties throughout the network. Unlike graph-based methods that rely on predefined topology and assume homogeneous spatial relationships, HELIX learns arbitrary feature dependencies end-to-end from temporal co-variation, naturally handling datasets where features mix spatial locations with semantic variables. Integrated with hybrid temporal-feature attention, HELIX achieves the state-of-the-art performance, surpassing all 16 baselines on 5 public datasets across 21 experimental settings in our evaluation. Furthermore, our mechanistic analysis reveals that HELIX aligns learned feature identities and dependencies with latent physical and semantic structure progressively across layers, demonstrating that it more effectively translates cross-feature structure into imputation accuracy.

</details>


### [103] [Graph Federated Unlearning for Privacy Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02297)
*Ruotong Ma,Wentao Yu,Qizhou Wang,Jie Yang,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对图联邦学习中用户退出导致隐私泄露的问题，提出结合机器遗忘的方法，通过正交梯度更新和虚拟客户端机制，在彻底移除用户信息的同时最小化对整体性能的影响，实验优于七种SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图联邦学习中用户退出后，其信息仍可能通过中央服务器传播给恶意客户端，造成隐私泄露。现有GFL研究忽视该问题，而经典的机器遗忘方法会显著降低性能，且在GFL中因局部消息传递和全局模型协作而更严重。

Method: 1) 确保遗忘更新的方向与学习其他数据的梯度正交，以最小化对整体性能的影响；2) 引入由中央服务器维护的虚拟客户端，在保留图拓扑和全局嵌入的同时不恢复已移除实体的信息。

Result: 在代表性用户退出场景下，所提方法在隐私保护可靠性上超越七种SOTA基线方法，并有效保持整体性能。

Conclusion: 提出的正交梯度更新与虚拟客户端机制能够有效解决GFL中用户退出后的隐私泄露问题，兼顾隐私保护与模型性能。

Abstract: Graph federated learning (GFL) facilitates decentralized training on distributed graph data while keeping sensitive user information local, aligning with policies such as GDPR and CCPA that grant users the right to freely join or withdraw from learning systems. However, even decentralized, user information can persist after quitting, potentially propagating to central servers and then redistributing to malicious clients. This privacy leakage during user withdrawal, despite its importance, has received seldom attention in GFL. To fill the gap, we explore the potential of machine unlearning (MU) to thoroughly remove user information. However, classical MU methods are known to degrade overall performance, a problem that is exacerbated in GFL due to local message passing and global model collaboration. To this end, we make two adjustments to mitigate this challenge for GFL. First, we ensure unlearning updates that minimally affect overall performance, steering them in directions orthogonal to the gradients from learning other data. Second, we introduce virtual clients, maintained by the central server, to preserve graph topology and global embeddings without recovering information of removed entities. We conduct comprehensive experiments under a representative user-withdrawal scenario and propose a novel membership inference framework to rigorously evaluate and validate the reliability of our privacy preservation. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, which also surpasses the performance of seven state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [104] [A Meta Reinforcement Learning Approach to Goals-Based Wealth Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02300)
*Sanjiv R. Das,Harshad Khadilkar,Sukrit Mittal,Daniel Ostrov,Deep Srivastav,Hungjen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于元强化学习（MetaRL）的财富管理方法，在数千个基于目标的财富管理（GBWM）问题上预训练，推理时可在百分秒内为新的投资者问题生成近最优的动态投资组合与目标实现策略，平均达到最优期望效用的97.8%，且对资本市场制度变化具有鲁棒性，并能解决动态规划无法处理的大状态空间问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GBWM问题需要为每个新投资者分别进行训练和优化，计算成本高；同时动态规划在大状态空间下计算不可行。本文旨在开发一种无需单独训练即可快速生成近最优策略的通用方法。

Method: 基于零样本元学习和基础模型预训练的思想，开发MetaRL方法，在数千个不同GBWM问题上进行预训练，学习跨任务的通用策略。推理时直接应用预训练模型为新问题生成投资组合与目标实现策略。

Result: MetaRL推理可在百分之几秒内完成；平均期望效用达到最优（动态规划）的97.8%；对资本市场制度变化具有强鲁棒性，即使训练仅使用单一市场制度；可处理动态规划无法计算的大状态空间问题。

Conclusion: MetaRL方法能够高效、鲁棒地解决GBWM问题，显著降低计算成本，并在大状态空间场景下扩展了可解问题的范围。

Abstract: Applying concepts related to zero-shot meta-learning and pre-training of foundation models, we develop a meta reinforcement learning approach (denoted MetaRL) that is pre-trained on thousands of goals-based wealth management (GBWM) problems. Each GBWM problem involves a multiple year scenario over which the investor looks to optimally choose an investment portfolio each year and choose to fulfill all, some, or none of the different financial goals that arise each year. These choices seek to maximize the expected total investor utility obtained from the fulfilled financial goals. By eliminating separate training and optimization for each new investor problem, the MetaRL model in inference mode produces near-optimal dynamic investment portfolio and goal-fulfilling strategies for a new GBWM problem within a few hundredths of a second. This delivers expected utilities that are, on average, 97.8% of the optimal expected utilities (determined via Dynamic Programming). These results are remarkably robust to capital market regime changes, even when training uses only one capital market regime. Further, the MetaRL approach can enable solving problems with larger state spaces where Dynamic Programming becomes computationally infeasible.

</details>


### [105] [When Attention Collapses: Residual Evidence Modeling for Compositional Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02323)
*Niklas Houba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现注意力机制在加性叠加（多成分混合）场景下存在"槽坍塌"问题——多个槽收敛到同一主导成分，而弱成分被忽略。原因是注意力对已解释证据无记忆性，导致梯度被最强成分主导。作者提出"证据耗竭"方法，通过乘法耗竭加注意力偏置，为顺序注意力引入残差状态，有效防止槽坍塌，在合成任务、真实音频混合和引力波源推断中均取得成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制在成分近似可分离的场景（如目标中心视觉）表现良好，但在加性叠加场景下，多个成分共同贡献于每个观测，现有注意力模型存在结构性失效模式——槽坍塌，即多个槽收敛到同一主导成分而弱成分被忽略。作者希望解决这一问题，实现加性叠加下的组合推理。

Method: 提出"证据耗竭"（evidence depletion），核心思想是为顺序注意力引入残差状态追踪已解释证据。具体实现包括：1）乘法耗竭（multiplicative depletion）；2）注意力偏置（attention bias）。通过这两种机制的组合，使后续槽能感知已由前面槽解释过的证据，避免重复分配。

Result: 控制消融实验表明并行注意力、纯顺序处理和基于损失的正则化均无法解决槽坍塌，只有证据耗竭持续成功。在合成基准和真实音频混合（FUSS）中，槽坍塌减少一个数量级。在ESA/NASA LISA任务的引力波源推断中，标准注意力完全失败，而证据耗竭成功防止坍塌并实现多源后验估计。

Conclusion: 在加性叠加条件下，残差证据追踪是防止槽坍塌、实现组合推理的关键操作要素。注意力机制的"无记忆性"是根本局限，而证据耗竭通过引入残差状态有效克服了这一局限。

Abstract: Compositional inference - the decomposition of observations into an unknown number of latent components - is central to perception and scientific data analysis. Attention-based models perform well when components are approximately separable, as in object-centric vision. Under additive superposition, however - where multiple components contribute to every observation - we identify a structural failure mode we term slot collapse: multiple slots converge to the same dominant component while weaker ones remain unrepresented. We trace this to a general limitation: attention is memoryless with respect to explained evidence. All slots repeatedly operate on the same input without accounting for what has already been explained, so gradients are dominated by the strongest component, inducing shared fixed points across slots. As a result, attention fails to enforce non-redundant allocation under additive superposition. We address this by introducing residual evidence modeling, instantiated via evidence depletion - a minimal modification combining multiplicative depletion with an attention bias. Controlled ablations show that parallel attention, sequential processing alone, and loss-based regularization fail to resolve collapse; evidence depletion, which adds residual state to sequential attention, consistently succeeds. Across synthetic benchmarks and real-world audio mixtures (FUSS), evidence depletion reduces slot collapse by up to an order of magnitude, generalizing beyond synthetic settings. On gravitational-wave source inference for the ESA/NASA LISA mission, under identical architectures, data, and losses, standard attention fails while evidence depletion prevents collapse and enables multi-source posterior estimation. These results show that under additive superposition, residual evidence tracking is the operative ingredient for preventing collapse and enabling compositional inference.

</details>


### [106] [A Near-optimal SQ Lower Bound for Smoothed Agnostic Learning of Boolean Halfspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02350)
*Tim Sinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了在{±1}^n上均匀分布下，通过独立以概率σ翻转每个输入坐标的平滑对抗学习半空间问题。证明L¹多项式回归达到复杂度Õ(n^{O(log(1/ε)/σ)})，并给出几乎匹配的SQ下界n^{Ω(log(1+σ/ε²)/σ)}，补充了连续高斯设定下的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索离散设定（布尔超立方体）下平滑对抗学习半空间的计算复杂度，填补连续设定（高斯分布）之外的理论空白。

Method: 采用L¹多项式回归方法分析上界；通过统计查询（SQ）模型建立下界。

Result: 上界复杂度为Õ(n^{O(log(1/ε)/σ)})，SQ下界为n^{Ω(log(1+σ/ε²)/σ)}，两者几乎匹配。

Conclusion: 离散设定下平滑对抗学习半空间的复杂度与连续高斯设定类似，均为n的多项式指数级依赖。

Abstract: We study the complexity of smoothed agnostic learning of halfspaces on $\{\pm 1\}^n$ under the uniform distribution in the model of \citet{KM25} where each input coordinate is independently flipped with probability $σ\in (0, {1}/{2})$. We show that $L^1$ polynomial regression achieves complexity $\tilde{O}(n^{O(\log(1/\varepsilon)/σ)})$, and prove a nearly matching Statistical Query complexity lower bound of $n^{Ω(\log(1+σ/\varepsilon ^2)/σ)}$. This complements the recent work of \citet{DK26}, which established analogous bounds in the continuous setting under Gaussian marginals.

</details>


### [107] [ZNO: Stable Rational Neural Operators in the Z-Domain for Discrete-Time Dynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02356)
*Xianli Zhu,Jia Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZNO是一种在z域参数化的因果神经算子，通过稳定低秩MIMO有理滤波器层，专门针对离散时间系统辨识问题设计。实验表明，ZNO在稳定有理离散时间动力学（特别是近单位圆和长记忆场景）中表现优异，但并非通用替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算子学习方法主要适用于连续时间问题，而大量系统辨识问题本质上是离散时间的，缺乏专门针对离散时间稳定有理动力学的神经算子模型。

Method: ZNO的层结构为稳定低秩MIMO有理滤波器，直接在z平面参数化。通过单位圆极点约束确保稳定性，结合低秩通道混合、平滑稳定极点重参数化、因果递归以及可选的短FIR分支，构成统一的z域有理递归层。

Result: 在受控离散系统辨识实验中，ZNO在目标为近单位圆轻阻尼极点的稳定有理系统时优势最明显。在5个公共非线性系统辨识基准上，ZNO在符合稳定有理离散时间滤波器的任务上取得最低平均误差。

Conclusion: ZNO是稳定有理离散时间动力学（尤其是近单位圆和长记忆场景）的强模型，但不是通用替代方案，经典或状态空间基线在某些系统上仍更优。

Abstract: We introduce the Z-Domain Neural Operator (ZNO), a causal neural operator whose layers are stable low-rank multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) rational filters parameterized directly in the $z$-plane. ZNO addresses a limitation of existing operator learning methods, many of which are primarily tailored for continuous-time problems, while a large class of system-identification problems is intrinsically discrete-time. The $z$-domain form expresses stability as a unit-disk pole constraint and makes learned discrete-time poles directly readable. The model combines low-rank channel mixing, smooth stable pole reparameterization, causal recurrence, and an optional short finite impulse response (FIR) branch in a single $z$-domain rational recurrent layer. Across controlled discrete system-identification experiments, ZNO's advantage is most evident when the target dynamics are stable rational systems with lightly damped poles near the unit circle. Under matched parameter budgets, ZNO is not uniformly dominant; however, with validation-selected configurations, the same architecture can achieve the lowest mean error across the controlled tasks. A five-bin difficulty sweep over near-unit-circle / long-memory dynamics shows that ZNO has the lowest mean error across memory regimes, from short (approximately 10 steps) to long (approximately 100-200 steps). On five public nonlinear system-identification benchmarks, ZNO is competitive with neural operator and state-space baselines, achieving the lowest mean error on benchmarks whose dynamics align with stable rational discrete-time filters, while classical or state-space baselines remain preferable on some systems. These results position ZNO as a strong model for stable rational discrete-time dynamics, especially in near-unit-circle and long-memory regimes, but not as a universal replacement for specialized system-identification methods.

</details>


### [108] [Predicting Post Virality with Temporal Cross-Attention over Trend Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02358)
*Sarvagya Somvanshi,Mohan Xu,Rakhi Chadalavada,Nathan Canera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ViralityNET模型，通过融合Reddit帖子内部特征与Wikipedia页面浏览峰值等外部注意力信号，预测社交媒体帖子的病毒式传播。在二分类任务中，模型相比纯文本基线AUC-PR提升0.015，AUC-ROC达到0.836，证明引入真实世界动态信号能显著提升病毒性预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交媒体病毒性预测模型过度依赖静态文本和结构特征，忽视了趋势信号的高度动态特性。作者研究真实世界的注意力信号能否超越帖子文本本身，提升病毒性预测效果。

Method: 提出ViralityNET架构，融合四个帖子级别流（标题嵌入、正文嵌入、结构元数据、学习的子版块嵌入），通过交叉注意力模块查询基于Wikipedia页面浏览峰值的每日滑动窗口趋势矩阵（前7天top-512峰值词条）。将病毒性预测建模为考虑子版块规模差异的二分类任务，标签定义为超过每个子版块参与度第90百分位数且达到最低绝对分数阈值。

Result: 融合外部注意力信号一致优于纯文本基线，AUC-PR提升0.015，整体AUC-ROC达到0.836。

Conclusion: 引入外部注意力信号能带来可衡量的性能提升，突出了真实世界动态在塑造在线病毒性传播中的重要性。

Abstract: Current models for predicting social media virality rely heavily on static textual and structural features, effectively ignoring the highly dynamic nature of trend signals. We study whether real-world attention signals can improve the prediction of social-media virality beyond what post text alone reveals. We introduce \model{}, an architecture that predicts Reddit post virality by fusing internal platform representations with exogenous temporal signals derived from Wikipedia pageview spikes. We frame virality as a binary classification task that accounts for differences in subreddit scale, labeling posts as viral if they exceed the 90th percentile of per-subreddit engagement and a minimum absolute score threshold.
  We introduce ViralityNET combines four post-level streams: title embeddings, body embeddings, structural metadata, and learned subreddit embeddings with a cross-attention block that queries a daily sliding-window trends matrix encoding the top-512 Wikipedia spike terms from the preceding seven days. Empirical results suggest that incorporating external attention signals yields consistent gains, outperforming text-only baselines by +0.015 AUC-PR and achieving an overall AUC-ROC of 0.836. Overall, we provide evidence that incorporating external attention signals yields measurable improvements over text-only baselines, highlighting the importance of real-world dynamics in shaping online virality.

</details>


### [109] [Binary Rewards and Reinforcement Learning: Fundamental Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02375)
*Marc Dymetman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了RLVR训练导致语言模型多样性坍塌的机制：二元奖励使策略梯度存在无穷多最优解，KL控制通过选择过滤模型$p_*$来解除简并，但该过程存在非对称性；在模型误设下，降低$\beta$会导致输出分布极度集中而非收敛到$p_*$，而直接以$p_*$为目标的替代散度可避免此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示RLVR训练中多样性坍塌的结构性原因，解释为何多样本覆盖率会下降甚至低于基座模型，为改进训练方法提供理论依据。

Method: 从二元奖励的性质出发，理论分析策略梯度方法中分布集合的简并性；通过KL控制分析过滤模型$p_*$作为极限目标的选择机制；推导超参数$\beta$与目标有效率的解析关系；通过玩具自回归实验验证模型误设下的坍塌现象。

Result: 二元奖励下最大化期望奖励的分布有无穷多个；KL控制在$\beta\to 0$时选择过滤模型$p_*$，但存在前向KL收敛而反向KL发散的对称性破缺；模型误设下降低$\beta$导致输出分布急剧集中，而非收敛到$p_*$。

Conclusion: 直接以$p_*$为目标的替代散度（如Kruszewski等人的方法）能够避免多样性坍塌，因为它奖励对$p_*$支撑集的覆盖而非集中在高有效性输出上。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard approach for improving reasoning in language models, yet models trained with RLVR often suffer from diversity collapse: while single-sample accuracy improves, multi-sample coverage degrades, sometimes falling below the base model. We provide a structural account of this phenomenon grounded in the properties of binary rewards. Binary rewards create a fundamental degeneracy for policy gradient methods: the set of distributions maximizing expected reward is infinite, with no distinguished element. KL-control resolves this degeneracy by selecting, in the limit $β\to 0$, the filtered model $p_*:=a(\cdot\mid\mathcal{Y}_1)$ -- the base model conditioned on validity -- which is the unique fully valid distribution closest to the base model in KL divergence. This selection operates through a nontrivial asymmetry: the tilted distribution $p_{[β]}\propto a(y)\,e^{v(y)/β}$ converges to $p_*$ in forward KL as $β\to 0$, yet $p_*$ cannot serve as a direct optimization target because $\mathrm{KL}(q\,\|\,p_*)$ is infinite for any full-support policy $q$. We develop explicit formulas relating the hyperparameter $β$ to the more interpretable target validity rate $μ$. Under model misspecification -- the typical practical regime -- the pressure to decrease $β$ drives the optimizer toward highly concentrated distributions over a small number of valid outputs, collapsing toward ever fewer as $β$ decreases, rather than toward the filtered model. We illustrate this mechanism on a toy autoregressive experiment and discuss how alternative divergences that target $p_*$ directly -- as pursued empirically by \citet{kruszewski_whatever_2026} -- avoid this failure mode by rewarding coverage of $p_*$'s support rather than concentration on high-validity outputs.

</details>


### [110] [Statistically-Lossless Quantization of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02404)
*Michael Helcig,Eldar Kurtic,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了量化大语言模型中“统计无损压缩”的中间地带，提出了三种互补的无损概念：任务无损（保留零样本准确率）、分布无损（保留下一个词元分布不可区分性）及相应的评估指标EAR，并证明了非对称量化对分布无损的必要性。提出的SLQ方法在低于4比特/参数时实现任务无损，5-6比特/参数实现分布无损，并带来1.7-3.6x推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型量化方法存在明确权衡：有损方法（如GPTQ、AWQ）可实现实际压缩但损失精度，无损方法（如蒸馏）保留精度但通常不加速推理。本文旨在探索“统计无损压缩”这一中间地带，寻找既能保持模型行为高保真度又能实现推理加速的量化方案。

Method: 1) 提出三种无损定义：任务无损（零样本准确率在自然采样方差内）、分布无损（量化模型的下一个词元分布与原始模型在统计上不可区分）、以及严格的分布无损形式化。2) 提出可解释的保真度指标——期望接受率（EAR），定义为最优耦合下最大词元一致概率。3) 证明gamma平方方差律：对称量化将噪声方差放大gamma²倍，因此对称量化无法实现分布无损。4) 提出SLQ方法：逐层非均匀量化、非对称量化、宽比特搜索。

Result: 任务无损：低于4比特/参数（低至3.3比特，视模型而定）；分布无损：平均5-6比特/参数；推理加速：相对于FP16达到1.7-3.6x（结合优化核）。

Conclusion: 非对称量化对于分布无损保真度是必要的，但对于任务级别保真度并非必需；任务无损可以在非常激进的比特宽度下实现（<4比特），而分布无损需要更高的比特宽度（5-6比特）。SLQ方法在保真度和加速之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Model quantization has become essential for efficient large language model deployment, yet existing approaches involve clear trade-offs: methods such as GPTQ and AWQ achieve practical compression but are lossy, while lossless techniques preserve fidelity but typically do not accelerate inference. This paper explores the middle ground of statistically-lossless compression through three complementary notions of losslessness for quantized LLMs. First, task-lossless compression preserves zero-shot benchmark accuracy within natural sampling variance and remains achievable at aggressive bitwidths. Second, we formalize the stricter notion of distribution-lossless compression, requiring the quantized model's next-token distribution to be practically indistinguishable from the original, and propose the Expected Acceptance Rate (EAR), the maximum token-agreement probability under optimal coupling, as a directly interpretable fidelity metric (for example, EAR >= 0.99 indicates 99% agreement). Third, we prove a gamma-squared variance law showing that symmetric quantization inflates noise variance by gamma squared relative to asymmetric quantization, making asymmetry necessary for distribution-lossless fidelity but not for task-level preservation. Using SLQ, a layer-wise non-uniform method with asymmetric quantization and wide bitwidth search, we achieve task-lossless compression at well below 4 bits per parameter (as low as 3.3 bits depending on the model), distribution-lossless compression at 5 to 6 bits per parameter on average, and inference speedups of 1.7 to 3.6x relative to FP16 with optimized kernels. Source code is available at https://github.com/IST-DASLab/SLQ.

</details>


### [111] [Closed-Loop CO2 Storage Control With History-Based Reinforcement Learning and Latent Model-Based Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02405)
*Sofianos Panagiotis Fotias,Vassilis Gaganis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于部分可观测深度强化学习的CO2地质封存闭环管理框架，通过历史条件策略和潜在模型自适应管线，在仅使用井口部署信息的情况下恢复近乎特权状态性能，并在异常工况下以更低的模拟器预算实现优于直接无模型重调的控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地质CO2封存的闭环管理需要能够适应不确定储层行为的控制策略，同时依赖实际操作中可获取的观测数据。现有方法在可部署性与性能之间难以兼顾，亟需一种既能利用高保真模拟又能在实际井口信息条件下有效运行的控制框架。

Method: 将CO2注入与盐水生产控制建模为部分可观测序贯决策问题，采用深度强化学习控制器。对比了五种无模型策略（特权状态、仅井口、历史条件、掩码课程、非对称师生），并评估了一种基于潜在模型的适应管线，该管线复用名义潜在动力学并在已知注入器故障、泄漏诱导动力学与奖励偏移、分隔储层连通性等异常场景下重新调整控制器。

Result: 历史条件策略仅使用可部署的井口信息即可恢复近乎特权状态的性能；在异常运行工况下，基于潜在模型的重调在相同场景特定真实模拟器预算下优于直接无模型重调。

Conclusion: 该框架为CO2封存闭环控制提供了一种模拟器预算感知的替代方案，可避免重复的在线历史匹配与重新优化，在实用性和计算效率之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: Closed-loop management of geological CO2 storage requires control policies that adapt to uncertain reservoir behavior while relying on observations that are realistically available during operation. This work formulates CO2 injection and brine-production control as a partially observable sequential decision problem and studies deployable deep reinforcement-learning controllers trained with high-fidelity reservoir simulation. We first compare privileged-state, well-only, history-conditioned, masking-curriculum, and asymmetric teacher-student model-free policies in order to quantify the value of temporal well-response information and training-time privileged simulator states. We then evaluate a latent model-based adaptation pipeline that reuses nominal latent dynamics and retunes controllers under known injector failure, leakage-induced dynamics and reward shift, and compartmentalized reservoir connectivity. The results show that history-conditioned policies recover nearly all of the privileged-state performance while using only deployable well-level information, and that latent model-based retuning outperforms direct model-free retuning under the same scenario-specific real-simulator budget in the abnormal operating cases. The proposed framework therefore provides a simulator-budget-aware alternative to repeated online history matching and re-optimization for closed-loop CO2 storage control.

</details>


### [112] [Inducing Permutation Invariant Priors in Bayesian Optimization for Carbon Capture and Storage Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02409)
*Sofianos Panagiotis Fotias,Vassilis Gaganis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的排列不变高斯过程核（GP-Perm）和基于Deep Sets的深度核学习模型（DKL-DS），用于解决碳捕集与封存（CCS）项目中井位优化的排列对称性问题，在8个用例（7个合成基准+1个真实Johansen地层案例）中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝叶斯优化中的高斯过程核更适合向量输入而非无序集合，无法利用注入井和生产井组内的排列对称性，导致碳捕集与封存项目中井位优化的效率低下。

Method: 提出GP-Perm核，通过集合诱导经验表示之间的稳定散度来编码排列不变性，并可结合标准核处理额外向量输入；同时提出基于Deep Sets架构的深度核学习模型DKL-DS作为可学习的排列不变基线方法。

Result: 在7个合成基准测试和1个真实CCS案例（Johansen地层）上评估了所提方法的性能。

Conclusion: GP-Perm核和DKL-DS模型能够有效利用排列对称性，提升碳捕集与封存项目中井位优化的贝叶斯优化效率。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization is an iterative method, tailored to optimizing expensive black box objective functions. Surrogate models like Gaussian Processes, which are the gold standard in Bayesian Optimization, can be inefficient for inputs with permutation symmetries, as the most common kernels employed are better suited for vector inputs rather than unordered sets of items. Motivated by this issue, we turn to permutation invariant Bayesian Optimization for well placement in Carbon Capture and Storage projects. The high fidelity black box simulator is instructed to operate wells under group control, giving rise to permutation symmetries within injector and producer groups that cannot be exploited with standard GP kernels. In this work, our main contribution is a novel Gaussian Process kernel (GP-Perm) that encodes permutation invariance by comparing sets through a stable divergence between their induced empirical representations, and can be combined with standard kernels for additional vector-valued inputs. As a learned invariant baseline, we also consider a Deep Kernel Learning model (DKL-DS) using the Deep Sets architecture to learn a permutation-invariant embedding. We evaluate the proposed methodology across 8 use cases, comprising seven synthetic benchmarks and one realistic CCS case study (Johansen formation)

</details>


### [113] [Reference-Sampled Boltzmann Projection for KL-Regularized RLVR: Target-Matched Weighted SFT, Finite One-Shot Gaps, and Policy Mirror Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02469)
*Yao Shu,Chenxing Wei,Hongbin Lin,Shuang Qiu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在固定参考策略下，带验证奖励的在线强化学习（RLVR）等价于一种参考采样的加权监督微调（SFT）目标，其诱导策略为Boltzmann目标策略。作者提出BOLT算法作为该投影的经验估计器，并通过单次分析分解了多种误差来源，揭示了温度-覆盖-方差之间的权衡边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线RLVR虽然有效，但需要持续进行rollout生成、验证器评分和参考策略评估，形成瓶颈。静态加权SFT在预计算rollout上似乎能消除这一瓶颈，但仅凭奖励无法确定加权似然的权重，因为采样器和权重共同决定了被拟合的策略。本文旨在建立两者之间的精确等价关系。

Method: 论文首先识别出参考采样的加权SFT目标，其诱导策略等价于固定参考KL正则化RLVR优化器（即Boltzmann目标策略）。通过密度比权重匹配，推导出唯一的最优权重形式为prompt归一化的Boltzmann权重。BOLT算法是该投影的经验估计。在有限单次分析中，将误差分解为存储支持代价、分区估计、有效样本量方差、泛化、优化和近似误差。

Result: 通过单次Qwen实验，证明了目标匹配权重的有效性、单次饱和现象、刷新采样器的增益以及优化时间节省。理论分析揭示了额外SFT轮次无法修复参考策略覆盖不足的问题，并展示了温度-覆盖-方差的前沿权衡。

Conclusion: 本文建立了固定参考KL正则化RLVR与加权SFT之间的精确等价关系，给出了唯一的权重形式，并通过理论分解和实验验证了该方法的有效性及其局限性。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) turns checkable outcomes into a scalable training signal, but it keeps rollout generation, verifier scoring, and reference-policy evaluations on the optimization path. Static weighted supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on precomputed rollouts seems to remove this bottleneck, yet a weighted likelihood is not specified by rewards alone: its sampler and weights induce the policy being fit. This paper identifies the reference-sampled weighted-SFT objective whose induced policy equals the fixed-reference KL-regularized RLVR optimizer. The optimizer is the standard Boltzmann target policy, obtained by exponentially tilting the reference policy by verifier reward. Matching a weighted-SFT induced policy to this target forces density-ratio weights; in the reference-sampled subclass, this reduces uniquely, up to prompt scaling, to the prompt-normalized Boltzmann weight $\exp(r(x,y)/β)/Z(x)$. BOLT, a Boltzmann-Targeted SFT procedure, is the empirical estimator of this projection. The finite one-shot analysis separates the exact stored-support price $β\log(1/π^*(S_N\mid x))$ from partition estimation, effective-sample-size variance, generalization, optimization, and approximation errors. This decomposition explains why extra SFT epochs cannot repair missing reference-policy coverage and exposes the temperature--coverage--variance frontier. When coverage needs adaptive sampling, refreshed Boltzmann projections become KL policy mirror descent; finite inner solves enter as additive drift from the exact mirror step. Single-run Qwen experiments provide projection evidence for the target-matched weight, one-shot saturation, refreshed-sampler gains, and optimization-time savings, within the stated single-run scope.

</details>


### [114] [Pretraining on Sleep Data Improves non-Sleep Biosignal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02500)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Phillip Hempel,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Rahul Thapa,Bryan He,Nicolai Spicher,Andreas Brink-Kjaer,Lars Kai Hansen,Emmanuel Mignot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究睡眠基础模型是否可以通过多模态对比预训练（睡眠脑电图/心电图），迁移到非睡眠领域的脑电图和心电图任务中。实验表明，睡眠预训练在8个下游任务上一致优于从头训练，并在多项任务上达到或超越现有专门模型与基础模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有睡眠基础模型在睡眠领域的多导睡眠图任务上表现优异，但其预训练表示能否有效迁移到非睡眠的相邻生物信号领域（如清醒状态下的脑电图和心电图）尚未被充分验证。
