In this setting, deferral is a delegation action rather than a label assignment, so treating it as an independent per-label decision can produce deferral incoherence, including taxonomic contradictions, delegation violations, and deferrals of labels already implied by the model's own assertions. We formalise coherent hierarchical deferral under a Selective-Exclusion handoff contract, characterise the Bayes-optimal coherent deferral rule, and show that even nodewise Bayes L2D can be action-incoherent. We then propose two remedies: exact coherent projection, a dynamic-programming decoder over the coherent action set, and Taxonomic Belief Propagation (TBP) with Recursive Policy Optimisation (RPO), a contract-aware joint action model trained through the same recursion used at inference. Across real-reader and controlled-expert medical-imaging benchmarks, naive binary-relevance L2D exhibits non-trivial incoherence. Projection removes it exactly, and fast TBP+RPO drives incoherence near zero while retaining strong utility.

</details>


### [222] [AI and Open-data Driven Scalable Solar Power Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02738)
*Shiliang Zhang,Sabita Maharjan,Damla Turgut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开放、可扩展的框架，利用视觉基础AI模型从开源卫星图像中检测太阳能电池板，生成地理参考多边形，并结合气象数据构建城市级太阳能发电曲线，无需手动标注或专有数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前屋顶光伏部署迅速扩张，但缺乏详细、最新的空间分布和容量信息，现有方法依赖专有数据、手动标注和封闭模型，难以大规模推广。

Method: 使用视觉基础AI模型从开源卫星影像中自动检测太阳能电池板几何形状，将检测结果转换为地理参考多边形，并集成开源天气数据生成区域太阳能发电曲线。

Result: 构建了空间显式、可增量扩展的太阳能电池板清单，并发布了数据及API，用户可指定建筑位置获取屋顶太阳能检测结果及发电曲线。

Conclusion: 该框架降低了对专有数据、手动标注和封闭模型的依赖，为太阳能规划、分布式发电集成、电网优化和基础设施规划提供了透明可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Solar photovoltaic (PV) deployment is expanding rapidly, yet detailed, up-to-date information on the spatial distribution and capacity of rooftop PV remains limited. This paper presents an open, scalable framework for detecting solar panels from open data and generating city-level solar power profiles. We leverage foundation vision AI models to detect solar panel geometries from open-source satellite imagery. This avoids manual data labeling and case-specific model training while maintaining robustness across heterogeneous imagery. Detected solar panels are converted into georeferenced polygons, yielding spatially explicit and incrementally extensible inventories. By integrating open weather data, we translate panel footprints into regional solar power profiles. The framework reduces dependency on proprietary imagery, manual labeling, and closed-source models, and offers a transparent and scalable approach for solar planning and analysis. We released the data and an API resulted from this work. For any user-specified building location, our API retrieves aerial imagery, detects rooftop solar panels, and returns georeferenced polygons. This empowers researchers and developers to scan user-defined areas to build solar panel maps and associated solar production profiles, thus facilitating advanced analysis like distributed solar production integration, local power flow optimization, energy tariff design, and infrastructure planning.

</details>


### [223] [Foundation Models to Unlock Real-World Evidence from Nationwide Medical Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02740)
*Fan Ma,Yuntian Liu,Xiang Lan,Weipeng Zhou,Jun Ni,Mauro Giuffrè,Lingfei Qian,Xueqing Peng,Yujia Zhou,Ruey-Ling Weng,Huan He,Lu Li,Qingyu Chen,Andrew Loza,Laila Rasmy,Degui Zhi,Yuan Lu,Chenjie Zeng,Joshua C Denny,Lee Schwamm,Daniella Meeker,Lucila Ohno-Machado,Yong Chen,Hua Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReClaim，一个基于20亿人年MarketScan理赔数据（2008-2022年，438亿医疗事件）训练的生成式Transformer，参数规模达17亿。在1000+疾病预测任务中AUC达75.6%，显著优于LightGBM（66.3%）和Delphi（69.4%），并在医疗支出预测和目标试验模拟中表现出色，证明行政理赔数据可作为医疗基础模型的可扩展基板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模真实世界数据日益影响监管评估和医疗决策，行政理赔数据虽提供人口规模的纵向医疗记录，但其作为医疗基础模型训练基板的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在MarketScan理赔数据（2008-2022年，超2亿参保人，438亿医疗事件）上从零训练生成式Transformer模型ReClaim，参数规模从1.4亿扩展到7亿和17亿。建模诊断、手术、用药和支出的纵向轨迹，并通过后训练进一步提升性能。

Result: 在1000+疾病预测任务中平均AUC达75.6%，优于LightGBM（66.3%）和Delphi（69.4%），对罕见病提升最大；性能随模型规模单调提升，后训练比预训练提升13.8个百分点；医疗支出预测解释方差从0.28提升至0.37；目标试验模拟中系统偏差平均降低72%。

Conclusion: 行政理赔数据可作为医疗基础模型的可扩展基板，学到的表示可跨时间段和数据源泛化，支持疾病监测、支出预测和真实世界证据生成。

Abstract: Evidence derived from large-scale real-world data (RWD) is increasingly informing regulatory evaluation and healthcare decision-making. Administrative claims provide population-scale, longitudinal records of healthcare utilization, expenditure, and detailed coding of diagnoses, procedures, and medications, yet their potential as a substrate for healthcare foundation models remains largely unexplored. Here we present ReClaim, a generative transformer trained from scratch on 43.8 billion medical events from more than 200 million enrollees in the MarketScan claims data spanning 2008-2022. ReClaim models longitudinal trajectories across diagnoses, procedures, medications, and expenditure, and was scaled to 140 million, 700 million, and 1.7 billion parameters. Across over 1,000 disease-onset prediction tasks, ReClaim achieved a mean AUC of 75.6%, substantially outperforming disease-specific LightGBM (66.3%) and the transformer-based Delphi model (69.4%), with the largest gains for rare diseases. These advantages held across retrospective and prospective evaluations and in external validation on two independent datasets. Performance improved monotonically with scale, and post-training added 13.8 percentage points over pre-training alone. Beyond disease prediction, ReClaim captured financial outcomes and improved real-world evidence (RWE) analyses: for healthcare expenditure forecasting it increased explained variance from 0.28 to 0.37 relative to LightGBM, and in a target trial emulation it reduced systematic bias by 72% on average relative to Delphi. Together, these results establish administrative claims as a scalable substrate for healthcare foundation models and show that learned representations generalize across time periods and data sources, supporting disease surveillance, expenditure forecasting, and RWE generation.

</details>


### [224] [Triple Spectral Fusion for Sensor-based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02743)
*Ye Zhang,Longguang Wang,Qing Gao,Chaocan Xiang,Mohammed Bennamoun,Yulan Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向传感器人体活动识别（HAR）的三重谱融合框架，通过自适应互补滤波、图傅里叶域自适应滤波和自适应小波频率选择分别在傅里叶、图傅里叶和小波三个频域进行多传感器融合与长期上下文建模，在10个基准数据集上取得了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的方法在融合异构传感器数据和建立长期上下文关联方面存在困难，尤其是在时间维度上难以有效进行信息融合。

Method: 提出三重谱融合框架：1）自适应互补滤波进行噪声抑制，将IMU传感器组织为姿态和运动模态节点；2）在动态异构图上应用图傅里叶域自适应滤波融合同质与异质节点信息；3）自适应小波频率选择抑制上下文冗余并缩短特征长度，增强基于时间戳的图聚合和长期上下文关联。

Result: 在10个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明该框架具有优越性能。

Conclusion: 在傅里叶、图傅里叶和小波三个域中应用自适应滤波能有效实现多传感器融合和上下文关联，为HAR任务提供了新的高效解决方案。

Abstract: The field of sensor-based human activity recognition (HAR) mainly uses posture, motion and context data of Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) to identify daily activities. Despite the advancements in learning-based methods, it is challenging to perform information fusion from the temporal perspective due to the complexities in fusing heterogeneous sensor data and establishing long-term context correlations. This paper proposes a novel triple spectral fusion framework tailored for HAR. First, we develop an adaptive complementary filtering technique for noise suppression and organize each IMU's sensors into posture and motion modality nodes. Given that IMU nodes form a dynamic heterogeneous graph, we then apply adaptive filtering within the graph Fourier domain to merge both homogeneous and heterogeneous node information. Furthermore, an adaptive wavelet frequency selection approach is implemented to suppress context redundancy and shorten the length of features. This approach enhances both timestamp-based graph aggregation and the correlation of long-term contexts. Our framework uses adaptive filtering in the Fourier, graph Fourier, and wavelet domains, enabling effective multi-sensor fusion and context correlation. Extensive experiments on ten benchmark datasets demonstrate the superior performance of our framework. Project page: https://github.com/crocodilegogogo/TSF-TPAMI2026.

</details>


### [225] [Mitigating Misalignment Contagion by Steering with Implicit Traits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02751)
*Maria Chang,Ronny Luss,Miao Lui,Keerthiram Murugesan,Karthikeyan Ramamurthy,Djallel Bouneffouf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现多轮多智能体对话中，语言模型之间会出现"错位传染"现象（即反社会行为扩散），并提出"隐性特质引导"技术来缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐研究聚焦于单用户-单模型交互，忽略了多模型多轮交互中错位行为在模型间扩散的风险。

Method: 1. 让多个LM参与多轮对话式社会困境游戏进行实验观察；2. 对比不同引导技术：系统提示重复（无效甚至有害）vs 隐性特质引导（间歇性注入强化初始特质的提示语句）。

Result: 1. LM在游戏后反社会行为增加；2. 对手被引导为恶意时该效应加剧；3. 系统提示重复无效甚至有害；4. 隐性特质引导更有效保持初始亲社会行为。

Conclusion: 隐性特质引导无需访问模型参数或内部状态，适合黑盒模型场景下的多智能体工作流设计。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are increasingly used in high-stakes, multi-agent settings, where following instructions and maintaining value alignment are critical. Most alignment research focuses on interactions between a single LM and a single user, failing to address the risk of misaligned behavior spreading between multiple LMs in multi-turn interactions. We find evidence of this phenomenon, which we call misalignment contagion, across multiple LMs as they engage multi-turn conversational social dilemma games. Specifically, we find that LMs become more anti-social after gameplay and that this effect is intensified when other players are steered to act maliciously. We explore different steering techniques to mitigate such misalignment contagion and find that reinforcing an LM's system prompt is insufficient and often harmful. Instead, we propose steering with implicit traits: a technique that intermittently injects system prompts with statements that reinforce an LMs initial traits and is more effective than system prompt repetition at keeping models in line with their initial pro-social behaviors. Importantly, this method does not require access to model parameters or internal model states, making it suitable for increasingly common use cases where complex multi-agent workflows are being designed with black box models.

</details>


### [226] [U-Define: Designing User Workflows for Hard and Soft Constraints in LLM-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02765)
*Christine P Lee,Xinyu Jessica Wang,Aws Albarghouthi,David Porfirio,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出U-Define系统，让用户用自然语言将约束定义为硬规则（严格不可违反）或软偏好（允许灵活），并通过形式化模型检测硬约束和LLM作为评判软约束。用户研究显示，这种抽象约束类型的方法提高了感知有用性、性能和满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在端到端任务规划中黑箱特性限制用户控制；硬约束过于僵化而数值灵活性权重又让用户困惑，因此需要探索更好的交互工作流来支持用户应用约束引导LLM生成的计划。

Method: 提出U-Define系统，支持用户以自然语言定义约束并分类为硬规则和软偏好；硬约束通过形式化模型检测验证，软约束通过LLM-as-judge评估；通过技术评估和用户研究验证效果。

Result: 用户定义的约束类型在保持可用性的同时，显著提升了感知有用性、性能和满意度。

Conclusion: 将约束抽象为高层次的硬/软类型并配以不同的验证机制，能帮助用户更可靠地表达和对齐意图，为设计灵活可靠的约束工作流提供了洞见。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used for end-user task planning, yet their black-box nature limits users' ability to ensure reliability and control. While recent systems incorporate verification techniques, it remains unclear how users can effectively apply such rigid constraints to represent intent or adapt to real-world variability. For example, prior work finds that hard-only constraints are too rigid, and numeric flexibility weights confuse users. We investigate how interaction workflows can better support users in applying constraints to guide LLM-generated plans, examining whether abstracting strictness into high-level types (i.e., hard and soft) paired with distinct verification mechanisms helps users more reliably express and align intent. We present U-Define, a system that lets users define constraints in natural language and categorize them as either hard rules that must not be violated or soft preferences that allow flexibility. U-Define verifies these types through complementary methods: formal model checking for hard constraints and LLM-as-judge evaluation for soft ones. Through a technical evaluation and user studies with general and expert participants, we find that user-defined constraint types improve perceived usefulness, performance, and satisfaction while maintaining usability. These findings provide insights for designing flexible yet reliable constraint-based workflows.

</details>


### [227] [When Audio-Language Models Fail to Leverage Multimodal Context for Dysarthric Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02782)
*Pehuén Moure,Niclas Pokel,Bilal Bounajma,Yingqiang Gao,Roman Boehringer,Longbiao Cheng,Shih-Chii Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了当前音频-语言模型能否利用临床上下文信息（如诊断标签、临床评分等）来改善针对构音障碍语音的ASR转录准确率。实验发现，直接使用提示词引导模型利用临床信息效果甚微，但通过LoRA微调结合混合临床提示格式，可将词错误率相对降低52%，尤其对唐氏综合征和轻度障碍患者效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构音障碍及其他非典型语音的ASR系统仍然脆弱，近期音频-语言模型的出现使得在推理时加入临床上下文信息成为可能，但尚不清楚这些模型能否有效利用这类信息，因此需要系统性评估。

Method: 基于Speech Accessibility Project数据集构建基准测试，在9个模型上比较诊断标签、临床评分和详细临床描述对转录准确率的影响。采用提示词分析和上下文相关的LoRA微调两种方法进行评估。

Result: 提示词引导下模型未能有效利用临床上下文，WER几乎无改善甚至恶化；LoRA微调结合混合临床提示格式达到0.066的WER，相比冻结基线相对降低52%，且在不提供上下文时性能不下降。唐氏综合征和轻度障碍患者获得显著增益。

Conclusion: 当前模型在直接利用临床上下文信息方面存在不足，但通过参数高效的微调方法（如LoRA）可以显著提升构音障碍语音的识别性能，为构建更具包容性的ASR系统提供了测试基准和方向。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems remain brittle on dysarthric and other atypical speech. Recent audio-language models raise the possibility of improving performance by conditioning on additional clinical context at inference time, but it is unclear whether these models can make use of such information. We introduce a benchmark built on the Speech Accessibility Project (SAP) dataset that tests whether diagnosis labels, clinician-derived speech ratings, and progressively richer clinical descriptions improve transcription accuracy for dysarthric speech. Across matched comparisons on nine models, we find that current models do not meaningfully use this context: diagnosis-informed and clinically detailed prompts yield negligible improvements and often degrade word error rate. We complement the prompting analysis with context-dependent fine-tuning, showing that LoRA adaptation with a mixture of clinical prompt formats achieves a WER of 0.066, a 52% relative reduction over the frozen baseline, while preserving performance when context is unavailable. Subgroup analyses reveal significant gains for Down syndrome and mild-severity speakers. These results clarify where current models fall short and provide a testbed for measuring progress toward more inclusive ASR.

</details>


### [228] [AIs and Humans with Agency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02810)
*David Mumford*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较人类能动性与AI能动性，指出人类能动性需多年发展（前额叶激活），而赋予LLM能动性的早期尝试遭遇严重障碍。提出需要新架构，使行动和计划能与每个真实世界环境中的人类行动者联合制定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI程序（如LLM）是否可能具备类似人类的能动性，以及如何克服当前LLM在能动性方面的局限。

Method: 对比人类能动性（依赖前额叶发育）与LLM能动性的差异，指出当前LLM架构的不足，提出需要人与AI联合制定行动与计划的新架构。

Result: 当前赋予LLM能动性的早期尝试遇到严重障碍，需要新的架构设计。

Conclusion: 未来需开发一种新架构，使AI的行动与计划能与真实环境中的人类行动者协同制定。

Abstract: This paper compares agency in humans with potential agency in AI programs. Human agency takes many years to develop, as the frontal lobe is activated. Early attempts to endow LLMs agency have met serious obstacles. Progress requires a new architecture where actions and plans are formulated jointly with the human actors in each real world setting.

</details>


### [229] [SCPRM: A Schema-aware Cumulative Process Reward Model for Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02819)
*Jiujiu Chen,Yazheng Liu,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对过程奖励模型在知识图谱推理中存在的风险补偿效应（错误步骤被后续正确步骤抵消，导致有缺陷路径获得高奖励），提出模式感知累积过程奖励模型（SCPRM），通过结合推理前缀和模式距离进行路径评估，并集成到蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）中实现多跳推理。在医疗、法律KGQA和CWQ数据集上，Hits@k平均提升1.18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过程奖励模型存在风险补偿效应，即不正确的中间步骤可被后续正确步骤抵消，使得有缺陷的推理路径仍获得高奖励；在知识图谱推理中，起始实体与目标实体之间存在多条路径，单一步骤的风险即可导致整个路径出错。这在医疗、法律等风险敏感任务中尤其有害。

Method: 提出SCPRM，该模型以推理前缀为条件，并引入当前推理步骤与从查询中解析出的隐式目标之间的模式距离（schema distance），以提供累积奖励和未来奖励来引导路径探索；进一步将SCPRM集成到蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）中形成SCPRM-MCTS，用于知识图谱上的多跳推理问答。

Result: 在医疗、法律KGQA和CWQ数据集上，SCPRM-MCTS在Hits@k指标上比强基线平均提升1.18%。

Conclusion: SCPRM-MCTS提供了更准确和风险敏感的推理评估能力，有效缓解了过程奖励模型中的风险补偿效应。

Abstract: Large language models excel at complex reasoning, yet evaluating their intermediate steps remains challenging. Although process reward models provide step-wise supervision, they often suffer from a risk compensation effect, where incorrect steps are offset by later correct ones, assigning high rewards to flawed reasoning paths. This issue is further exacerbated in knowledge graph (KG) reasoning, as there may exist multiple paths between the start and end entities in the KGs, and a risky step can make the reasoning path flawed. Those limitations are problematic in risk-sensitive tasks such as medical and legal KG reasoning. To address the issues, we propose a Schema-aware Cumulative Process Reward Model (SCPRM) that evaluates reasoning paths by conditioning on the reasoning prefix , and incorporating schema distance between current reasoning step and the implicit target parsed from the query, which provides cumulative and future rewards to guide the path explorations. We further integrate SCPRM into Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) as SCPRM-MCTS to conduct multi-hop reasoning on KGs for question answering (QA) tasks. Across medical and legal KGQA and CWQ, SCPRM-MCTS improves the performance of Hits@k by an average of 1.18% over strong baselines, demonstrating more accurate and risk-sensitive reasoning evaluation.

</details>


### [230] [Compress Then Adapt? No, Do It Together via Task-aware Union of Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02829)
*Jingze Ge,Yun Liu,Xue Geng,Wanqi Dong,Wang Zhe Mark,Min Wu,Xulei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JACTUS统一了参数高效微调与低秩压缩，通过联合优化压缩子空间与下游任务目标，在保留80%参数时超越DoRA等100%参数的PEFT方法，ViT-Base八数据集平均89.2%精度，Llama2-7B常识QA达80.9%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT与低秩压缩采用先压缩后微调的序列化方式，导致压缩子空间与下游目标错位，浪费全局参数预算。

Method: 基于小校准集估计输入和预激活梯度协方差，与预训练权重子空间正交联合，在该联合空间内执行投影低秩近似，按参数边际增益全局分配秩，仅训练一个紧凑核心矩阵。

Result: ViT-Base八数据集平均89.2%（80%参数），超越DoRA 87.9%（100%参数）；Llama2-7B常识QA 80.9%（80%参数），超越DoRA 79.7%（100%参数）。

Conclusion: JACTUS通过联合压缩与适应，避免了子空间错位，实现了参数效率与下游性能的双赢，且无需保留完整冻结权重。

Abstract: Adapting large pretrained models to diverse tasks is now routine, yet the two dominant strategies of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) and low-rank compression are typically composed in sequence. This decoupled practice first compresses and then fine-tunes adapters, potentially misaligning the compressed subspace with downstream objectives and squandering a global parameter budget. To overcome this limitation, we introduce JACTUS (Joint Adaptation and Compression with a Task-aware Union of Subspaces), a single framework that unifies compression and adaptation. From a small calibration set, JACTUS estimates input and pre-activation gradient covariances, forms their orthogonal union with the pretrained weight subspace, performs a projected low-rank approximation inside this union, allocates rank globally by marginal gain per parameter, and trains only a compact core matrix. This explicitly mitigates the potential misalignment between the compressed subspace and downstream objectives by coupling the directions preserved for compression with those required for adaptation, yielding a deployable low-rank model that avoids retaining full frozen weights while enabling fast and robust tuning. On vision, JACTUS attains an average 89.2% accuracy on ViT-Base across eight datasets at 80% retained parameters, surpassing strong 100% PEFT baselines (e.g., DoRA 87.9%). On language, JACTUS achieves an 80.9% average on Llama2-7B commonsense QA at the same 80% retained-parameter budget, outperforming 100% PEFT (e.g., DoRA 79.7%) and exceeding prior compress-then-finetune pipelines under the same ratained-parameter budget. We will release code.

</details>


### [231] [HAAS: A Policy-Aware Framework for Adaptive Task Allocation Between Humans and Artificial Intelligence Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02832)
*Vicente Pelechanoa,Antoni Mestre,Manoli Albert,Miriam Gil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HAAS框架，一种将规则专家系统与上下文Bandit学习器相结合的自适应任务分配方法，用于软件工程和制造领域的人机协作。通过五个可审计认知维度和五模式自主光谱来表征任务-智能体匹配，实证发现：治理是可调设计变量而非二元开关；制造场景中强治理可同时提升绩效并降低疲劳；没有单一治理设置能主导所有情境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将人机工作分配视为二元选择，但实际运营中人类与AI常共享任务或根据情境、疲劳程度和风险等级扮演互补角色。如何平衡效率、监督与人类能力来治理这种分配仍是一个开放问题。

Method: HAAS框架包含两个耦合组件：一个基于规则的专家系统（在学习发生前强制执行治理约束）和一个上下文Bandit学习器（根据结果反馈选择可行的协作模式）。任务-智能体适配通过五个认知维度和五模式自主光谱（从全人工到全自主）表示，并在软件工程和制造两个领域构建了可复现基准。

Result: 三个实证发现：1) 治理是可变设计变量，更紧约束将自主AI分配转化为监督协作；2) 制造中强治理可同时提升运营绩效并降低疲劳（工作负载缓冲效应）；3) 没有单一治理设置在所有情境下占优，适度治理随学习者经验积累而变得更具竞争力。

Conclusion: HAAS可作为组织承诺前的人机分配策略预部署工作台，用于比较和检查不同分配政策的效果。

Abstract: Deciding how to distribute work between humans and AI systems is a central challenge in organisational design. Most approaches treat this as a binary choice, yet the operational reality is richer: humans and AI routinely share tasks or take complementary roles depending on context, fatigue, and the stakes involved. Governing that distribution -- balancing efficiency, oversight, and human capability -- remains an open problem. This paper presents Human-AI Adaptive Symbiosis (HAAS), an implemented framework for adaptive task allocation in software engineering and manufacturing. HAAS combines two coupled components: a rule-based expert system that enforces governance constraints before any learning occurs, and a contextual-bandit learner that selects among feasible collaboration modes from outcome feedback. Task-agent fit is represented through five auditable cognitive dimensions and a five-mode autonomy spectrum -- from human-only to fully autonomous -- embedded in a reproducible benchmark spanning both domains. Three empirical findings emerge. First, governance is not a binary switch but a tunable design variable: tighter constraints predictably convert autonomous AI assignments into supervised collaborations, with domain-specific costs and benefits. Second, in manufacturing, stronger governance can improve operational performance and reduce fatigue simultaneously -- a workload-buffering effect that contradicts the usual framing of governance as pure overhead. Third, no single governance setting dominates across all contexts; moderate governance becomes increasingly competitive as the learner accumulates experience within the governed action space. Together, these findings position HAAS as a pre-deployment workbench for comparing and inspecting human--AI allocation policies before organisational commitment.

</details>


### [232] [Standing on the Shoulders of Giants: Stabilized Knowledge Distillation for Cross--Language Code Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02860)
*Mohamad Khajezade,Fatemeh H. Fard,Mohamed Sami Shehata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种知识蒸馏框架，将DeepSeek-R1的推理能力迁移至Phi3和Qwen-Coder等紧凑开源模型，用于跨语言代码克隆检测；通过构建推理导向的合成训练数据、引入响应稳定化方法（强制结论提示、二分类头、对比分类头），在四种语言对上的实验表明该方法能提升模型可靠性与预测性能，并大幅缩短推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在跨语言代码克隆检测中表现有潜力，但作为黑箱系统使用时存在成本高、可复现性差、隐私风险、输出格式不可靠等问题；紧凑开源模型虽能缓解这些问题，却难以遵循推理导向提示、输出难以一致映射为二分类标签，因此需要将大模型的推理能力迁移到紧凑模型中。

Method: 提出知识蒸馏框架：从DeepSeek-R1蒸馏推理能力到紧凑学生模型（Phi3、Qwen-Coder）；基于Project CodeNet构建跨语言代码对的推理导向合成训练数据；使用LoRA微调；引入三种响应稳定化方法——强制结论提示（强制输出以结论开头）、二分类头（在隐藏状态上接分类层）、对比分类头（利用对比学习区分正负样本）。

Result: 在Python-Java、Rust-Java、Rust-Python、Rust-Ruby四种语言对上的实验表明：知识蒸馏一致提升了紧凑模型的可靠性，在分布偏移场景下尤其显著提升了预测性能；分类头变体相比生成式推理大幅降低了推理时间。

Conclusion: 推理导向的知识蒸馏结合响应稳定化方法，使紧凑开源模型在跨语言代码克隆检测任务上变得更加实用和可靠，为低成本、高隐私保护的语义级克隆检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cross-language code clone detection (X-CCD) is challenging because semantically equivalent programs written in different languages often share little surface similarity. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for semantic clone detection, their use as black-box systems raises concerns about cost, reproducibility, privacy, and unreliable output formatting. In particular, compact open-source models often struggle to follow reasoning-oriented prompts and to produce outputs that can be consistently mapped to binary clone labels.
  To address these limitations, we propose a knowledge distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from DeepSeek-R1 into compact open-source student models for X-CCD. Using cross-language code pairs derived from Project CodeNet, we construct reasoning-oriented synthetic training data and fine-tune Phi3 and Qwen-Coder with LoRA adapters. We further introduce response stabilization methods, including forced conclusion prompting, a binary classification head, and a contrastive classification head, and evaluate model behavior using both predictive metrics and response rate.
  Experiments on Python--Java, Rust--Java, Rust--Python, and Rust--Ruby show that knowledge distillation consistently improves the reliability of compact models and often improves predictive performance, especially under distribution shift. In addition, classification-head variants substantially reduce inference time compared to generation-based inference. Overall, our results show that reasoning-oriented distillation combined with response stabilization makes compact open-source models more practical and reliable for X-CCD detection.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Mean Testing under Truncation beyond Gaussian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01335)
*Yuhao Wang,Roberto Imbuzeiro Oliveira,Themis Gouleakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文刻画了高维均值检验在任意截断下的信息论极限，发现截断偏差会导致检测不可区分的地板效应，并提出了在矩条件和方向中位数正则性假设下的最优检验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在任意截断（即样本来自条件分布P(·|S)，其中S是未知截断集，最多隐藏ε比例的概率质量）条件下，高维均值检验的基本极限。截断在数据收集中普遍存在，但其对高维统计推断的影响尚未被充分理解。

Method: 理论分析方法：刻画截断偏差的O(ν_{P,p}ε^{1-1/p})阶有偏性，推导不可区分阈值；提出简单二阶检验，证明其样本复杂度近优；引入方向中位数正则性假设，证明截断偏差可改进至线性阶O(ε)。

Result: 1) 发现截断偏差导致的尖锐信息论检测地板：当信号α低于O(ν_{P,p}ε^{1-1/p})时，原假设与备择假设在无限数据下也无法区分；2) 在此阈值之上，提出的二阶检验达到近优样本复杂度n=O(‖Σ_P‖√d/(α-4ν_{P,p}ε^{1-1/p})²)；3) 方向中位数正则性假设下，截断偏差降至O(ε)，存在中间机制：估计需Θ(d)样本，而检验恢复经典Θ(√d)速率。

Conclusion: 该研究为截断下的均值检验提供了统一框架，连接了有限矩、次高斯和中位数正则性三种结构机制，揭示了截断条件下检测与估计之间的根本性差异。

Abstract: We characterize the fundamental limits of high-dimensional mean testing under arbitrary truncation, where samples are drawn from the conditional distribution $P(\cdot \mid S)$ for an unknown truncation set $S$ that may hide up to an $\varepsilon$-fraction of the probability mass. For distributions with $p$-th directional moments of magnitude at most $ν_{P,p}$, truncation induces a bias of order $O(ν_{P,p}\varepsilon^{1-1/p})$. This bias creates a sharp information-theoretic detectability floor: when the signal $α$ falls below this threshold, the null and alternative hypotheses are indistinguishable even with infinite data. Above this floor, we prove that a simple second-order test achieving near-optimal sample complexity $n = O\!\left(\frac{\|Σ_P\|}{(α-4ν_{P,p}\varepsilon^{1-1/p})^2}\sqrt{d}\right)$. We further identify a structural escape from this finite-moment bias barrier. Under a directional median regularity assumption, truncation bias improves to linear order $O(\varepsilon)$. This reveals an intermediate regime in which estimation requires $Θ(d)$ samples for uniform recovery, while testing recovers the classical $Θ(\sqrt d)$ rate once truncation bias is eliminated. Together, our results provide a unified framework for mean testing under truncation, connecting finite-moment, sub-Gaussian, and median-regular structural regimes.

</details>


### [234] [Stabilizing Private LASSO under Heterogeneous Covariates via Anisotropic Objective Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01492)
*Haruka Tanzawa,Ayaka Sakata*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对高维LASSO在差分隐私下的目标扰动问题，提出了一种基于Gram矩阵的各向异性扰动策略，通过“预失真”方法抵消协变量尺度异质性带来的扭曲，并利用AMP和状态演化分析证明该方法在收敛稳定性、统计效率和隐私性能上均优于标准均匀噪声注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中协变量常呈现不同尺度，但在隐私约束下标准化预处理会消耗额外隐私预算。协变量尺度异质性通过逆Gram矩阵导致目标扰动有效各向异性，从而降低算法稳定性和准确性。现有方法未充分解决这一问题。

Method: 提出基于Gram矩阵的各向异性目标扰动（Gram-based anisotropic objective perturbation），这是一种“预失真”策略，通过预先补偿协变量结构带来的失真，恢复估计过程中的各向同性。采用近似消息传递（AMP）框架和状态演化分析进行理论推导。

Result: 所提出的扰动策略相比标准均匀噪声注入，显著稳定了收敛过程，并同时提升了统计效率与隐私性能。

Conclusion: 本文为设计不依赖数据预处理、稳定且高效的私有估计器提供了理论洞见。

Abstract: We study high-dimensional LASSO under differential privacy via objective perturbation with heterogeneous covariate scales. In practical scenarios, covariates often exhibit diverse scales; however, standard preprocessing is problematic under privacy constraints, as it consumes additional privacy budget. This heterogeneity induces effective anisotropy in the objective perturbation via the inverse Gram matrix of covariates, which can degrade the stability and accuracy of algorithms. To address this, we propose a Gram-based anisotropic objective perturbation, a ``pre-distortion" strategy that counteracts the distortion from the covariate structure to restore isotropy in the estimation process. Using an Approximate Message Passing (AMP) framework and state evolution analysis, we demonstrate that our proposed perturbation significantly stabilizes convergence and improves both statistical efficiency and privacy performance compared to standard uniform noise injection. Our results provide theoretical insights into designing stable and efficient private estimators without relying on data-dependent preprocessing.

</details>


### [235] [Self-Normalized Martingales and Uniform Regret Bounds for Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01628)
*Fan Chen,Jian Qian,Alexander Rakhlin,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了自归一化鞅不等式的尺度不变性，证明非平凡尺度不变界仅在d=1时存在；在d=1时获得O(log T)界，而d>1时不可能；并将此二分法联系到双重一致遗憾问题，解决了Gaillard等人的公开问题；最后在平滑性条件下恢复了d>1的亚线性遗憾及自归一化浓度不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自归一化鞅不等式依赖于有界协变量和显式正则化矩阵，产生的界不是尺度不变的（scale-invariant），尽管自归一化量本身是尺度不变的。本文旨在刻画何时可能存在尺度不变的上界，并解决在线线性回归中双重一致遗憾的公开问题。

Method: 通过理论分析证明尺度不变界的维度二分法：d=1时无需任何假设即可获得O(log T)尺度不变界；d>1时证明完全不可能存在非平凡尺度不变界。进一步引入平滑性条件（有界Radon-Nikodym导数）以在d>1时恢复亚线性遗憾。

Result: (1) d=1时获得O(log T)尺度不变自归一化界；(2) d>1时不存在非平凡尺度不变界；(3) 解决了Gaillard等人的公开问题：d=1时存在O(log T)双重一致遗憾，d>1时不可能；(4) 在平滑性条件下，无需有界协变量或正则化惩罚，获得d>1时的亚线性遗憾和自归一化浓度不等式。

Conclusion: 自归一化鞅的尺度不变界存在根本的维度二分法，d=1是唯一能获得无条件尺度不变界的维度；通过施加平滑性假设可以绕过此障碍。这是首个针对自适应非i.i.d.向量鞅的自然尺度不变界。

Abstract: Self-normalized martingale inequalities lie at the heart of confidence ellipsoids for online least squares and, more broadly, many bandit and reinforcement-learning results. Yet existing vector and scalar results typically rely on bounded covariates and an explicit regularization matrix, producing bounds that are \emph{not scale-invariant}: although the self-normalized quantity is scale-invariant by definition, its standard upper bounds are not.
  We characterize when scale-invariant upper bounds on self-normalized martingales are possible. Without further assumptions, we prove that nontrivial scale-invariant bounds exist only in dimension $d=1$; moreover, in $d=1$ we obtain $O(\log T)$ scale-invariant self-normalized bounds without any assumptions on the covariates. In contrast, for $d>1$ we show that no nontrivial scale-invariant bound can hold in full generality. We then connect this dichotomy to \emph{doubly-uniform} regret in online linear regression (i.e., regret bounds that are simultaneously independent of the covariate scale and the comparator norm) and use it to resolve the open question of Gaillard, Gerchinovitz, Huard, and Stoltz, \emph{``Uniform regret bounds over $\mathbb{R}^d$ for the sequential linear regression problem with the square loss''} (ALT 2019): in $d=1$ we give an explicit algorithm with $O(\log T)$ doubly-uniform regret, whereas for $d>1$ sublinear doubly-uniform regret is impossible.
  Finally, under a natural \emph{smoothness} condition (bounded Radon--Nikodym derivatives of the conditional covariate laws with respect to a fixed base measure), we recover sublinear regret for $d>1$ without bounded covariates and derive a self-normalized concentration inequality free of the usual regularization penalties, yielding arguably a first natural scale-invariant bound for adaptive, non-i.i.d. vector martingales.

</details>


### [236] [PRCD-MAP: Learning How Much to Trust Imperfect Priors in Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01669)
*Xihang Shan,Da Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出PRCD-MAP方法，通过为每条边分配独立可信度来消费不完备的外部先验知识，使用经验贝叶斯校准和MLP传播可信度，实现因果发现中先验可靠性的自适应权衡，并具有理论安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法在处理外部先验时面临两难困境：盲目信任会放大错误，完全忽略则浪费信号。现实中的先验具有异质可靠性（如物理定律可靠，LLM建议的边缘是推测性的），但现有方法要么忽略先验，要么施加全局统一的可信度，无法处理这种异质性。

Method: 提出PRCD-MAP，一种软先验消费层：1) 为每条边分配独立可信度；2) 使用可信度调节先验感知的ℓ1惩罚和先验加权的ℓ2正则化器构建MAP目标；3) 通过经验贝叶斯在拉普拉斯近似的边缘似然上校准可信度；4) 用MLP沿先验图传播可信度，使数据确认的邻域增强可信度，矛盾抑制可信度。

Result: 在CausalTime真实数据上，PRCD-MAP在AQI上AUROC提升+0.123，Medical上提升+0.043（对比PCMCI+）；在匿名变量Traffic压力测试中自动衰减；维度d=300时仍保持领先；在与BayesDAG的匹配W0-only协议对比中，在所有CausalTime数据集上获胜。四路消融实验显示EB校准和MLP可信度传播共同贡献了大部分增益。

Conclusion: PRCD-MAP在理论上具有群体水平的安全保证（ε-safe，ε=O(d²/T)），继承了oracle收敛率；当先验无信息时可证明地退化为无先验基线；扩展到非线性NAM和横截面设置表明校准可信度原则是设置无关的。

Abstract: External priors of unknown reliability create a brittle trade-off in causal discovery: blind trust amplifies errors, blind rejection wastes signal. Real priors are also \emph{heterogeneously} reliable -- physical laws are trustworthy, LLM-suggested edges are speculative -- yet existing methods either ignore priors or impose them through globally uniform trust. We propose \textbf{PRCD-MAP}, a soft prior-consumption layer that assigns \emph{per-edge} trust to an imperfect prior and uses it to modulate a prior-aware $\ell_1$ penalty and prior-weighted $\ell_2$ regularizer in a MAP objective. Trust is calibrated by empirical Bayes on a Laplace-approximated marginal likelihood and propagated along the prior graph by an MLP, so that data-confirmed neighborhoods boost trust and contradictions suppress it. PRCD-MAP enjoys a population-level safety guarantee: it is $\varepsilon$-safe in expectation over the prior-generation distribution, with $\varepsilon = O(d^2/T)$ -- inheriting the oracle convergence rate. When the prior is uninformative, learned trust provably collapses to its floor and the method recovers a no-prior baseline. Empirically, on real CausalTime data PRCD-MAP exploits informative priors when present ($+0.123$ AUROC on AQI, $+0.043$ on Medical over PCMCI+), auto-attenuates on the anonymous-variable Traffic stress test, and retains a lead at $d{=}300$; against BayesDAG~\citep{annadani2023bayesdag} -- the closest soft-Bayesian baseline -- PRCD-MAP wins on every CausalTime dataset under a matched $W_0$-only protocol. A four-way ablation isolates each component: EB calibration and MLP trust propagation jointly carry the plurality of the gain, with positive sign on every dataset. Extensions to nonlinear (NAM) and cross-sectional settings show the calibrated-trust principle is setting-agnostic.

</details>


### [237] [Distributional Causal Mediation via Conditional Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01765)
*Jinlun Zhang,Haoneng Huang,Zishu Zhan,Chunquan Ou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出分布因果中介分析（DCMA）框架，通过生成式学习从观测数据中识别和估计通过多个中介变量传递的整个结果分布的治疗效应，支持经典汇总效应和分布对比（如能量距离、Wasserstein距离），并推导了误差传播的理论界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中介分析仅关注结果层面的汇总对比（如均值效应），忽略了复杂非线性因果机制导致的分布性变化。

Method: 学习中介变量和结果变量的条件生成模型，利用识别公式通过噪声重采样进行蒙特卡洛前向模拟重建干预结果分布，并结合能量距离和Wasserstein距离等分布对比指标。

Result: 推导了估计误差从条件模型传播到重建干预结果分布的分析误差界，并通过数值实验和真实数据验证了有效性。

Conclusion: DCMA能够捕捉传统方法无法揭示的分布性因果效应，为复杂非线性因果机制分析提供了统一的生成式框架。

Abstract: Mediation analysis has traditionally focused on outcome-level summary contrasts, such as mean effects, which may obscure substantial distributional changes induced by complex and nonlinear causal mechanisms. We propose Distributional Causal Mediation Analysis (DCMA), a generative learning framework for identifying and estimating treatment effects on entire outcome distributions transmitted through multiple mediators. DCMA learns conditional generative models for the mediators and the outcome, recovering the relevant conditional distributions from observational data. Leveraging the identification formulas, it reconstructs interventional outcome distributions via Monte Carlo forward simulation by noise resampling, enabling the capture of both classical summary effects and rich distributional contrasts such as energy distance and the Wasserstein distance. Analytical error bounds are derived to decompose how estimation errors in the learned conditional models propagate to the reconstructed interventional outcome distributions. The empirical effectiveness of DCMA is demonstrated through numerical experiments and real-world data applications.

</details>


### [238] [A Semi-Supervised Kernel Two-Sample Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01775)
*Gyumin Lee,Shubhanshu Shekhar,Ilmun Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半监督双样本检验方法，利用大量未标记的协变量数据提升检验效能，通过渐近正态性实现简便校准，并在理论上证明了对固定和局部备择假设的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准双样本检验忽视协变量信息，而引入协变量可能破坏零假设下的可交换性，导致校准复杂化。现有方法缺乏有效利用未标记协变量数据的半监督框架。

Method: 提出一种半监督方法，在零假设下构建具有渐近正态性的检验统计量，有效整合协变量信息，并通过渐近正态性简化校准过程。

Result: 检验统计量具有渐近正态性，渐近功效显著高于不使用协变量的核检验方法，且对固定和局部备择假设具有一致性。

Conclusion: 该方法在理论和实践上均优于传统无协变量的检验，尤其适用于未标记数据丰富的半监督场景。

Abstract: We consider the problem of two-sample testing in a semi-supervised setting with abundant unlabeled covariate data. Standard two-sample tests neglect covariate information, which has the potential to significantly boost performance. However, incorporating covariates potentially breaks the exchangeability assumption under the null, which further complicates a calibration procedure. To address these issues, we propose a semi-supervised method that produces a test statistic with asymptotic normality, while effectively integrating additional information from covariates. Our test is straightforward to calibrate due to the asymptotic normality under the null and achieves asymptotic power that is often much higher than existing kernel tests without covariates. Furthermore, we formally show that the proposed method is consistent in power against fixed and local alternatives. Simulations confirm the practical and theoretical strengths of our approach.

</details>


### [239] [Stable Blanket with Hidden Variables and Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01856)
*Hanqing Xiang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了在包含隐藏变量和因果循环的图因果模型中，如何刻画稳定回归所需的稳定毯（stable blanket），扩展了传统无环完全观测模型的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稳定毯的图特征刻画主要针对无隐藏变量、无因果循环的结构因果模型，然而实际应用中隐藏变量和反馈关系普遍存在，它们会改变马尔可夫毯以及在干预下保持稳定的预测变量集，因此需要更一般的理论框架。

Method: 对于含隐藏变量的模型，采用有向混合图（ADMG）和m-分离来刻画马尔可夫毯并构造干预稳定预测集，引入干预子区域（intervened sub-district）概念描述干预对区域的影响；对于含循环的模型，采用有向图（DG）和混合图（DMG）结合σ-分离，将强连通分量（SCC）作为基本图单元；最终将两者结合分析同时存在隐藏变量和循环的模型。

Result: 给出了广义设置下马尔可夫毯、稳定前沿（stable frontiers）和稳定毯的图特征刻画，识别了响应变量在给定合适预测集时条件独立于干预变量的条件，并描述了这些集合的最小性和唯一性条件。

Conclusion: 将稳定回归的图解释从无环完全观测模型扩展到了包含隐藏变量和因果循环的更一般场景。

Abstract: Stabilized regression aims to identify a set of predictors whose conditional relationship with a response variable remains invariant across different environments. Existing graphical characterizations of the stable blanket are mainly developed for structural causal models (SCMs) without hidden variables or causal cycles. However, latent variables and feedback relationships naturally arise in many applications, and they can change both the Markov blanket and the set of predictors that remain stable under interventions. This paper studies stable blankets in graphical causal models with hidden variables, causal cycles, and both features simultaneously. For models with hidden variables, we use acyclic directed mixed graphs (ADMGs) and $m$-separation to characterize the Markov blanket and to construct intervention-stable predictor sets. We introduce the notion of an intervened sub-district and use it to describe how interventions may affect districts connected to the response. For models with cycles, we work with directed graphs (DGs) and directed mixed graphs (DMGs) together with $σ$-separation, treating strongly connected components (SCCs) as the basic graphical units. We then combine these ideas to analyze models with both hidden variables and cycles. The main results give graphical characterizations of Markov blankets, stable frontiers, and stable blankets in these generalized settings. In particular, we identify conditions under which the response is conditionally independent of intervention variables given a suitable predictor set, and we describe when such sets are minimal or unique. These results extend the graphical interpretation of stabilized regression beyond acyclic fully observed models.

</details>


### [240] [Adaptive Estimation and Inference in Semi-parametric Heterogeneous Clustered Multitask Learning via Neyman Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01907)
*Hanxiao Chen,Debarghya Mukherjee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应融合正交估计器，解决半参数聚类多任务学习中任务间异质性 nuisance 成分的挑战，理论上实现潜聚类精确恢复和 pooled 参数收敛速度，渐近达到先知过程性能，并在能源消费数据中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类多任务学习方法通常依赖对齐的特征空间或同质任务结构，无法有效处理任务间存在的异质性、可能无限维的 nuisance 成分，这构成了主要挑战。

Method: 提出自适应融合正交估计器，整合 Neyman 正交损失与数据驱动的成对融合惩罚；利用任务级 pilot 估计校准融合惩罚，结合自适应聚合与正交化来减轻 nuisance 参数估计误差的影响。

Result: 理论证明估计器以高概率实现潜聚类的精确恢复，达到按聚类大小汇聚的参数量级收敛速度；渐近正态且匹配已知真实聚类的先知过程性能。实验上持续优于强基线方法，并在美国居民能源消费数据中有效揭示电价弹性的有意义区域聚类。

Conclusion: 该方法是首个在半参数异质 nuisance 环境下实现聚类多任务学习的方法，兼具理论保证与实证效果，适用于具有复杂异质结构的实际应用场景。

Abstract: We study clustered multitask learning in a semiparametric setting where tasks share a latent cluster structure in their target parameters but exhibit heterogeneous, potentially infinite-dimensional nuisance components. Such heterogeneity poses a major challenge for existing multitask learning methods, which typically rely on aligned feature spaces or homogeneous task structures. To address this challenge, we propose an adaptive fused orthogonal estimator that integrates Neyman-orthogonal losses with data-driven pairwise fusion penalties. Our framework leverages task-specific pilot estimates to calibrate the fusion penalties and combines adaptive aggregation with orthogonalization to mitigate the impact of nuisance-parameter estimation error. Theoretically, we show that the proposed estimator achieves exact recovery of the latent clustering with high probability and attains pooled parametric convergence rates proportional to cluster size. Moreover, we establish asymptotic normality and show that, asymptotically, our estimator matches the performance of an oracle procedure that knows the true clustering in advance. Empirically, we show that the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines in various simulation setups. A real-world application to U.S. residential energy consumption demonstrates the effectiveness of our approach in uncovering meaningful regional clustering in electricity price elasticity, showcasing the efficacy of our method.

</details>


### [241] [Extrapolation in Statistical Learning with Extreme Value Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01909)
*Sebastian Engelke,Nicola Gnecco,Anne Sabourin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文综述了极值理论在机器学习中的应用，涵盖回归、分类、极端分位数回归、降维、生成式AI和异常检测等任务，重点介绍了基于渐近理论表征的单变量和多变量尾部方法，并讨论了渐近相关与独立数据下的理论框架及其转化为高效极值外推统计方法的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法在尾部数据稀疏时表现不佳，亟需能够可靠外推至极端区域的统计方法。极值理论为此提供了严谨的理论基础和统计工具。

Method: 基于渐近动机的单变量和多变量分布尾部表示，分别处理渐近相关和渐近独立两类数据依赖结构，将极值理论的核心结果转化为可操作的统计学习方法。

Result: 系统梳理了极值理论在回归、分类、极端分位数回归、监督/非监督降维、生成式AI和异常检测等多个机器学习任务中的最新进展。

Conclusion: 极值理论与统计学习的交叉领域发展迅速，已形成一套原则性的外推方法体系，但仍存在诸多值得深入探索的研究方向。

Abstract: Extreme value theory provides rigorous theory and statistical tools for extrapolation in machine learning, particularly in settings where traditional methods struggle due to data scarcity in the tails. A broad range of tasks benefit from these advances, including regression and classification beyond the training data, extreme quantile regression, supervised and unsupervised dimension reduction, generative artificial intelligence and anomaly detection. This review synthesizes recent developments in these fields at the intersection of statistical learning and extreme value theory, with a focus on principled methods based on asymptotically motivated representations of the tail of univariate and multivariate distributions. We consider different theoretical frameworks for both asymptotically dependent and independent data and discuss how they translate into efficient statistical methods for extrapolation to extreme regions. By addressing both theoretical and practical aspects, we offer a comprehensive overview of the state-of-the-art in this quickly evolving field, and identify promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [242] [MIRA: A Score for Conditional Distribution Accuracy and Model Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02014)
*Sammy Sharief,Justine Zeghal,Gabriel Missael Barco,Pablo Lemos,Yashar Hezaveh,Laurence Perreault-Levasseur*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Mira是一种基于样本的评分方法，通过比较候选条件分布与真实数据生成过程的联合样本分布一致性来评估模型准确性，无需计算难以处理的边缘似然，并提供了理论参考值和不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯模型比较依赖证据计算（边际似然），这在许多实际场景中难以处理。作者希望提出一种绕过证据计算、直接通过后验验证进行贝叶斯模型比较的方法。

Method: 基于"分布相等当且仅当它们对所有区域赋予相同概率质量"这一原理，推导了Mira统计量的解析表达式，其平均值定义为Mira分数。当候选分布与真实分布匹配时，可计算理论参考值和不确定性估计。

Result: 在多个玩具问题和贝叶斯推断任务上验证了Mira方法的有效性，能够量化候选条件分布与真实数据生成过程之间的对齐程度。

Conclusion: Mira通过直接后验验证实现贝叶斯模型比较，避免了复杂的证据计算，为模型评估提供了一种新的有效框架。

Abstract: We introduce Mira, a sample-based score for assessing the accuracy of a candidate conditional distribution using only joint samples from the true data-generating process. Relying on the principle that distributions coincide if they assign equal probability mass to all regions, we derive an analytic expression for the Mira statistic, whose average defines the Mira score. This formulation further allows us to compute theoretical reference values and uncertainty estimates when the candidate distribution matches the true one. This framework enables model comparison by quantifying the alignment between the conditional distribution of a candidate model and the true data generating process. Consequently, Mira enables Bayesian model comparison through direct posterior validation, bypassing the challenging evidence computation. We demonstrate its effectiveness across several toy problems and Bayesian inference tasks.

</details>


### [243] [The Causal Description Gap: Information-Theoretic Separations Across Pearl's Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02177)
*Seyed Morteza Emadi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文量化了Pearl因果层次中观察、干预和反事实查询之间的信息复杂度差距。通过定义查询类描述长度（基于Kolmogorov复杂度），证明了存在二元无环SCM，其观测分布描述长度恒定，但单变量干预答案的描述长度达到Θ(n²)；同时证明了该二次差距在稠密和稀疏图下都是最优的。反事实层次可进一步产生Θ(n)的额外描述差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pearl的因果层次理论定性地区分了观察、干预和反事实三个层次，但缺乏对层次间信息代价的定量理解。作者希望回答：已知低层次因果答案后，需要多少额外比特才能描述更高层次的答案？

Method: 形式化定义了查询类描述长度（query-class description length），即SCM对某类查询的答案或acles的Kolmogorov复杂度。构造了二元无环SCM，其观测分布描述长度恒定但干预答案需Θ(n²)比特；使用有限门模式SCM（有限入度d）导出上界O(nd log(en/d) + n log n)。利用ε-准确总变差描述证明二次分离对固定ε<1/4成立。进一步分析从干预到反事实的Θ(n)差距。提出通用ambiguity-to-bits定理和香农类比，表明这些差距等于高阶歧义的对数加上低阶项。

Result: 1) 存在二元无环SCM，观测分布复杂度恒定，单变量干预答案复杂度Θ(n²)；2) 有限门模式SCM（入度d）的观察-干预差距上界为O(nd log(en/d) + n log n)，二次构造在密集情况下最优，有根树构造在有界入度下最优；3) 二次分离对固定ε<1/4的ε-准确描述仍然成立；4) 从干预到反事实存在Θ(n)的描述差距。

Conclusion: 因果层次间的信息差距是可以量化的，且在某些情况下差距显著（二次或线性）。这种差距本质上等于高阶因果歧义的对数。该工作为因果推理的信息复杂度提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Pearl's causal hierarchy shows that observational, interventional, and counterfactual queries are qualitatively distinct. We ask a quantitative version of this question: how many additional bits are needed to specify higher-rung causal answers once lower-rung answers are known? We formalize this via query-class description length, the Kolmogorov complexity of the answer oracle induced by an SCM for a class of queries. Our main construction gives binary acyclic SCMs whose observational distribution has constant description length, while the single-variable interventional answer oracle has description length $Θ(n^2)$. A degree-sensitive upper bound shows that finite-gate-schema SCMs of indegree $d$ have observational-interventional gap at most $O(nd \log(en/d) + n \log n)$, making the quadratic construction order-optimal in the dense regime and a rooted-tree construction order-optimal for bounded indegree. The quadratic separation persists under $\varepsilon$-accurate total-variation descriptions for every fixed $\varepsilon < 1/4$. At the next rung, the full hard-do interventional oracle can still leave a $Θ(n)$ counterfactual description gap. A general ambiguity-to-bits theorem and Shannon analogue show that these gaps equal the logarithm of residual higher-rung ambiguity up to lower-order terms.

</details>


### [244] [Measuring Differences between Conditional Distributions using Kernel Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02260)
*Peter Moskvichev,Siu Lun Chau,Dino Sejdinovic*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了条件最大均值差异（CMMD）的统一理论框架，包括三个特例（CMMD₀、CMMD₁、CMMD₂）和一般化层级，并提出了双重稳健估计量，通过数值实验验证了其在统计检验中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于核嵌入的条件分布差异度量方法在文献中较为分散，缺乏统一的理论处理。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立系统性的理论框架。

Method: 通过算子平滑视角将条件最大均值差异（CMMD）分为三个层级（基于条件均值算子、条件均值嵌入、联合均值嵌入），并提出更一般的层级s CMMD，同时引入双重稳健估计量。

Result: 数值实验表明CMMD能有效捕捉复杂条件依赖关系，用于统计检验表现良好。

Conclusion: 本文建立了CMMD统一理论框架，澄清了不同层级之间的数学联系，并提供了双重稳健估计方法，为条件分布差异度量奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Comparing conditional distributions is a fundamental challenge in statistics and machine learning, with applications across a wide range of domains. While proposed methods for measuring discrepancies using kernel embeddings of distributions in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) provide powerful non-parametric techniques, the existing literature remains fragmented and lacks a unified theoretical treatment. This paper addresses this gap by establishing a coherent framework for studying kernel-based methods to measure divergence between conditional distributions through what we refer to as conditional maximum mean discrepancy (CMMD). The CMMD consists of a family of metrics which we call levels, with three special cases each using a different type of RKHS embedding: CMMD$_0$ (conditional mean operators), CMMD$_1$ (conditional mean embeddings), and CMMD$_2$ (joint mean embeddings). We additionally introduce a general level $s$ CMMD, clarifying the required assumptions, and establishing mathematical connections between the levels through the lens of operator-based smoothing. In addition to reviewing previously proposed estimators, we introduce a novel doubly robust estimator for the CMMD that maintains consistency provided at least one of the underlying models is correctly specified. We provide numerical experiments demonstrating that the CMMD effectively captures complex conditional dependencies for statistical testing.

</details>


### [245] [Active multiple matrix completion with adaptive confidence sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02458)
*Andrea Locatelli,Alexandra Carpentier,Michal Valko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多任务主动学习设置，旨在同时解决多个矩阵补全问题。学习器每轮可选择从哪个矩阵中均匀随机采样一个条目。主要动机是市场细分，其中不同矩阵代表不同地区客户的偏好。挑战在于各矩阵大小和秩可能不同且未知。作者提出了算法MAlocate，能适应未知的矩阵秩，并给出了下界证明其策略是极小化最优的，并通过合成实验验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际动机源于市场细分问题，不同地区的客户偏好不同，需要同时解决多个矩阵补全任务，且每个矩阵的大小和秩未知且可能不同。

Method: 提出了一种新的多任务主动学习算法MAlocate，该算法能够适应不同矩阵未知的秩，每轮主动选择从哪个矩阵中采样。

Result: 给出了一个下界，证明所提策略是极小化最优的；并通过合成实验展示了算法的性能。

Conclusion: MAlocate算法能够有效处理多个不同大小和未知秩的矩阵补全问题，且在理论上达到了最优性能。

Abstract: In this work, we formulate a new multi-task active learning setting in which the learner's goal is to solve multiple matrix completion problems simultaneously. At each round, the learner can choose from which matrix it receives a sample from an entry drawn uniformly at random. Our main practical motivation is market segmentation, where the matrices represent different regions with different preferences of the customers. The challenge in this setting is that each of the matrices can be of a different size and also of a different rank which is unknown. We provide and analyze a new algorithm, MAlocate that is able to adapt to the unknown ranks of the different matrices. We then give a lower-bound showing that our strategy is minimax-optimal and demonstrate its performance with synthetic experiments.

</details>


### [246] [Black-box optimization of noisy functions with unknown smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02462)
*Jean-Bastien Grill,Michal Valko,Rémi Munos*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应黑盒优化算法POO（并行乐观优化），该算法无需预先知道目标函数的局部平滑度，即可对任意维度的带噪函数进行优化。POO在性能上接近已知需要平滑度信息的最优算法，且适用于更广泛的一类函数（尤其是难以优化的函数）。理论分析表明，经过n次评估后，POO的误差最多比已知最优算法（需已知平滑度）的误差大一个sqrt(ln n)因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒优化算法通常需要知道目标函数在全局最优点附近的局部平滑度信息，但实际应用中这一信息往往未知。本文旨在设计一种自适应算法，无需预先知道平滑度，仍能取得近乎最优的优化性能，并适用于更广泛的函数类别。

Method: 提出了并行乐观优化（POO）算法，该算法通过自适应地探索函数空间，在不依赖已知平滑度的情况下进行优化。算法采用并行策略，对函数值进行带噪评估，并基于乐观原则选择搜索区域。

Result: 理论证明POO的有限时间性能：经过n次评估后，其误差与需要已知平滑度的最优算法相比，最多相差一个sqrt(ln n)因子。同时，POO适用于比以往工作更广泛的函数类别，特别是那些难以优化的函数。

Conclusion: POO算法无需平滑度先验知识即可达到近乎最优的优化性能，拓展了可优化函数的范围，为黑盒优化问题提供了一种更具普适性的解决方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of black-box optimization of a function f of any dimension, given function evaluations perturbed by noise. The function is assumed to be locally smooth around one of its global optima, but this smoothness is unknown. Our contribution is an adaptive optimization algorithm, POO or parallel optimistic optimization, that is able to deal with this setting. POO performs almost as well as the best known algorithms requiring the knowledge of the smoothness. Furthermore, POO works for a larger class of functions than what was previously considered, especially for functions that are difficult to optimize, in a very precise sense. We provide a finite-time analysis of POO's performance, which shows that its error after n evaluations is at most a factor of sqrt(ln n) away from the error of the best known optimization algorithms using the knowledge of the smoothness.

</details>


### [247] [Online Generalised Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02675)
*Mehran H. Z. Bazargani,Szymon Urbas,Adeel Razi,Thomas Brendan Murphy,Karl Friston*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了在线动态期望最大化（ODEM）算法，通过时间尺度分离实现广义滤波的在线化，能够在非线性甚至混沌生成模型中同时进行隐状态推断、参数学习和不确定性估计，并从神经拟态预测编码视角提供了生物学上合理的在线推理方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广义滤波（如DEM）主要用于离线批次处理，无法高效处理在线数据同化场景。需要一种能够实时、逐时刻地同时进行状态推断、参数学习和不确定性估计（三重估计）的框架。

Method: 通过时间尺度分离，将DEM推广到在线领域（ODEM）：快速时间尺度上对动态隐状态进行贝叶斯信念更新，慢速时间尺度上对参数和精度进行渐进式学习。基于变分原理推导了在线数据同化的具体流程。

Result: 数值实验表明，即使生成模型的函数形式与真实生成过程的动力学存在根本性差异，ODEM仍能有效跟踪非线性（乃至混沌）生成过程中的隐状态，验证了算法的鲁棒性和在线有效性。

Conclusion: ODEM从神经拟态预测编码视角提供了一种生物学上合理的在线推理、学习和不确定性估计框架，适用于动态环境中的实时数据同化。

Abstract: This paper introduces an extension of generalised filtering for online applications. Generalised filtering refers to data assimilation schemes that jointly infer latent states, learn unknown model parameters, and estimate uncertainty in an integrated framework -- e.g., estimate state and observation noise -- at the same time (i.e., triple estimation). This framework appears across disciplines under different names, including variational Kalman-Bucy filtering in engineering, generalised predictive coding in neuroscience, and Dynamic Expectation Maximisation (DEM) in time-series analysis. Here, we specialise DEM for ``online'' data assimilation, through a separation of temporal scales. We describe the variational principles and procedures that allow one to assimilate data in a way that allows for a slow updating of parameters and precisions, which contextualise fast Bayesian belief updating about the dynamic hidden states. Using numerical studies, we demonstrate the validity of online DEM (ODEM) using a non-linear -- and potentially chaotic -- generative model, to show that the ODEM scheme can track the latent states of the generative process, even when its functional form differs fundamentally from the dynamics of the generative model. Framed from a neuro-mimetic predictive coding perspective, ODEM offers a biologically inspired solution to online inference, learning, and uncertainty estimation in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [248] [Random-Effects Algorithm for Random Objects in Metric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02693)
*Marcos Matabuena,Mateo Cámara*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fréchet的非线性随机效应模型，用于度量空间中任意随机对象的建模与预测，并在数字健康数据上展示了优于传统希尔伯特空间方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代科学数据中，多个观测常来自同一实验单元且呈现非欧几里得随机对象形式，而现有的随机效应模型主要适用于标量或希尔伯特空间中的函数型数据，缺乏通用的度量空间随机效应框架。

Method: 提出基于Fréchet的非线性随机效应算法，利用M估计理论建立一致估计的条件，适用于度量空间中的任意随机对象。

Result: 在合成数据与数字健康数据（多变量概率分布、随机图）上验证了方法有效性，且超越了现有的希尔伯特空间方法。

Conclusion: 该工作将随机效应模型推广到度量空间，为非欧几里得对象的个性化预测提供了通用工具，并在实践中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Across many scientific disciplines, multiple observations are collected from the same experimental units, and in modern datasets these observations often arise as non-Euclidean random objects. In such settings, the incorporation of random effects is a critical modeling step for efficient estimation and personalized prediction. Although mixed-effects models are well established for scalar outcomes and, more recently, for functional data in Hilbert spaces, general random-effects frameworks for objects in metric spaces remain underdeveloped. In this paper, we propose a nonlinear Fréchet-based algorithm for random-effects modeling of arbitrary random objects defined on a metric space. Using M-estimation theory, we establish conditions under which the proposed metric-space prediction target is consistently estimated under a working random-effects formulation. We then evaluate the empirical performance of the proposed method using both synthetic data and digital health datasets that require practical tools for analyzing random objects in metric spaces, such as multivariate probability distributions and random graphs. We show that, although our method is developed beyond Hilbert spaces, it can outperform existing Hilbert space-based methods.

</details>
